# JOURNEY.md

<!-- The structural and temporal design spec. Pairs with DESIGN.md (visual tokens). For visual tokens, see DESIGN.md (created in Phase 2 of the Stage 1 plan; it does not exist yet). -->

**Product:** Kiwi (`kiwi-ui`) · **Plan:** Kiwi UI refresh, Stage 1 (visual and verbal layer) · **Phase:** 1, as-is Discover · **Written:** 2026-10-16 · **Base commit:** `ef65c1d` on `feature/kiwi-ui-stage-1-design`

This file records the interface **as it is today**. Stage 1 changes colour, type, density, the mascot and the words. It does not move page regions, add routes, change navigation or change flows, so nothing here is redesigned: every section describes, and the one forward-looking column, *Fix* in the Defect register's summary, names a direction and an owning phase, not a design. Later phases append to it (mascot placements, revised microcopy and the revision index, chart and table specs) and leave the as-is record intact. Phase 4's mascot placements are the `Companion (Phase 4)` bullet on each affected entry (SR-1, SR-5, SR-6, S-01, S-05, S-06, S-08, S-10, A-01, A-02, A-13, A-19), each naming the `companion.slot.*` token, the size from `md` and below it, the pose and the backing; the state machine itself is `kiwi-ui/DESIGN.md § Companion`.

| Section | State |
|---|---|
| [Job](#job) | Deferred to Stage 2 |
| [Journey](#journey) | Deferred to Stage 2 |
| [IA](#ia) | Current route map recorded as-is and marked **UNVALIDATED**; validation deferred to Stage 2 |
| [Flows](#flows) | Deferred to Stage 2 |
| [Page specs](#page-specs) | As-is: 7 shared regions, 34 page entries, 5 redirect rows (three redirect-only routes, R-1 to R-3; one conditional redirect, R-4; one direct-load redirect, R-5) |
| [Verification matrix](#verification-matrix) | Generated from the state ids (`m:<id>`) in the page specs and from the role outcomes: 84 page rows (22 record-only), 1576 page cells, 2006 verification cells with the shared regions, and 5 redirect rows |
| [Defect register](#defect-register) | Every tour finding classified against the Stage 1 boundary, with a severity-sorted summary and a Fix per finding |

## Reading guide

**Sources.** Brief: `.design-foundations/research/2026-10-16-kiwi-ui-visual-refresh.md` (G1-G15, Appendix A). Tour: `.design-foundations/research/2026-10-16-kiwi-ui-tour.md`. Code: `kiwi-ui/src/` at the base commit, read-only.

**Evidence markers.**

| Marker | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `[T]` | Observed live in the 2026-10-16 tour (student side at 1440 wide, admin side at about 960 wide, older backend, one demo class, default states). |
| `[S]` | Taken from source only. **Not observed live**, except where `[L]` says so. Every error state, empty state, role variant and dialog is `[S]` unless the tour or a live check says otherwise. |
| `[L]` | Checked live by the coordinator on 2026-10-16, after the tour, on the dev server with an admin account: the course list (S-01) with its view-switch icon and class card, the Experiment toggle row on a student class page, and a typed app URL (R-5). It is not part of the tour, so it is not `[T]`. |
| `` `src/pages/Courses.tsx:11` `` | Code anchor, path relative to `kiwi-ui/`, line number at the base commit. Every claim about code behaviour has one. |
| `TD-nn` | A finding from the tour, in the [Defect register](#defect-register). |
| `SF-nn` | Found in source and not in the tour. Recorded for later phases; kept apart from `TD-` so DW-1.5 stays auditable. |
| `m:<id>` | The matrix state that a States bullet or an overlay item describes, for example `m:loaded` or `m:ov:edit-dialog`. The [Verification matrix](#verification-matrix) is generated from these ids (see [How an entry is organised](#how-an-entry-is-organised)). |
| "string" | A UI string quoted verbatim from source. Capitals are noted where they come from CSS rather than the string. |

**Severity** (Nielsen 0-4: 0 not a problem, 1 cosmetic, 2 minor, 3 major, 4 catastrophe) is frequency x impact x persistence. It is a single-evaluator rating from tour notes and source, so it finds *likely* problems, not real frequency, and should be re-rated against user data in Stage 2. Contrast and legibility findings are filed under heuristic 4 (Consistency and standards, with WCAG AA as the standard; the brief names no version, and 2.2 is assumed) because Nielsen's ten name neither. A contrast row cites its WCAG 2.2 success criterion beside the heuristic: SC 1.4.3 Contrast (Minimum) for text, SC 1.4.11 Non-text Contrast for icons, borders and chart marks.

**Roles.** S student (`systemRole` `user`), I instructor, A admin (`systemRole` `admin` or `isAdmin`), "signed out". Role information has three layers and each entry keeps them apart: *Appendix A* (the brief's intent), *entry visibility* (which roles are shown a sidebar or menu entry, `[S]`), and the *guard* (what the frontend route wrappers in `src/auth/` actually admit). The [Verification matrix](#verification-matrix) follows Appendix A as the user updated it on 2026-10-16, the intended access model: a role gets a verified row if and only if Appendix A grants it, and a role that today's guards let in but Appendix A does not grant gets a record-only defect row. Every page was walked once per role in source against today's guards; none was run. The outcomes are in the [Role outcomes](#role-outcomes).

**Links deferred to Phase 2.** DESIGN.md does not exist yet, so the cross-links the journey template asks for are written when Phase 2 creates it: in DESIGN.md, "For IA and flow spec, see JOURNEY.md"; in `kiwi-ui/CLAUDE.md`, a `## Design Context` block with `- **Journey spec**: JOURNEY.md` (that file has no such block today). The repository-root `JOURNEY.md` link is the orchestrator's setup step after Phase 1.

---

## Job

**Deferred to Stage 2.**

Why:

- Stage 1 changes no flows, so a job story would not change a single Stage 1 decision. The brief says so directly: "Jobs-to-be-done and journeys are Stage 2 inputs" (brief, *Who*).
- A job story is research output, not a drafting exercise. The method the journey doctrine recommends, the Switch interview (Moesta: push, pull, anxiety, habit), needs interviews with real users of Kiwi. None has been run and no JTBD school has been chosen for the project. A story written now would be a hypothesis presented as a finding, and could not rule out any product decision (journey-caveats, *JTBD school-mixing* and the circular-statement warning).
- The audiences are named in the brief and listed here as context only, not as jobs: students; instructors and admins; Kiwi App developers; researchers running chat experiments.

Stage 2 needs: one JTBD school picked for the whole project, interviews per audience, and a named owner.

## Journey

**Deferred to Stage 2.** No journey map, swim lane or emotion curve is drawn in this file.

Why:

- A journey map is an organisational change tool and needs a **named owner**, a **research basis** (interviews, diary study, analytics or support-call analysis) and an **update cadence**. This project has none of the three. A map drawn from a tour and a code read would be journey-map theatre: 83% of CX professionals report that their maps do not drive change (Watermark Consulting 2023), and an emotion curve drawn without research is the commonest sign of it (NN/g, Gibbons 2018).
- The tour is a current-state page review of two accounts at two viewports. It records what pages look like, not what users tried to do or felt while doing it.

Research basis: **UNGROUNDED**. Owner: none assigned. Review cadence: none.

## IA

**Validation and any redesign: deferred to Stage 2.** What follows is the current route map recorded as-is and **UNVALIDATED**. A sitemap is not an information architecture (Rosenfeld/Morville): a tidy tree can encode the wrong mental model, and neither a card sort (to test grouping and labels) nor a tree test (to test the structure) has been run. Nothing below asserts that the structure or the labels match how users think.

Why deferred: Stage 1 may not move or resize page regions, add or remove navigation items, or add routes (brief, *Stage 1 boundary*), and IA validation can recommend exactly those changes.

### Observed, not chosen

- **Organisation scheme:** audience first (student area, auth area, admin area), then inside admin *system scope* (sidebar) versus *class scope* (class sub-menu). Observed, not validated.
- **Structure:** student side is a hub (course list) feeding a class workspace with tabs; admin side is a tree (sidebar, then a class sub-menu under "Classes" or "My Classes"). `SR-2`, `SR-3`.
- **Global navigation:** the top bar (`SR-1`): logo, class switcher, a view switch for instructors and admins, logout.
- **Local navigation:** the student class tab bar (`SR-2`); the admin sidebar and class sub-menu (`SR-3`); the admin page header's back button (`SR-4`).
- **Contextual navigation:** class cards on the course list (S-01); navigation actions returned by the AI tutor (`src/components/GlobalChat/GlobalChatModal.tsx:69-95`).
- **Breadcrumbs:** none. **Search:** a class finder in the class switcher, plus per-page searches.
- **Labelling:** sidebar labels and page headings disagree in four pairs (TD-21); instructors see "My Classes" where admins see "Classes" (`src/pages/admin/AdminLayout.tsx:464`).

**Validation: NOT VALIDATED.** No card sort. No tree test.

## Flows

**Deferred to Stage 2.** No user flow or task flow is drawn.

Why: Stage 1 does not change flows (brief, *Stage 1 boundary*: "changing flows" is out), so a diagram would document nothing Stage 1 touches and would invite changes it may not make. Flows are better drawn once Job and Journey have a research basis.

As-is fragments the page specs already carry (pointers, not diagrams):

- **Sign-in and role redirect:** S-05, S-07, `UnprotectedRoute`; instructors and admins land on `/admin`, everyone else on `/` (`src/pages/Login.tsx:72-78`).
- **Consent gate:** `ProtectedRoute` to S-09 and back to `/`, only when a consent form URL is configured (`src/auth/ProtectedRoute.tsx:55-58`).
- **Session expiry and B2:** a second 401 clears the session and goes to `/login?timeout=true` (`src/utils/middleware.ts:61-102`), shown as a banner on S-05.
- **MCP OAuth round trip:** SR-6 and A-07 store a return context, leave for the provider, and `ProtectedRoute` replays it (`src/auth/ProtectedRoute.tsx:17-44`). See SF-03 for the student-side return path.

## Page specs

34 page entries (`S-01`-`S-10` student, auth and fallback; `A-01`-`A-24` admin), preceded by the seven shared regions (`SR-1`-`SR-7`) that the pages sit inside and followed by the redirect rows (`R-1`-`R-5`). Order follows the route map in [IA](#ia). Four components serve several routes and are specified once, with every route in the heading: `ClassEdit` (A-13), `AdminAppPage` (A-12, three routes), `ExperimentForm` (A-20), `AnnotateDocument` (A-24).

### How an entry is organised

| Field | Content |
|---|---|
| Source | The page component and the children that were read, with anchors. |
| Roles (Appendix A) | Exactly as the brief's Appendix A records them (S, I, A). |
| Guard (code) | What the frontend wrapper admits. Where it differs from Appendix A the entry says so and cites a discrepancy `D-n` from [Role discrepancies](#role-discrepancies). |
| Entry visibility | Which roles are *shown* a sidebar or menu entry, where that differs from the guard. A third layer between intent and guard; `[S]`. |
| Density | One value per page, set by the route area: **comfortable** for student and auth routes, **compact** for `/admin/*` (brief, *Density*). |
| Density overrides | Reading surfaces (chat answers, PDF pages, materials) are comfortable even inside a compact page; tool chrome (IDE, PDF and table toolbars) is compact even inside a comfortable page. Listed as found; Phase 3 fixes the boundary. |
| Paper surfaces (G5) | PDF pages, templated-email previews and Kiwi App iframes: their *content* stays light in dark mode, their frames and toolbars follow the mode. User-uploaded images and Python/R plots are listed apart as "never recoloured" (G5 keeps them outside "paper"). |
| Purpose, Entry points, Content blocks, States, Primary CTA, Exit / next | The journey page-spec format. |
| State ids (`m:<id>`) | Each States bullet starts with the id of the matrix state it describes, written as a code span such as `m:loaded` or `m:ov:edit-dialog`; each overlay item carries its id. `m:none` marks a bullet that is deliberately not a matrix state (the state is absent, or it is a component state), `m:to:X` a hand-over to another page or region, `m:G-n` a bullet covered by mechanism row G-n of the [Role outcomes](#role-outcomes), and `@S,I,A` after an id limits it to those roles. A "Page-specific" bullet is a note and needs no id, unless it also describes a state, in which case it carries one. The [Verification matrix](#verification-matrix) is generated from these ids. |
| Stage 1 hooks | Where the brief's allowed element changes land (mascot avatar and composer peek, empty-state illustrations, class-card fallback, not-found content, Display-menu button). Existing surfaces only, not designs. |
| Tour defects (in boundary) | `TD-nn (heuristic, severity)` from the [Defect register](#defect-register). `TD-C1` (the Material Design 2014 purple palette) applies to every entry and is listed first in each. |
| Source-found | `SF-nn`: seen in code and not in the tour. |
| Not observed live | States and variants known from source only. |

### Role discrepancies

The matrix follows Appendix A as the user updated it on 2026-10-16 (the intended access model). These are the places where Appendix A, the entry-visibility layer and today's guards do not agree. Each is a defect and none is designed around; by the one rule a role that Appendix A does not grant gets a record-only defect row, never a supported row.

| ID | Discrepancy | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| D-1 | Appendix A marks A-04, A-05, A-07 and A-08 "A (admin-only)", and the user's decision of 2026-10-16 keeps every system-level page admin-only for instructors. `AdminProtectedRoute` admits `isAdmin === true` **or** `systemRole === 'instructor'`, so instructors pass it. Behind the wrapper the backend refuses an instructor on the first request (`AdminGuard` or `ProfGuard`, 401), so today an instructor sees the first frame and is signed out (B2); the intended outcome is a no-permission state (D-8). By the one rule the instructor rows of these four pages are record-only defect rows, not supported rows. | `src/auth/AdminProtectedRoute.tsx:40`; `src/App.tsx:140-159`; [Instructors on admin-only pages](#instructors-on-admin-only-pages-d-8) |
| D-2 | Appendix A marks `/login`, `/register`, `/auth/callback` "all". `UnprotectedRoute` admits signed-out users only; a signed-in user (both `authToken` and `user` present) is redirected to `/admin` (instructor, admin) or `/`; with only one of the two present the page renders. | `src/auth/UnprotectedRoute.tsx:19-26` |
| D-3 | Appendix A marks `/authorize` "per route guard". There is no wrapper. | `src/App.tsx:84-87` |
| D-4 | Entry visibility against Appendix A. The system sidebar entries are admin-only for instructors, as the decision wants (`AdminLayout.tsx:50-110`). In the class sub-menu three entries are hidden from instructors although Appendix A grants them: Enroll Students (A-16) and Class Email (A-17), for the instructors of the class, and Class Apps (A-23). Class Flags (A-15) and Class MCP & Tools (A-22) are hidden and admin-only, as intended; Materials (A-14) and Class Analytics (A-18) need a per-class instructor permission. Entry visibility is not a guard: an instructor can still open every URL (D-8). | `src/pages/admin/AdminLayout.tsx:50-110`; `src/pages/admin/ClassLayout.tsx:36-44,148-166`; SR-3 |
| D-5 (B1, B2) | The brief says `/admin` admits admins and instructors, and the intended model is that students cannot enter it. The `/admin` layout is wrapped in `ProtectedRoute` only, so any signed-in user reaches the shell (B1). What a student gets today depends on the page: 5 render (A-01, A-02, A-12, A-18, A-23), 2 raise an `alert()` (A-14, A-24), 5 redirect (A-03, A-04, A-05, A-07, A-08) and 12 end in B2, where the first request is refused with 401 and the middleware signs the user out as "session expired". These outcomes are recorded in [Students on `/admin/*`](#students-on-admin-b1), not as rows. | `src/App.tsx:129-135`; tour B1, B2; `src/utils/middleware.ts:61-102` |
| D-6 | The OAuth-return handler sends students to `/class/{classId}` and `/class/{classId}/pdf/{pdfId}`. Neither is in the route table (the class route is `/:classID`), so both land on S-10. | `src/auth/ProtectedRoute.tsx:31-35` vs `src/App.tsx:112-124` |
| D-7 | Appendix A makes A-03 (`/admin/analytics`) admin-only. The page redirects a non-admin to `/admin/classes` after its first render, while its data requests start anyway and are refused with 403 (two `alert()`s for a student, one for an instructor). The turn-away is intended; its form is the defect: an instructor should see a no-permission state (D-8), not an `alert()` and a bounce. | `src/pages/admin/Analytics.tsx:241-252`; [Instructors on admin-only pages](#instructors-on-admin-only-pages-d-8) |
| D-8 | Appendix A makes 12 admin pages admin-only for instructors: A-02 (New class) to A-11, and inside a class A-15 and A-22. An instructor who reaches one should see a no-permission state, not be signed out and not be sent back with an `alert()`. That state does not exist today. Instead the guards let the instructor in and the backend refuses the first request with 401, which the front end treats as an expired session (B2) on 10 of the pages; A-03 raises an `alert()` and redirects (D-7); on A-02 the form renders and the submit is refused (B2); `AdminProtectedRoute` admits instructors on four of them (D-1). The chat agent's sitemap even offers A-02 and A-03 to instructors (`src/agent/sitemap.ts:60-70`). Register: B4. | `../kiwi/src/auth/auth.guard.ts:53-133`; `src/utils/middleware.ts:61-102`; [Instructors on admin-only pages](#instructors-on-admin-only-pages-d-8) |
| D-9 | Appendix A grants Enrolment (A-16) and Class email (A-17) to the instructors of the class. `ProfGuard` needs a `professor` enrolment, and assigning an instructor creates none, so the backend refuses the instructors of the class (401) and the front end signs them out (B2). Their rows stay verified rows; the refusal is the defect. Register: B5; SF-65. | `../kiwi/src/auth/auth.guard.ts:84-133`; `../kiwi/src/instructor-class/instructor-class.service.ts:14-62` |

### Rules used in every entry

- **Density.** Student and auth route areas: comfortable. `/admin/*`: compact. Reading surfaces (chat answers, PDF pages, materials): comfortable everywhere. Tool chrome (IDE toolbar, PDF toolbar, table toolbars): compact everywhere.
- **Paper (G5).** PDF pages and templated-email previews stay light in dark mode (fidelity); Kiwi App iframes stay light (no theme channel reaches them, `kiwi-app-sdk/src/types.ts`). Code editors, code blocks, output consoles and every Kiwi-drawn surface follow the mode and are **not** paper.
- **Primary CTA** is the page's single filled action. Where a view region shows several filled buttons the entry lists them all: the brief allows at most one filled primary per view region.

**Shared regions.** Regions that appear on many routes are specified once here and referenced from the page entries.

### SR-1 · Top bar (`TopNav`)

- **Source:** `src/components/TopNav/TopNav.tsx:42-248`; `src/components/TopNav/ClassSwitcher.tsx`; `src/components/Brand/InstitutionLogo.tsx:18-73`
- **Appears on:** S-01 (`src/pages/Courses.tsx:39`), S-02 to S-04 through SR-2 (`src/pages/ClassLayout.tsx:314`), every admin page through SR-3 (`src/pages/admin/AdminLayout.tsx:525`). Not on auth routes, S-09 or S-10.
- **Roles (Appendix A):** not a route. **Entry visibility:** the view-switch button is shown to I and A only; the "Create New Class" action in the class switcher to A only (`TopNav.tsx:58-59,185-187,209,222`). `[S]`
- **Density:** inherits the page's route area. One component with a 52 px bar (`TopNav.tsx:39`) and 44 px icon buttons (`:66-80`) on both areas today.
- **Paper surfaces:** none.
- **Content blocks (left to right):**
  1. Menu toggle "Toggle navigation menu": admin routes only, and only when the admin sidebar is a drawer (below the `md` breakpoint). `src/components/TopNav/TopNav.tsx:109-119`; `src/pages/admin/AdminLayout.tsx:527-531`
  2. Logo link to `/admin` on admin routes and `/` elsewhere (`TopNav.tsx:122-144`). The content is `InstitutionLogo`: with `VITE_LOGO_LIGHT_URL` set, a CSS-masked institution mark filled with `primary.main` by default; otherwise the square `kiwi-mark.svg` image plus the text "Kiwi" in `primary.main` (`InstitutionLogo.tsx:19,23-43,44-73`). Brief G2 wants the logo neutral black or white.
  3. Divider and context area (`TopNav.tsx:146-203`): a skeleton labelled "Loading class name" while the class name loads; the class switcher when `onClassChange` is passed; otherwise a plain `h6` label.
  4. Class switcher (`ClassSwitcher.tsx:51-390`): class icon (the admin "All Classes" scope uses an admin icon in `primary.main`), the label truncated with the full text in `title`, and a button "Switch class scope. Current selection: {label}" (`:136`). It opens a popover with a search field "Find class…" (`:219`, accessible name "Search classes" `:222`), a listbox "Classes" (`:257`) whose selected row carries a check mark, and for admins a footer action "Create New Class" (`src/constants/classCopy.ts:1`, `ClassSwitcher.tsx:388`) that opens `/admin/new-class` (`TopNav.tsx:185-187`).
  5. Spacer.
  6. View-switch icon button (I and A): on admin routes "Switch to Student View" to `/` (`TopNav.tsx:208-218`) `[T]`; on student routes "Switch to Admin View" (A) or "Switch to Instructor View" (I) to `/admin` (`:221-232`) `[S]`; seen live on the course list with an admin account `[L]`.
  7. Logout icon button "Logout": a plain anchor to `/logout`, so it loads the page afresh (`:234-244`) `[T]`.
- **States:**
  - `m:loaded` Default: student bar = logo, class switcher (on class routes), logout `[T]`; admin bar adds the view switch `[T]`.
  - `m:loading` Loading: class-name skeleton (`TopNav.tsx:169-175`); the class list shows "Loading classes…" with a spinner (`ClassSwitcher.tsx:269-280`). `[S]`
  - `m:empty` Empty: the class list shows "No classes found" (`ClassSwitcher.tsx:53`) or, for an instructor, "No assigned classes" (`AdminLayout.tsx:569-573`). `[S]`
  - `m:ov:class-switcher` Switcher open `[S]`: the popover of block 4, with its search field, its "Classes" listbox and, for admins, the footer action.
  - `m:none` Error: none shown. A failed class-list fetch leaves an empty list and logs to the console (`AdminLayout.tsx:363-365`; `src/pages/ClassLayout.tsx:177-179`). `[S]`
  - `m:none` Fallback label: "Unnamed class" (`src/pages/ClassLayout.tsx:317`; `AdminLayout.tsx:540`).
- **Primary CTA:** none (navigation chrome).
- **Exit / next:** logo to `/` or `/admin`; switcher to `/:classID…` or `/admin/class/:classID…`; view switch; Logout to R-3.
- **Stage 1 hooks:** the Display-menu button (brief G4) joins the right-hand icon group, next to Logout. G2 (neutral institution logo) applies to block 2; the mascot mark is what block 2 already shows when no institution is configured.
- **Companion (Phase 4):** no companion placement here. Block 2's fallback mark stays the P2 app tile (`kiwi-mark.svg`, as today; G2 lets Kiwi's own marks keep the brand palette); it is not a companion state and never animates. The Display menu's Kiwi animation switch (G4) is the user's motion control for every placement below (`DESIGN.md § Companion › Motion controls`).
- **Tour defects (in boundary):** TD-C1 (heuristic 4, sev 1); TD-07 (heuristic 4, sev 1).
- **Not observed live:** the instructor variant, the switcher popover and every loading and empty state.

### SR-2 · Student class layout (`StudentClassLayout`)

- **Source:** `src/pages/ClassLayout.tsx:63-381`
- **Appears on:** S-02, S-03, S-04 (route `/:classID` and its children, `src/App.tsx:112-124`).
- **Roles (Appendix A):** S, I, A. **Guard (code):** `ProtectedRoute`, any signed-in user, consent redirect when configured. Class access is decided by the backend. **Density:** comfortable. **Paper surfaces:** none of its own (hosts S-02's PDF pages and S-04's iframe).
- **Content blocks (in order):**
  1. SR-1 with class context: name from navigation state or fetch, switcher fed by the student's enrolled classes (`:314-324`).
  2. Tab bar, 44 px: "Materials" (index route), "Scratchpad" (`code`), then one tab per enabled Kiwi App labelled `tabLabel || name`, ordered by `tabOrder` (`:51-54,106-114,271`). Scrollable; 12 px, weight 600, letter-spacing 0.8 px, colour `#77777F`, selected tab `primary.main`, bar `#fff` with a `#E5E1EB` bottom border: literal values (`:327-352`).
  3. Content area on `grey.100` showing the child route, or a centred spinner until the class has loaded (`:356-377`).
- **States:**
  - `m:loaded` Default `[T]`: "Materials", "Scratchpad" and the app tab "CSS R Lab"; the active tab has a purple underline.
  - `m:class-loading` Loading `[S]`: centred spinner, no text (`:366-375`); the top bar shows a name skeleton until the name is known (`:319`).
  - `m:class-loading` Error `[S]`: **none visible.** A failed class fetch is `console.error` only (`:252-254`), `myClass` stays unset and the spinner never clears (SF-04). A 401, which only an expired token produces here, signs the user out instead (B2); the class lookups answer 200 for every role (SF-64).
  - `m:none` Unknown tab path `[S]`: replaced by the first tab (`:192-207`).
  - `m:none` Empty `[S]`: if the enabled-apps fetch fails only the two built-in tabs show (`:263-277`).
- **Primary CTA:** none (navigation).
- **Exit / next:** tab change (`:130-136`); class switch, which keeps the sub-route (`:138-154`); SR-1.
- **Stage 1 hooks:** none beyond SR-1 and SR-6.
- **Tour defects (in boundary):** TD-C1 (purple active-tab underline `[T]`; heuristic 4, sev 1).
- **Source-found:** SF-04.

### SR-3 · Admin shell (`AdminLayout`)

- **Source:** `src/pages/admin/AdminLayout.tsx:182-665`; `src/components/NavigationSidebar/NavigationSidebar.tsx`; class sub-menu from `src/pages/admin/ClassLayout.tsx:29-196`
- **Appears on:** every `/admin/*` page (route `/admin`, `src/App.tsx:129-135`).
- **Roles (Appendix A):** admin area "admits admins and instructors". **Guard (code):** `ProtectedRoute` only, so any signed-in user reaches the shell (B1, D-5). **Density:** compact. **Paper surfaces:** none of its own.
- **Content blocks (in order):**
  1. SR-1 with admin context (`:525-574`). Context label: "All Classes" (admin, no class chosen), the class name inside a class, or "Select a class". The class list is fed by `classControllerList` (A) or the instructor's own classes (I) (`:312-372`).
  2. Sidebar, 220 px expanded and 56 px collapsed (`NavigationSidebar.tsx:98-100`), with a footer button "Collapse admin navigation" / "Expand admin navigation" (`AdminLayout.tsx:598-611`). Below the `md` breakpoint it becomes a 260 px temporary drawer opened by the top-bar menu button (`:618-643`). Entries are filtered by the role fetched from `authControllerGetUserRole` (`:205-219,279-282`); the system entries and who sees them, from source (`[S]`):

     | Sidebar label | Path | Leads to | Shown today to | Instructor may use it (Appendix A) | Flag |
     |---|---|---|---|---|---|
     | Classes (instructors see "My Classes", `:464`) | `/admin/classes` | A-01 | A, I | yes | none |
     | User Management | `/admin/user-management` | A-11 | A | no, admin-only | none: hidden, as intended |
     | System Analytics | `/admin/analytics` | A-03 | A | no, admin-only | none: hidden, as intended |
     | System Email | `/admin/email` | A-04 | A | no, admin-only | none: hidden, as intended |
     | Email Audit | `/admin/email-audit` | A-05 | A | no, admin-only | none: hidden, as intended |
     | System Flags | `/admin/feature-flags` | A-06 | A | no, admin-only | none: hidden, as intended |
     | MCP Server Registry | `/admin/mcp-servers` | A-07 | A | no, admin-only | none: hidden, as intended |
     | App Registry | `/admin/kiwi-apps` | A-08 | A | no, admin-only | none: hidden, as intended |
     | Webhooks | `/admin/webhooks` | A-09 | A | no, admin-only | none: hidden, as intended |
     | Webhook Approvals | `/admin/webhooks/approvals` | A-10 | A | no, admin-only | none: hidden, as intended |

     Source of the table: `AdminLayout.tsx:50-110` (the `roles` array of each item; the filter is `:280-282`). Admin apps that declare an enabled `admin-panel` UI are appended, one entry each, when the role is allowed to see that UI (`:221-269,476-509`); they lead to A-12.
  3. Class sub-menu, nested under "Classes" / "My Classes" while the path starts with `/admin/class/` (`:290,461-474`). `src/pages/admin/ClassLayout.tsx:36-44` defines the entries and `:148-166` filters them for instructors (`hideForInstructor`, or a per-class permission). The active child shows a dot (`NavigationSidebar.tsx`, `sidebar-child-indicator`).

     | Class sub-menu entry | Path | Leads to | Shown today to | Instructor may use it (Appendix A) | Flag |
     |---|---|---|---|---|---|
     | Edit Class | `edit-metadata` | A-13 | A, I | yes | none |
     | Materials | `manage-materials` | A-14 | A; I with `canManageMaterials` | yes | none |
     | Class Flags | `feature-flags` | A-15 | A | no, admin-only | none: hidden, as intended |
     | Class MCP & Tools | `mcp-settings` | A-22 | A | no, admin-only | none: hidden, as intended |
     | Class Apps | `kiwi-apps` | A-23 | A | yes | **D-4: hidden from instructors although granted** |
     | Enroll Students | `enroll-students` | A-16 | A | yes, the instructors of the class | **D-4: hidden although granted; D-9: the backend refuses them** |
     | Class Email | `send-emails` | A-17 | A | yes, the instructors of the class | **D-4: hidden although granted; D-9: the backend refuses them** |
     | Class Analytics | `professor-analytics` | A-18 | A; I with `canViewAnalytics` | yes | none |
     | Experiments | `experiments` | A-19 | A, I | yes | none |

     **What an instructor sees today:** one system entry, Classes ("My Classes"), the app entries, and inside a class Edit Class, Experiments, and Materials and Class Analytics when the permission is set. **No entry an instructor sees leads to an admin-only page**, so the 12 admin-only pages are reachable only by a typed URL or through the chat agent (D-8). The mismatches go the other way: three entries that Appendix A grants are hidden (Class Apps, Enroll Students, Class Email).

     **Other navigation surfaces.** The chat agent's sitemap (`src/agent/sitemap.ts:12-166`, read by `AgentBridge.tsx:72,159,213`) lists `new-class` and `analytics`, which lead an instructor to A-02 and A-03: **the two entries besides the sidebar that lead an instructor to an admin-only page** (`:60-64`, `:66-70`; D-8). Its other instructor destinations (`admin-classes`, `new-class`, `class-edit`, `class-materials`, `class-enroll`, `class-emails`, `class-analytics`, `class-experiments`, `new-experiment`) are all pages Appendix A grants, `class-enroll` and `class-emails` being refused by the backend today (D-9).
  4. Main content region on `grey.50`, scrolling inside the viewport below the 52 px bar (`:646-660`); the child page renders here.
- **States:**
  - `m:sidebar-open` `m:class-submenu` Default `[T]` (admin account): 11 system entries (10 built-in plus one admin app, "CSS Teacher Works…" truncated), 9 class entries inside a class; active entry has a light-purple fill and purple text, the active child a purple dot, and a purple collapse icon sits at the foot.
  - `m:sidebar-loading` Loading `[S]`: a skeleton sidebar, `aria-busy`, "Loading admin navigation", 10 placeholder rows (`:121-180,587`).
  - `m:sidebar-collapsed` Collapsed rail `[S]`: icons only, label in a tooltip (`NavigationSidebar.tsx:199`).
  - `m:sidebar-error` Error `[S]`: a failed role fetch leaves `userRole` null, so **no sidebar and no class switcher render** and the page content shows alone (`:205-219,284-285`); admin-app fetch errors are console-only (`:261-263`).
  - `m:none` Instructor redirect `[S]`: an instructor on the classless admin-app route is sent to their first assigned class, or to `/admin/classes` (`:381-408`).
  - `m:none` A student on `/admin/*` `[T]` (B1): the shell mounts with no sidebar and a plain context label instead of the class switcher, because the role is neither admin nor instructor (`AdminLayout.tsx:284-285,299-300,568,589`). A student is not an intended role on `/admin/*`: this is defect B1, recorded in [Students on `/admin/*`](#students-on-admin-b1) with what each page does today, and it has no matrix row.
- **Primary CTA:** none (navigation).
- **Exit / next:** sidebar entries; the class sub-menu; SR-1.
- **Stage 1 hooks:** none beyond SR-1 and SR-6. The sidebar's labels are copy in scope (TD-21).
- **Tour defects (in boundary):** TD-C1 (light-purple active fill, purple dot, purple collapse icon `[T]`; heuristic 4, sev 1); TD-21 (heuristic 4, sev 2).
- **Out of boundary, recorded:** B1, B2 (guard behaviour), TD-18.
- **Not observed live:** the instructor sidebar, per-class permission filtering, the drawer, the collapsed rail, and every loading and error state.

### SR-4 · Admin page frame (`AdminPage` and form helpers)

- **Source:** `src/components/AdminPage/AdminPage.tsx:10-18`; `AdminPageHeader.tsx:18-116`; `AdminFormSection.tsx:18-77`; `AdminFormSurface.tsx:10-26`; `AdminFormActions.tsx:10-40`
- **Appears on:** the admin pages that import it (named per entry).
- **Roles / Guard:** inherits SR-3. **Density:** compact. **Paper surfaces:** none.
- **Content blocks:**
  1. `AdminPage`: a container at `maxWidth` `lg` (1200 px) by default, padding 16/24 px sides and top, 32 px bottom (`AdminPage.tsx:10-18`).
  2. `AdminPageHeader`: a `header` with an `h1` (variant `h5`, weight 600), optional back button "Back to {label}", optional status node, optional description (max 720 px) and an actions row that wraps (`AdminPageHeader.tsx:18-116`).
  3. `AdminFormSection`: a section with an `h2` (`subtitle1`), a description and a content column, in a two-column grid (190 px | 1fr) from the `md` breakpoint, divided by a rule (`AdminFormSection.tsx:18-77`).
  4. `AdminFormSurface`: an outlined `Paper`, radius 3, no shadow (`AdminFormSurface.tsx:10-26`).
  5. `AdminFormActions`: a button row with a top border, optionally sticky, buttons at least 44 px tall (`AdminFormActions.tsx:10-40`).
- **States:** presentational; none of its own beyond its one view, `m:loaded`.
- **Primary CTA:** none (frame).
- **Exit / next:** the header's back button, where a page passes one.
- **Tour defects (in boundary):** TD-C1 (heuristic 4, sev 1). The tour calls Edit Class (A-13), which uses these helpers, "the most composed admin page — a good pattern to carry forward" (a positive finding, kept in A-13).
- **Not observed live:** none.

### SR-5 · Auth layout (`AuthLayout` split and centred, `FormCard`)

- **Source:** `src/components/AuthLayout/AuthLayout.tsx:13-115`; `src/components/FormCard/FormCard.tsx:10-43`
- **Appears on:** the split variant on S-05 and S-06 (`src/pages/Login.tsx:108`, `src/pages/Register.tsx:18`); the centred variant (`AuthLayout.tsx:118-137`, the component default) on S-08 only (`src/pages/MCPAuthorize.tsx:103,122,177`), with a glass `FormCard` (`src/components/FormCard/FormCard.tsx:21-37`). The blocks below describe the split variant.

  1. Left aside, from the `md` breakpoint up (`display: none` below it, `:50`): a rounded panel, minimum 640 px tall, with a dark overlay gradient (`rgba(24, 10, 34, …)`) over `/assets/images/bg-campus-1.jpg` (`:59`); top, `InstitutionLogo` in white when an institution is configured and the `KiwiWordmark` in white at 2rem (`:73-78`); bottom, the quote "“Learn deeply. Ask freely. Grow with AI.”" (`:92`) and a second line, "An AI learning companion built for the {INSTITUTION_NAME} community." or, without an institution, "An AI learning companion for your courses." (`:95-98`).

- **Companion (Phase 4):** the aside keeps the wordmark (G6) and gets no mascot; the sign-in slot lives in the page's form column (S-05, S-06): `companion.slot.signin` 96 px from `md`, `companion.slot.inline` 64 px below it, the idle still above the display line at the column's left edge (24 px right of the ruler, 12 at 390), on the `--companion-backing` disc in dark mode. The centred variant (S-08) takes the 64 px slot above the card's heading.

### SR-6 · Kiwi Chat: launcher and panel

- **Source:** host `src/components/GlobalChat/GlobalChatContainer.tsx:10`, `GlobalChatButton.tsx:83`, `GlobalChatModal.tsx:35`; the panel body `src/components/Chat/ChatCore.tsx:118`, whose **only mount is `GlobalChatModal.tsx:362`**; `ChatMessages.tsx:604`, `ChatField.tsx:66`, `ChatCommands.tsx:52`, `AgentStepChain.tsx:198`, `ToolStatusIndicator.tsx:41`, `src/components/Feedback/Feedback.tsx:16`, `ExperimentCompare.tsx:146`. The older `ChatComponent.tsx:90` (v1 endpoint, header "Chat" / "Code") is **not rendered anywhere**; only its `IDEContext` export is imported.
- **Appears on:** every route, but only while a user is signed in (`GlobalChatContainer.tsx:13-16`), through `RootLayout` (`src/components/RootLayout.tsx:16`). That includes S-08, S-09 and S-10 and excludes the signed-out auth pages. The panel stays mounted when closed (`visibility: hidden`), so a conversation survives closing.
- **Roles / Guard:** inherits the host route. **Entry visibility:** the Experiment controls show to admins and instructors with a class (`ChatCore.tsx:335-338,1119`; the backend checks `systemRole`, `../kiwi/src/student/student.service.ts:1920-1932`). **Density:** the answers are reading surfaces (comfortable); the header, session bar, model select and composer controls are tool chrome. **Paper surfaces:** none (markdown, KaTeX and code cards follow the mode).
- **Content blocks (in order):**
  1. **Launcher** `m:closed` (`GlobalChatButton.tsx:83-281`): a 56 px round `Fab` in `primary.main` with a chat icon, tooltip "Open Kiwi Chat", aria-label "open chat", `z-index` 1200, hidden while the panel is open. Default place: bottom right, 24 px from the right and 96 px above the bottom; **bottom left on app tabs** (the active tab is "app"). Draggable, snaps to the nearest vertical edge on release, position saved in `localStorage` `globalChatButtonPosition` and then wins on every tab (`:33,90-95,131-138,154-207`). A red count badge is coded but never shows: it reads `GlobalChatContext.messages`, which nothing writes (`addMessage` has no callers) (`:88,263-272`).
  2. **Backdrop and container** (`GlobalChatModal.tsx:298-352`): the default mode is **modal** `m:open:modal` (centred, up to 900 px wide, 80vh, radius 2, shadow 24, backdrop `rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.5)`); **side panel** `m:open:side` (420 px by default, 320-800, backdrop `rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.3)`); **snap** `m:open:snap` (docked, no backdrop, the page narrows by the panel width); **fullscreen** `m:open:fullscreen`; below `sm` it is always fullscreen (`:37,117,144-224`; `GlobalChatContext.tsx:122-124,200`). Side and snap modes have a resize grip, aria-label "resize chat panel".
  3. **Header**, 52 px (`GlobalChatModal.tsx:228-296`): "Kiwi Chat" and five icon buttons, tooltips "Modal view", "Side panel", "Snap to side", "Fullscreen" / "Exit fullscreen" and an unlabelled close; the active mode is `primary`. Icon-only; tooltips and aria-labels only.
  4. **Session bar**, 45 px (`ChatCore.tsx:1368-1403`): a history button (`title="Chat history"`), the label "New session" or "Current session", a "+" (`title="New chat"`) and an unlabelled chevron. **The capitals are CSS** (`textTransform: 'uppercase'`, 12 px, weight 600, `letterSpacing: '0.8px'`, `#77777F`), so it reads "NEW SESSION" (`:1380-1389`). The history list below it: "Loading sessions...", "No previous sessions", or rows (the first 50 characters of the first message or "New conversation", a `toLocaleDateString()` date, a count chip; counts cover only the latest 100 messages) (`:1406-1438,172-213`).
  5. **Experiment controls** `m:experiment-controls@I,A`, admins and instructors only: a switch (aria-label "Experiment mode", label "Experiment") that opens an "Experiment" select, "Loading experiments...", "No experiments have been created for this class.", "Experiments are currently unavailable.", a switch "Compare two variants side by side", "Variant A" / "Variant B" selects and variant buttons — `ChatCore.tsx:1119-1351`. The toggle row sits above the message region; it shows on a student class page for an admin account (seen live `[L]`) and not for a student.
  6. **Message region** (scrolls; smooth auto-scroll on every change): a **welcome row** "Welcome to Kiwi! How can I help you?", always prepended, even for restored sessions (`ChatMessages.tsx:639-645`); **user rows**, right-aligned with no bubble, a 32 px `primary.main` circle with a person icon; **tutor rows**: the **avatar** is a 32 px `primary.main` circle holding MUI `SmartToyIcon` (a robot) in white, **identical in every state** (`:279-293`), then the optional agent-step chain, the loader or the markdown text, "[Page N]" citation buttons and the **Feedback toolbar** of five small icons: "Regenerate response" (permanently disabled, no handler), "Flag this answer" (a native `prompt()` "Please enter your feedback:"), "Thumbs up", "Thumbs down", "Share answer" (permanently disabled) (`Feedback.tsx:47-101`); there is no message-level copy. An **error row** shows a red circle with an Android icon, the text or "Something went wrong. Please try again." and a contained "Retry" (`ChatMessages.tsx:314-349,252-276`). In compare mode the region becomes two columns (`ExperimentCompare.tsx`).
  7. **Composer** (`ChatCore.tsx:1467-1513`): a model select "Model" with "ChatGPT" and "Qwen" (hidden in experiment mode; saved in `localStorage` `engineChoice`); a 3-row textarea, placeholder **"Ask Kiwi... You can type / to see available commands."**, fixed height, Enter sends and Shift+Enter breaks the line; an icon-only Send on its own row with no label or tooltip, enabled even when the text is empty (`ChatField.tsx:112-183`); a slash menu "Grab code from slide", "Explain this slide", "Generate exercise" (always the "class materials" list in the floating panel) (`ChatCommands.tsx:35-67`).
  8. **Overlays:** a snackbar (bottom right, 3,000 ms); the dialog "Authorization Required" with the server's message and "Cancel" / "Connect Account" — `ChatCore.tsx:1516-1536`.
- **States** (the ★ marks the state the mock review found undrawn):
  - `m:empty-conversation` Empty conversation `[T]`: the welcome row only, "NEW SESSION", an enabled composer, "No previous sessions"; no suggestion chips, cards or illustration exist in code (`ChatCore.tsx:154-156,253-269`).
  - `m:ov:slash-menu` Typing `[S]`: nothing is exposed; no counter or draft; the slash menu opens on a bare "/".
  - `m:waiting` Waiting for the first token `[S]`: the user row, a tutor row with the cycling dots ".", "..", "..." (every 500 ms) in place of text, and the composer and Send disabled; the snackbar "Connected to Kiwi" for 3 s once the connection opens; **no "thinking" wording and no stall message** (`ChatCore.tsx:1030-1092,791-794`; `Loader.tsx:5-15`).
  - `m:agent-steps` Tool or agent step running `[S]`: a collapsible chain, "Working…" with a pulsing sparkle icon and a spinner while running and "N steps" with a static grey icon when done, open while running; step labels "Reading the sitemap", "Looking up classes", "Navigating", "Opening class content", "Looking at the page", "Clicking", "Filling a field", "Reading class analytics", "Searching materials", "Running code" or a title-cased raw name; a "×N" badge on repeats and, past two repeats, "The same step repeated — the agent may be stuck." A server-side tool shows as a collapsed "1 step" with no spinner. A "Using …" pill appears only for text that begins with "Using …" and never for narration bubbles (derived) (`AgentStepChain.tsx:198-361`; `ToolStatusIndicator.tsx:41-96`).
  - `m:streaming` ★ Answer streaming `[S]`: the text grows and re-renders as markdown; the Feedback toolbar is already visible and clickable; the composer and Send are disabled; **there is no Stop control**, no cursor and no progress element. The only aborts are programmatic, and "+" or choosing another session during a stream leaves the stream running (derived) (`ChatCore.tsx:938-957`; `ChatField.tsx:175-182`).
  - `m:answer-complete` Answer complete `[S]`: the composer re-enables and the chain collapses; nothing else changes and focus is not returned to the composer.
  - `m:feedback` Feedback `[S]`: a thumb sets local state and saves an interaction; both thumbs then turn grey and disabled, so the chosen thumb is not coloured; no toast or thanks; Flag opens the browser `prompt()` and runs even if cancelled (`Feedback.tsx:21-44`).
  - `m:error` Errors `[S]`: snackbar texts "No token found, please login again" (the loader stays and the composer stays disabled), "Token expired, refreshing...", "Token refreshed, retrying...", "An error occurred: {statusText}", "Server error ({status}). Please try again.", "You are out of tokens." (429), "You are not allowed to use this feature." (403), "Connection lost. Reconnecting..." (any network error; the library retries about once a second), and an error bubble "Something went wrong while answering. Please try again." with "Retry"; backend errors arrive as an ordinary tutor bubble "Error: {raw message}"; the loop cap "I went in circles trying to complete that on the page and stopped to avoid looping…" (`ChatCore.tsx:759-996,658-675`).
  - `m:offline` Offline `[S]`: **no offline detection exists** (no `navigator.onLine` and no listener); a network failure follows the "Connection lost" path.
  - `m:error` Rate limit or model unavailable `[S]`: "You are out of tokens." and the generic bubble; no dedicated model-unavailable message.
  - `m:ov:oauth-dialog` MCP tool needs authorisation `[S]`: the tutor bubble carries "Authorization required for {serverName}" and the dialog opens; "Connect Account" stores `sessionStorage["mcp_oauth_return"]` = `{ classId, pdfId }` and leaves for the provider; the return lands on **S-10** because `ProtectedRoute` navigates to `/class/{classId}` (D-6, SF-03) (`ChatCore.tsx:862-892,1011-1023`).
  - `m:feedback` Regenerate `[S]`: permanently disabled with no reason; the tooltip likely never opens because the disabled button is not wrapped (derived).
  - `m:history` History `[S]`: as in block 4; a failed fetch looks like "No previous sessions"; selecting a row has no loading indicator.
  - `m:experiment-compare@I,A` Experiment compare `[S]`: two columns at container width of at least 680 px, otherwise stacked; column headers "A" / "B", the variant label and key, an engine chip, a spinner, a "Clear this column" button; each column repeats the welcome row and toolbars (`ExperimentCompare.tsx:22-124`).
  - `m:answer-complete` Page context `[S]`: no chip or label shows the open PDF or page; "[Page N]" citation buttons render under answers, and in the floating panel their click handler is a no-op because `ChatContext` is `{}` outside the class layout (derived).
- **Answer rendering (as-is):** `react-markdown` 10 with `remark-math` and `rehype-katex`; **no GFM, so tables and strikethrough are not interpreted**; paragraphs are `margin: 0` with `line-height: 1.618`; headings, lists and quotes take browser defaults; inline code has no background; a fenced block with a language becomes an outlined card (radius 12) whose title is the raw language token in an `h5`, a contained button group "Import to Scratchpad" and "Copy" (which reads "Copied" and never reverts), and a Prism "tomorrow" block (`#2d2d2d`, text `#ccc`); a fence without a language is a bare `<pre>`. **Display math is a bare KaTeX block** (`margin: 1em 0; text-align: center`) with no wrapper, box or overflow rule (`ChatMessages.tsx:161-244`). Links are default anchors; images have no size limit. Font Montserrat; the answer wrapper is 0.875rem (14 px); **no measure limit**, so the text column is about 828 px in the modal, 348 px in the default side panel and 248 px at the minimum width.
- **Literal colours in the chat files:** `#77777F` (session label, Experiment label), `rgba(87, 6, 140, 0.04)` and `rgba(87, 6, 140, 0.08)` (Experiment panel, compare slot chip), `#4a0577`, `#fff`, `rgba(255,255,255,0.22)` (variant buttons), the switch track `rgba(0,0,0,0.25)`, the backdrops `rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.3)` and `rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.5)`; `ChatMessages.tsx` uses theme keys and the name "white" only (`ChatCore.tsx:1124,1140,1152-1158,1317,1338-1339`; `ExperimentCompare.tsx:70`).
- **State signals in code today (for the companion):** `botAnswering` (true from send to "stop", close or error; false while a browser tool runs); per-message `typing`, `streaming` (`"null"` for loaders, `null` mid-stream, `"stop"` at the end), `error`, `uniqueId`, `toolExecution` and `agentSteps`; `turnStepsRef` for the latest tool; `oAuthRequired`, `snackbarMessage`, `currentConversationId`. **Not present:** an `isStreaming` variable, online status, a typing or focus flag, a panel-level error flag, a user-abort flag, a regenerating flag and any panel status enum (`ChatCore.tsx:280-343`; `ChatMessages.tsx:49-76`).
- **Primary CTA:** the unlabelled Send icon button (`ChatField.tsx:175-182`); the launcher opens the panel. No labelled filled button except "Retry" on an error row and "Connect Account" in the dialog.
- **Exit / next:** "Import to Scratchpad" → S-03 and closes the panel (`GlobalChatModal.tsx:101-109`); navigation actions in a reply move to S-02 or S-04 (`:69-95`, no message in the panel); the OAuth round trip; the close button.
- **Stage 1 hooks:** the tutor avatar on every tutor row (mascot, 32 px, G9); the composer edge for the peek; the empty conversation (the welcome row is the existing surface for the "no conversation yet" illustration); the Display menu does not live here; the Stop control and the streaming state are undrawn today; the welcome copy; the session label.
- **Companion (Phase 4):** (1) **Tutor avatar**, `companion.slot.avatar` 32 px on every tutor row (12 px gap to the answer column), on the `--companion-backing` disc in dark mode; only the newest tutor message's avatar animates, through the seven avatar states of `DESIGN.md § Companion` (`walk` while waiting or while a tool runs before any text, the shallow `speaking` dip while text streams, one `hop` of about 1.2 s at completion, a `wave` on Thumbs up, the `error` pose on the error row, `offline` asleep, the `idle` still otherwise); older rows and the welcome row hold the idle still; in experiment compare mode only the avatar of the most recently started answer animates (ties: column A), the other column holds stills. (2) **Composer peek**: `peek` and `retract` belong to the composer, not the avatar. `companion.slot.peek` 48 px anchored to the composer field's top-right corner: its right edge on the field's right edge and its bottom edge on the field's top edge, inside the 48 px lane formed by the message region's 24 px bottom padding and the composer's 24 px top padding (raised from 12 px for the lane). It shows once per focus session, at the first keystroke after the student focused the composer by pointer or keyboard (the focus the app hands back after an answer starts no session), only while the message region is at scroll end (so it never overlaps text) and the newest avatar is still; it retracts 1.5 s after the last keystroke or on blur, reversing from the current drawing; it is cut, with no retract, on send (`walk` has priority) and when the slash-command menu opens in its lane; it is absent whenever motion is off at any level. (3) **Empty conversation**: the welcome row renders as the empty-state frame's inline variant, `companion.slot.inline` 64 px idle still above the welcome line. (4) **Launcher**: the locked 56 px `--accent-solid` disc carries the companion's portrait silhouette with a filled speech bubble (`launcher-glyph.svg`, `companion.slot.launcher` 28 px, `--accent-on-solid`); it never animates. (5) **Announcements**: the error row is `role="alert"`; the composer's offline line is `role="status"`, set once after the 2 s hysteresis (Stop stays enabled while an answer was pending; the field is disabled with the offline reason); completion is announced once by a polite live region ("Kiwi finished answering."), after which focus moves to the composer, which announces only its own name; a like is a polite live region ("Feedback saved.") plus the pressed thumb. Sizes are the same at 390 px (the panel is fullscreen). Signals the state machine needs beyond those listed above: a per-focus-session typing flag with a 1.5 s expiry, `isStreaming`, online status with 2 s hysteresis, a user-abort flag, scroll-end detection on the message region (implementation plan). Every mascot element is `aria-hidden`; the text twins are in the state table.
- **Tour defects (in boundary):** TD-C1 (heuristic 4, sev 1); TD-05 (a purple round launcher; heuristic 4, sev 1); TD-09 (four purple display-mode icons and a close icon in the header; heuristic 4, sev 1); TD-10 (the capitalised "NEW SESSION" label; the string is sentence case and the capitals are CSS; heuristic 4, sev 1); TD-11 (the tutor avatar is a purple circle with a robot icon; heuristic 4, sev 1); TD-12 ("Welcome to Kiwi! How can I help you?" with an exclamation mark; heuristic 2, sev 1); TD-13 (the model select's dropdown arrow overlaps the model name, "ChatGP▾"; B3; heuristic 1, sev 2; the upstream fix is on `NYUSH-ML/kiwi-ui` branch `fix/model-select-arrow-spacing`); TD-24 (the history rows show `toLocaleDateString()`, `ChatCore.tsx:1427`; heuristic 4, sev 2).
- **Out of boundary, recorded:** TD-18 (the launcher's resting position covers content on app pages and admin pages; it is draggable, which mitigates).
- **Source-found:** SF-03 (the student OAuth return lands on S-10); SF-08 (the launcher's count badge is coded and never shows); SF-52 (no Stop control while streaming, and "+" and the session list stay clickable mid-stream); SF-53 (Regenerate and Share are permanently disabled with no reason); SF-54 (no offline detection; `ChatComponent` is dead code that still carries the old copy); SF-55 (the citation buttons and the slash-menu list depend on a context the floating panel does not have; display strings such as "Using …" and step labels are English templates).
- **Not observed live:** every state; the tour saw the empty panel in modal and snap modes, the header icons, the session bar, the avatar, the welcome copy, the model select and the composer (no message was sent).

### SR-7 · Experiment Assistant (floating panel)

- **Source:** `src/pages/admin/experiments/ExperimentAssistant.tsx:144-680`. Mounted by A-19 (`ExperimentList.tsx:241`), A-20 (`ExperimentForm.tsx:1241`) and A-21 (`ExperimentDetail.tsx:426`); each page has its own instance and module-level variables carry state between them (`ExperimentAssistant.tsx:121-133`).
- **Appears on:** A-19 in every state; A-20 except while an edit load shows its spinner (`ExperimentForm.tsx:607-612`); A-21 except during its spinner and not-found returns, and the spinner returns after every Activate, Re-activate or Turn off (`ExperimentDetail.tsx:93-95,127-134,156-163`).
- **Roles / Guard:** inherits the host page; no role gate inside (B1, D-5). **Density:** compact. **Paper surfaces:** none.
- **Opening rule:** `useState(() => persistedOpen ?? /\/experiments\/?$/.test(location.pathname))` (`ExperimentAssistant.tsx:167-169`). With no earlier state this session the panel starts **open on the list page** (path ends in `/experiments`) and as a launcher on the form and detail pages; afterwards the last open or closed state is reused. It resets when the class changes or on a full reload (`:129,153-161,188-190`). A draft reply does not open it; it navigates to A-20 (`:221-230`).
- **Content blocks (in order):**
  1. Launcher (closed) `m:launcher`: a 56 px circular button, aria-label "open experiment assistant", white with a 2 px `#6a1b9a` border, a sparkle icon (`AutoAwesomeOutlined`) and a violet shadow, `z-index` 1250, draggable, no tooltip or visible text — `:411-454`.
  2. Panel (open) `m:panel-open`: MUI `Paper`, elevation 12, fixed, `z-index` 1250, 390 x 560 by default, clamped to the viewport, minimum 320 x 360; non-modal (no backdrop, no focus trap, no `role`), so the page behind stays usable — `:55-58,76-105,457-482`. Derived at 1440 x 900 with the sidebar open: panel x 794-1184, y 316-876, which covers the main content right of centre. It sits above the top bar (1000) and the Kiwi Chat launcher (1200) and below the Kiwi Chat panel (modal, 1300) (`TopNav.tsx:88`; `GlobalChatButton.tsx:239`; `GlobalChatModal.tsx:149`).
  3. Header, the drag handle: `linear-gradient(135deg, #6a1b9a 0%, #616161 100%)` with white text, an avatar with a sparkle icon, title "Experiment Assistant", caption "Describe it · I'll draft the config", and a white close icon with no accessible name — `:484-517`; `experimentUi.tsx:35-37`.
  4. Message area on a faint violet tint, auto-scrolling; empty state: a 56 px circle with a sparkle icon and "Describe the experiment you want — e.g. "Two groups 50/50: control on openai with default prompts, treatment on qwen that never gives direct answers." I'll draft the config and open it for review." — `:520-548`.
  5. Bubbles: plain `pre-wrap` text, 14 px, no markdown, no names or timestamps; user bubbles on the violet gradient, assistant bubbles white with a violet hairline — `:549-576`. A three-dot typing indicator while waiting (`:577-581`). After a draft, a text button `m:draft-json` "View last draft JSON" / "Hide last draft JSON" revealing a `<pre>` of the config (`:585-614`).
  6. Composer: multiline field with placeholder "Describe your experiment…", Enter sends and Shift+Enter breaks the line; a 40 px send icon button on the gradient with no accessible name — `:616-648`.
  7. Resize grip, aria-label "resize experiment assistant" — `:649-672`.
- **States:**
  - `m:waiting` Loading `[S]`: the typing indicator; Send disabled; no stop or cancel (`:386`).
  - `m:empty` Empty `[S]`: block 4.
  - `m:error` Error `[S]`: an assistant-style bubble "⚠️ {e.message}", which stays in the history and is sent with the next request; no retry (`:387-402`). A server-side failure because the chat model is not configured arrives as an ordinary reply: "The design assistant is unavailable — the server's chat model isn't configured. Set LLM_API_KEY (or QWEN_API_KEY) in the backend .env and restart, then try again." (`../kiwi/src/experiment/experiment.service.ts:107-115`): developer-facing text.
  - `m:reply` `m:to:A-20` Success `[S]`: the reply is shown unmodified, possibly with a fenced JSON block as plain text; a valid config makes the panel navigate to A-20 with no toast (`:396-397`).
  - Page-specific `[S]`: conversation, input and positions persist across experiment pages; switching class clears messages (`:153-161`); no control to clear the conversation; the close icon is the only way to hide the panel (no Escape, no click-away, `:514`).
- **Primary CTA:** the unlabelled Send icon button → posts the whole conversation and, if the reply holds a config, opens A-20 prefilled (`:385-406,634-647`). There is no labelled filled button.
- **Exit / next:** close icon → launcher at its saved position; a draft → A-20.
- **Stage 1 hooks:** none allowed here; the panel is a second AI surface beside SR-6 (two floating launchers with different icons, `AutoAwesomeOutlined` and `Chat`).
- **Tour defects (in boundary):** TD-C1 (heuristic 4, sev 1); TD-31 (heuristic 8, sev 1).
- **Out of boundary, recorded:** TD-30 (opens by itself on A-19 and covers the empty state and its call to action).
- **Source-found:** SF-18 (icon-only close and send controls with no accessible name; no `role` or `aria-live` on the panel).
- **Not observed live:** only the auto-open on the empty list was seen in the tour; all other states are source-only.
