**Student, auth and fallback pages (comfortable density, S-01 to S-10)**

### S-01 · Course list — `/`

- **Source:** `src/pages/Courses.tsx:11` (route `src/App.tsx:60-66`); `src/components/HeroCarousel/HeroCarousel.tsx:15`, `src/components/ClassCard/ClassCard.tsx:21`, `src/components/ResponsiveGrid/ResponsiveGrid.tsx:17`; SR-1.
- **Roles (Appendix A):** S, I, A. **Guard (code):** `ProtectedRoute`: any signed-in user, consent redirect when configured. Instructors and admins are not redirected to `/admin` from here; they get the view-switch icon in SR-1 (seen live with an admin account `[L]`), and an instructor or admin who is enrolled in a class sees its card, as S does (the Experiment row needs a class in context, so it is absent here).
- **Density:** comfortable · **Paper surfaces:** none · **Never recoloured:** the class cover images (`imageSnippetUrl`). · **Density overrides:** none.
- **Purpose:** Lets a signed-in user see their enrolled classes and open one.
- **Entry points:** the student sign-in redirect (`src/pages/Login.tsx:77`, `src/pages/GoogleRedirectHandler.tsx:48`) and its fallbacks (`GoogleRedirectHandler.tsx:59,66`); `UnprotectedRoute` for signed-in students visiting `/login`, `/register` or `/auth/callback` (`src/auth/UnprotectedRoute.tsx:23-25`); `AdminProtectedRoute` for users who are neither admin nor instructor (`src/auth/AdminProtectedRoute.tsx:41`); the top-bar logo on student routes (`TopNav.tsx:124`) and the "Switch to Student View" icon (`:208-218`); the end of the consent flow (`src/pages/Consent.tsx:37,46`).
- **Content blocks (in order):**
  1. SR-1 with no class context (no switcher or label): logo, the view switch (I and A), logout — `Courses.tsx:39`.
  2. Hero banner (`HeroCarousel.tsx`): three full-bleed slides, a green (`#3f7d58` to `#1f4a34`), a blue (`#2f5d8a` to `#16324d`) and a violet (`#7a5c9e` to `#3d2c52`) gradient SVG with two translucent white circles each (`public/assets/images/bg-hero-1.svg`, `-2`, `-3`; alt texts "Decorative green gradient", "Decorative blue gradient", "Decorative violet gradient", `HeroCarousel.tsx:10-12`), cross-fading every 8 s over 1.2 s, infinite, with no dots or arrows; reduced motion shortens the fade to 250 ms and stops the zoom (`:17,21-32,70`). Over them a dark gradient and the `h1` "Welcome to {BRAND_LABEL}, {username}!" in white, bold, 2rem from `md`, where `{username}` is the part of the email before "@" (`:18-19,77-106`). Height 208 / 236 / 280 px by breakpoint, radius 4, up to 1440 px wide (`:35-48`).
  3. Class grid: `repeat(auto-fill, minmax(310px, 1fr))`, gap 40 px, 48 px below the hero (`ResponsiveGrid.tsx:17-31`; `Courses.tsx:60-71`). One `ClassCard` per enrolled class, a link `aria-label="Open {title}"` to `/{classId}` carrying `{ className }` in router state: a 252 px card with a 152 px cover (the class image under a faint bottom fade, or, with none or on load failure, a grey gradient with the italic `KiwiWordmark` in `grey.500` at 50% opacity), and a content panel overlapping the cover by 16 px (28 px on hover or focus) with a translucent paper fill and backdrop blur (reduced-transparency and reduced-motion variants exist); title (2-line clamp), instructor (one line) — `ClassCard.tsx:7-229`. The fallbacks differ: `Courses.tsx` passes "Class" and "TBD", while the card falls back to "Unnamed class" and "Instructor TBD" (`Courses.tsx:64-65`; `ClassCard.tsx:30-31`).
  4. A 120 px spacer; the surface is `grey.100` in a container up to 1440 px with a 32 px top margin — `:40-58,73`.
  5. SR-6.
- **States:**
  - `m:loaded` Default `[T]`: the hero, headline and one class card (the demo class); the tour saw a violet-to-black slide on one load and a green-to-black one on another, a large decorative circle, and a light-grey canvas with large unused space beside one card.
  - `m:none` Loading `[S]`: **none**; the grid is empty until the request returns, with no skeleton or spinner (`Courses.tsx:14-30`).
  - `m:empty` Empty `[S]`: **no empty state.** With no enrolled classes the grid renders nothing under the hero: no text and no call to action (SF-07).
  - `m:empty` Error `[S]`: console-only (`:25-27`); the page looks the same as the empty case.
  - `m:none` Success `[S]`: none; nothing is submitted from this page.
  - `m:none` Image failure `[S]`: the card falls back to the wordmark placeholder (`ClassCard.tsx:96`).
  - `m:none` Hover and focus `[S]`: the content panel rises; focus ring 2 px `primary.main` with a 2 px offset (`ClassCard.tsx:56-61,217-222`).
- **Primary CTA:** none filled; the page's job is "open a class card" → `/{classId}` (S-02) (`Courses.tsx:67`).
- **Exit / next:** S-02 via a card; SR-1 (view switch to `/admin`, logout); SR-6.
- **Stage 1 hooks:** the hero (colour, gradient and type are in scope; its region is not to move); the class-card image fallback (the mascot pose replaces the wordmark, G9); the "no classes" empty state, which does not exist yet (G9 lists it as a mascot placement); the Display-menu button through SR-1.
- **Companion (Phase 4):** (1) **No classes** (the empty state SF-07 records as missing): the empty-state frame on the ruled column with `companion.slot.empty` 96 px (64 below `md`), the idle still, no-action variant for students (JOURNEY D-4). (2) **Class-card image fallback**: `companion.slot.inline` 64 px idle still centred on the card's 152 px cover, a flat `--surface-hover` tile (replaces the grey wordmark, TD-04). (3) **Load error** (console-only today): the frame's error state with the error still at 96 px (64 below `md`), the cross icon, the error copy and Retry. The `--companion-backing` disc sits under each still in dark mode.
- **Tour defects (in boundary):** TD-C1 (heuristic 4, sev 1); TD-01 (a rotating gradient hero with decorative circles; heuristic 8, sev 1); TD-02 (the headline "Welcome to Kiwi, ⟨username⟩!" ends in an exclamation mark, against the voice rule, G12; heuristic 2, sev 1); TD-03 (Montserrat is set as a heading font; the type is theme-wide, `src/Theme.ts:92`; heuristic 4, sev 1); TD-04 (a grey gradient with the Montserrat wordmark at 50% opacity on a card without an image; heuristic 8, sev 1); TD-07 (heuristic 4, sev 1, via SR-1); TD-05, TD-09, TD-10, TD-11 (heuristic 4, sev 1), TD-12 (heuristic 2, sev 1) and TD-13 (heuristic 1, sev 2), all via SR-6.
- **Out of boundary, recorded:** TD-06 (a light-grey canvas with large unused space when there is one class: the canvas colour follows the tokens, the unused space is layout).
- **Source-found:** SF-07 (no loading, empty or error state, and two different fallbacks for a missing instructor).
- **Not observed live:** empty, error, image-failure and hover states; the other slides on one load.

### S-02 · Materials — `/:classID`

- **Source:** `src/pages/ClassChat.tsx:11` (route `src/App.tsx:120`, inside SR-2), which renders only `PDFComponent` (`src/components/Chat/PDFComponent.tsx:65`) and so `PDFViewerForStudents` (`src/components/PDFViewer/PDFViewerForStudent.tsx:89`). Despite its name the page holds no chat: chat is the global panel SR-6 (`ClassChat.tsx:7-10`).
- **Roles (Appendix A):** S, I, A. **Guard (code):** `ProtectedRoute` (`src/App.tsx:112-124`); class access is decided by the backend. **Entry visibility:** the first tab, "Materials", of every class. **Role-specific chrome (I, A):** SR-1's view-switch icon and SR-6's Experiment toggle row (SR-6e); students have neither. The student comment and summary reads need an enrollment (`../kiwi/src/document/document.service.ts:1512`), so an unenrolled instructor or admin sees no comment pins and no summary banner.
- **Density:** comfortable · **Paper surfaces:** the PDF pages (react-pdf pages on a white wrapper, `PDFViewerForStudent.tsx:660-683`). **Never recoloured:** the rendered PDF canvas and its figures; the "💬" glyph on comment pins. · **Density overrides:** the PDF pages and summary text are reading surfaces (comfortable); the icon rail, the "Class Materials" drawer, the 44 px viewer toolbar and the summary banner are tool chrome (compact).
- **Purpose:** Lets an enrolled student pick a lecture or recitation PDF from the class's list and read it page by page, while the open PDF and page are shared with the global chat.
- **Entry points:** a class card on S-01 (`src/pages/Courses.tsx:62-69`); tab "Materials" (`src/pages/ClassLayout.tsx:52,130-136`); the class switcher, which keeps the suffix; the chat's navigation actions to `/{classId}` and `/{classId}?pdfId=…&page=…` (`GlobalChatModal.tsx:68-91`) — no code reads `pdfId` or `page` from the URL (grep), so the query has no effect; the chat's citation buttons "[Page N]" switch the open PDF through context (`src/components/Chat/ChatMessages.tsx:394,408`).
- **Content blocks (in order):**
  1. SR-1 and the SR-2 tab bar. `ClassChat` pushes tab "materials" and mirrors class id, PDF id, 0-based page, material type and `pdfContent` to the global chat (`ClassChat.tsx:26-54`).
  2. Fallback `<h2>Loading class...</h2>`, in effect unreachable because SR-2 shows a spinner first (`ClassChat.tsx:56-62`; `pages/ClassLayout.tsx:363-376`).
  3. Icon rail, 48 px wide, holding one 36 px icon button with a folder glyph, native `title="Class materials"` and no aria-label; it toggles the drawer — `PDFComponent.tsx:376-408`.
  4. Viewer toolbar, 44 px: the current file name; icon-only buttons with aria-label only — "previous page", the label "{current} / {total}" (or "– / –"), "next page", "zoom out", "{n}%" (100%, step 0.2, min 0.5, max 3), "zoom in", "fullscreen" (which fullscreens only the scroll area) — `PDFViewerForStudent.tsx:540-616`. While no document is chosen the whole viewer, toolbar included, is replaced by the bare line "Loading your class lectures..." with no spinner (`:427-429`).
  5. Scroll area on a literal `#EDEBF1`, 20 px padding: a centred spinner while loading; the error panel; or the page stack. Each page sits on a white wrapper with a shadow `0 1px 4px rgba(28,12,48,0.14)`; **the current page carries a 2 px outline in `primary.main` (`#6a1b9a`)**, marking the page the AI has in context; text and annotation layers are on; professor comments appear as 24 px round blue pins (`#1976d2`) that open a 280 px popover `m:ov:comment-popover` — `PDFViewerForStudent.tsx:618-702,684-693,362-411`.
  6. Overlay `m:loaded`, the "Class Materials" drawer, open by default, 248 px, overlaying the left of the viewer (`PDFComponent.tsx:423-439`): title "Class Materials", an icon-only close, a segmented switch "Lecture" · "Recitation", then the file list (three skeleton rows while loading; "No documents"; one row per file showing the raw file name and a "✓" on the selected one). Picking a row loads that PDF and keeps the drawer open — `:457-565`.
  7. Overlay `m:ov:summary-banner`, the summary banner: a floating Paper, blue `#1976d2`, "Professor added {n} summary" / "Professor added {n} summaries", a filled white "Read" and an icon-only close; after dismissal a round blue book button with a red count badge re-shows it — `PDFViewerForStudent.tsx:465-538`.
  8. Overlay `m:ov:summaries-dialog`, Dialog "Document Summaries": a count, per summary "Summary {n}", the text and "Added by:" with the author name or email, "No summaries available for this document.", filled "Close" — `PDFViewerForStudent.tsx:715-778`.
  9. Overlay `m:native:pdf-password`: the browser's own `prompt()` "Enter the password to open this PDF file." for password-protected PDFs (react-pdf default, unverified at runtime).
- **States:**
  - `m:loaded` Default (loaded) `[T]`: the toolbar shows "1 / 1" and "100%", the page has a purple outline, the drawer lists the class's files with the selected one tinted and ticked, and the Lecture / Recitation switch.
  - `m:loading` Loading `[S]`: three skeleton rows in the drawer plus "Loading your class lectures..."; after picking a row the same line until the URL arrives; then a spinner with "– / –"; per page the library's "Loading page…" (`PDFComponent.tsx:297-301,516-521`; `PDFViewerForStudent.tsx:576,633-643`).
  - `m:empty` Empty `[S]`: the drawer shows "No documents" while the viewer keeps "Loading your class lectures..." **indefinitely**, with no dedicated empty copy and no spinner (`PDFComponent.tsx:201-202,522-527`) (SF-33).
  - `m:error` Error `[S]`: red text "Couldn't load this PDF. This can happen on a slow or unstable network connection." with a filled "Retry" (`PDFViewerForStudent.tsx:431-451`). Document-list and document-URL failures have no catch, so the skeletons or the loading line persist; comment and summary failures are console-only (`PDFComponent.tsx:157-160,190-253,286-312`).
  - `m:none` Success `[S]`: none; the chosen PDF and page are saved silently to `localStorage` (`materialsPdf_{classId}`, `PDFComponent.tsx:39,314-334`).
  - Page-specific `[S]`: no search UI on this page; no disabled controls (previous, next and zoom clamp silently); the last PDF and page are restored per class; only documents of type "class materials" or "recitation" are listed (`PDFComponent.tsx:362-364`).
- **Primary CTA:** none designated; the main action is choosing a file row (a text row, not filled). Filled buttons: "Retry" (error only), "Read" (banner), "Close" (dialog).
- **Exit / next:** tab "Scratchpad" → S-03; app tabs → S-04; the class switcher; the global chat can navigate back here.
- **Stage 1 hooks:** the neutral frame for the paper page (G5); the drawer's "No documents" and the viewer's loading line (empty-state frame); the lilac greys and blue literals listed in SF-32 move to tokens.
- **Tour defects (in boundary):** TD-C1 (heuristic 4, sev 1); TD-08 (the 2 px purple outline on the PDF page, where paper should keep a neutral frame; heuristic 4, sev 1); TD-07 (heuristic 4, sev 1, via SR-1); TD-05, TD-09, TD-10, TD-11 (heuristic 4, sev 1), TD-12 (heuristic 2, sev 1) and TD-13 (heuristic 1, sev 2), all via SR-6.
- **Out of boundary, recorded:** TD-18 (the launcher's resting position; the tour recorded the overlap on S-04 and on admin pages, not here); B2 does not reach this page: the class lookups behind it answer 200 for every signed-in role, and the one enrollment-sensitive request, `POST /document`, answers 404 with an `alert()`, not 401 ([Role outcomes](#role-outcomes), SF-64).
- **Source-found:** SF-32 (the PDF rail, drawer, toolbar and banner use hard-coded lilac greys `#FAF9FC`, `#E5E1EB`, `#EFE8F6`, `#F3F1F7`, `#EDEBF1`, `#77777F`, `#55555E`, `#2A2A30` and a literal blue `#1976d2` equal to `info.main`, `PDFComponent.tsx:380-565`, `PDFViewerForStudent.tsx:475-538`); SF-33 (an empty class shows "Loading your class lectures..." forever); SF-34 (the icon rail button has only a native title, and the toolbar buttons only aria-labels); SF-64 (the class shell opens for any role and any class).
- **Not observed live:** every state except the default, the banner, the dialog and the comment pins (the demo class has none).

### S-03 · Code space — `/:classID/code`

- **Source:** `src/pages/ClassIDE.tsx:68` (route `src/App.tsx:121`, inside SR-2) → `src/components/CodeInterpreter/CodeInterpreter.tsx` → `IDE.tsx:72`; `FileTabs.tsx`, `Pyodide.tsx`, `WebR.tsx`; workers `public/assets/pyodide.worker.js`. `IDE.css` is not imported anywhere.
- **Roles (Appendix A):** S, I, A. **Guard (code):** `ProtectedRoute` only. **Entry visibility / feature gate:** the feature flag `RLanguageClass` (a user-level grant anywhere, a class-level grant for this class) shows the language select and, for R, the "Plots" and "Vars" tabs (`ClassIDE.tsx:132-139`; `IDE.tsx:429-460,605-610`). No role check. **Role-specific chrome (I, A):** SR-1's view-switch icon and SR-6's Experiment toggle row (SR-6e); students have neither.
- **Density:** comfortable · **Paper surfaces:** none (the editor, output console and code blocks follow the mode and are not paper). **Never recoloured:** R plot images (PNG data URLs) and uploaded image previews (`IDE.tsx:331-332,374-384,657-665`). · **Density overrides:** the file-tab bar, upload row, language select, editor overlay buttons and output tab strip are tool chrome (compact); the output text is the nearest thing to a reading surface.
- **Purpose:** A scratch editor where a student writes and runs Python (R if the class has it), reads the output, and can hand the code and output to Kiwi Chat.
- **Entry points:** tab "Scratchpad" (`src/pages/ClassLayout.tsx:53,130-136`); a typed URL; the class switcher; the chat's code-block button "Import to Scratchpad" (`src/components/Chat/ChatMessages.tsx:110,217-219`), which stores the code, navigates here and closes the chat (`GlobalChatModal.tsx:101-109`; loaded at `ClassIDE.tsx:146-154`).
- **Content blocks (in order):**
  1. SR-1 and the SR-2 tab bar. The page tells the chat it is the tab "materials", view "code", app slug "codegen", tutoring mode "guidance", and sends the code (first 6,000 characters), output (first 2,000) and language as context (`ClassIDE.tsx:91-111,187-201`).
  2. Page frame: 16 px padding; an inner column of 960 px by default (minimum 480, maximum 100%), its width stored in `localStorage` `ideEditorWidth` and changed by a drag handle on its right edge (aria-label "resize editor") — `ClassIDE.tsx:252-269,298-329`.
  3. The "card": a full-height stack on `background.default`, with **no page title, heading, border, radius or shadow**. "Code Space" is only the first tab's label (`ClassIDE.tsx:143`); the document title is static "Kiwi" (`index.html:7`) — `IDE.tsx:387-392`.
  4. File-tab bar (aria-label "basic tabs example"): the first tab "Code Space" (no close), then a tab per opened file with an icon-only close button with no label — `FileTabs.tsx:162-187`.
  5. Upload row: an outlined small button "Upload" with a cloud icon wrapping a hidden multi-file input; then one **filled** 30 px button per file in the sandbox (uploaded names, or `code-<timestamp>.txt` from Save Code) that opens it as a tab — `FileTabs.tsx:196-255`.
  6. Language selector, only with `allowR`: a select with options "Python" and "R" and the caption "Python" or "R Language" — `IDE.tsx:429-460`.
  7. Editor (Ace): theme "clouds", 14 px, mode python / r / text, border `1px solid alpha(divider, 0.2)`, radius 4; starts empty unless restored from `localStorage` `ideState_{classId}`. Colours from the Ace theme: editor `#FFFFFF`, gutter `#ebebeb`, **active line `#FFFBD1` (pale yellow)**, selection `#BDD5FC` (`node_modules/ace-builds/src-noconflict/theme-clouds.js`). A `.txt` tab shows that file; other types show the editable text "This file type is not supported."; a lost buffer shows "File is no longer available. Please re-upload." — `IDE.tsx:463-486,313-337`.
  8. Editor overlay controls at the bottom right: an icon-only **Run** (play, `color="primary"`) and an icon-only **Upload** (file-upload icon, `color="info"`, which is `#1976d2` blue), neither with a label or tooltip — `IDE.tsx:487-521`. Run opens the Snackbar at once.
  9. Output tab strip: "Output"; for R also "Plots ({n})" and "Vars ({n})"; output panel: monospace `<pre>` showing `{output || "Output will appear here."}`; "No plots generated."; a "Vars" table with "Name" and "Preview" or "No variables." — `IDE.tsx:586-737`.
  10. Bottom action row, three **filled** buttons: "Move to Chat", "Check Answer", "Save Code" — `IDE.tsx:741-817`.
  11. Overlays: a Snackbar `m:success` "Code executed and files updated." (bottom centre, 3,000 ms, `IDE.tsx:576-583`); the browser's own `prompt()` `m:native:input-prompt` for every `input("…")` call in user code (`:147-158`); the Kiwi Chat panel `m:to:SR-6` opened with a prefilled prompt by "Move to Chat" and "Check Answer" (`ClassIDE.tsx:245-249`).
- **States:**
  - `m:loaded` Default `[T]`: one tab "Code Space", an empty editor, "Output will appear here.", the three bottom buttons; Upload (outlined) and a language select where allowed.
  - `m:none` Loading `[S]`: **no loading UI for either runtime.** Pyodide's worker is created when the module first loads (inferred from the import chain, not observed) and loads Pyodide from `https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/pyodide/v0.25.0/full/pyodide.js` (`public/assets/pyodide.worker.js:3,12-32`); WebR loads from `https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/webr@0.6.0/dist/` on the first R run (`WebR.tsx:22-27`). The only feedback is the Snackbar at click.
  - `m:loaded` Empty `[S]`: "Output will appear here." (also after a run that printed nothing), "No plots generated.", "No variables.".
  - `m:r-tabs` R output tabs `[S]`: with the language select on R the output strip adds "Plots ({n})" and "Vars ({n})"; their panels read "No plots generated." and "No variables." when empty and otherwise show a plot image or the "Name" / "Preview" table (`IDE.tsx:586-737`).
  - `m:error` Error `[S]`: Python exceptions replace stdout with the raw traceback text in the same `<pre>` with no error styling (`pyodide.worker.js:235-236`; `IDE.tsx:229-231`); R errors arrive in the captured console text or as `evalError.message`; a guard string "Package installation/loading is not supported in the browser R environment. Only base packages are available." (`WebR.tsx:52-65,205-209`). Runtime failures (CDN unreachable) show nothing: neither worker has an `onerror` handler (inferred).
  - `m:success` Success `[S]`: the Snackbar shows at click **before the run finishes**, so it appears whatever the outcome; Save Code adds a file button with no confirmation.
  - Page-specific `[S]`: no running indicator and Run stays clickable; no control is ever disabled; "Check Answer" does not check anything, it only fills the chat input; "Save Code" does not download; files and file tabs are lost when the student leaves the tab, only the code persists (debounced 400 ms) (`ClassIDE.tsx:140-182`).
- **Primary CTA:** no labelled primary. The functional primary is the icon-only Run → `runCode` (`IDE.tsx:497-505`). **Three filled buttons sit side by side in one region** and are never disabled: "Move to Chat" (builds a prompt "I have the following solution for question #." with the code and output and opens the chat, `:750-775`), "Check Answer" (prompt "Check my answer for question #.", `:776-801`), "Save Code" (`:802-816`); the per-file buttons are filled too. **The capitals are CSS:** the DOM strings are "Move to Chat" and "Check Answer", set inside `<Typography variant="button">`, whose MUI default uppercases them (`IDE.tsx:766-774,792-800`; `src/Theme.ts:91-93`); "Save Code" is a plain child and reads as typed (`Theme.ts:95-101`).
- **Exit / next:** the chat panel opens with the prompt prefilled and unsent; the tab bar to S-02 or app tabs.
- **Stage 1 hooks:** the editor and output themes for both modes (brief OQ3: code editors are not paper); button emphasis; the unlabelled icon buttons.
- **Tour defects (in boundary):** TD-C1 (heuristic 4, sev 1); TD-14 (default light Ace theme with a pale-yellow active line and grey gutter, and an off-palette blue upload icon; heuristic 4, sev 1); TD-15 (three filled primary buttons in one region; heuristic 8, sev 2); TD-16 ("MOVE TO CHAT", "CHECK ANSWER" in capitals beside "Save Code"; heuristic 4, sev 1); TD-07 (heuristic 4, sev 1, via SR-1); TD-05, TD-09, TD-10, TD-11 (heuristic 4, sev 1), TD-12 (heuristic 2, sev 1) and TD-13 (heuristic 1, sev 2), all via SR-6.
- **Out of boundary, recorded:** TD-18 (the launcher's resting position; no overlap was recorded on this page).
- **Source-found:** SF-35 (the Pyodide and WebR runtimes are fetched from a public CDN, which the brief treats as unreliable in mainland China; outside Stage 1, recorded for the implementation plan); SF-36 ("Check Answer" and "Save Code" labels promise more than they do; the Snackbar claims success before the run ends); SF-37 (errors render as plain monospace text with no error colour or icon).
- **Not observed live:** every state beyond the default; the R tabs; both runtimes.

### S-04 · Kiwi App (student host frame) — `/:classID/app/:appSlug`

- **Source:** `src/pages/ClassAppPage.tsx:22` (route `src/App.tsx:122`, inside SR-2) rendering `ClassAppView` (`src/pages/ClassAppView.tsx:35`). **Only the host frame is specified; the embedded app's content is out of scope.**
- **Roles (Appendix A):** S, I, A. **Guard (code):** `ProtectedRoute` (`src/App.tsx:112-124`). **Entry visibility:** a tab in SR-2, labelled `tabLabel || name`, for each app enabled in the class; the tab list is not role-filtered (`src/pages/ClassLayout.tsx:106-114,262-278`). On a hard load of this URL the enabled-app list is empty, so the layout replaces the route with the first tab, "Materials" (R-5, seen live on 2026-10-16 with an admin account `[L]`: the typed URL `/local-css-demo/app/ics-r-lab` ended at `/local-css-demo` with no iframe; `ClassLayout.tsx:85,117-128,192-207`). **Role-specific chrome (I, A):** SR-1's view-switch icon and SR-6's Experiment toggle row (SR-6e); students have neither.
- **Density:** comfortable (the host chrome); the embedded app sets its own. · **Paper surfaces:** the Kiwi App iframe (`ClassAppView.tsx:656-669`): the embedded document stays light in dark mode (G5). **Never recoloured:** everything inside the iframe. · **Density overrides:** none drawn by the host.
- **Purpose:** Looks up one of the class's apps and embeds its class-tab UI in a full-height iframe as a tab of the student class view, passing class and user context and brokering the app's AI-chat, token, navigation and API-proxy requests.
- **Entry points:** the app tab in SR-2; a chat reply carrying a navigation action with target tab "app" (`src/components/Chat/ChatCore.tsx:894-900`; `GlobalChatModal.tsx:84-88`); `kiwi:navigate` from an embedded app (`ClassAppView.tsx:508-546`); the class switcher, which keeps the `/app/{slug}` suffix.
- **Content blocks (in order):**
  1. SR-1 and the SR-2 tab bar; the content area shows SR-2's spinner until the class loads.
  2. Lookup: a centred spinner with "Loading app..." (`ClassAppPage.tsx:133-151`).
  3. Lookup error: a centred red Alert with the text or "App not found": "Missing class ID or app slug", `App "{appSlug}" is not enabled for this class`, "Failed to load app"; no retry (`:153-168,86,102,124`).
  4. Iframe-load error: a centred red Alert "Failed to load {appName}", no retry (`ClassAppView.tsx:391-395,601-615`).
  5. Default frame: a full-size flex box, minimum height `calc(100vh - 180px)`, holding a loading veil (`background.paper`, spinner and "Loading {appName}...") over the iframe. The iframe is mounted at once, has `title={appName}`, `border: none`, `display: none` until `load`, no host-set background, and the sandbox "allow-scripts allow-same-origin allow-forms allow-popups allow-popups-to-escape-sandbox" (no `allow-downloads`); its `src` is the class-tab UI URL, else the app's `baseUrl`, plus `classId`, `appId`, `userId` and `uiSlug` — `ClassAppView.tsx:618-669,373-384`. **No toolbar, header or retry surrounds it.**
  6. Bridge handshake (no visible status): `kiwi:ready` → `kiwi:context` (class, user, role defaulting to "student", capabilities); `kiwi:askAI` streams a Kiwi Agent answer back; `kiwi:updateContext` feeds the floating chat; `kiwi:apiRequest` proxies named operations, with error texts such as "unknown op: {op}", "no class context yet", "missing scope(s): {list}"; `kiwi:navigate`, `kiwi:requestToken` and `kiwi:setChatDisplayMode` — `ClassAppView.tsx:424-578`.
  7. Over the frame, from the host: the Kiwi Chat launcher (SR-6), whose default resting place is **bottom-left on app tabs** (24 px from the left, 96 px above the bottom) because this page sets the active tab to "app"; the code comment says apps place their own primary actions bottom-right (`src/pages/ClassAppPage.tsx:42`; `GlobalChatButton.tsx:90-95,131-138`). Opening the chat panel in modal or side-panel mode dims the page, the iframe included.
- **States:**
  - `m:loaded` Default `[T]`: the app fills the content area (the tour saw "CSS R Lab" with its own wordmark, eyebrow, cards and left nav, English and Chinese headings mixed).
  - `m:loading` Loading `[S]`: "Loading app..." then the veil "Loading {appName}..."; the veil is the only progress indicator.
  - `m:none` Empty: none; a missing app gives the lookup error; with neither a class-tab UI nor a `baseUrl` the iframe gets an empty `src` and the host shows no message (outcome unverified).
  - `m:error:lookup` `m:error:iframe` Error `[S]`: blocks 3 and 4, fixed English strings; the real errors are logged only.
  - `m:none` Success: none.
  - Page-specific `[S]`: changing the class or slug re-runs the lookup; leaving the page clears the chat's app context; the lookup treats an app as enabled when it appears in the class list, without checking `isEnabled` (`ClassAppPage.tsx:99-104`); the iframe `src` may change, and reload, if the user id resolves after the first render (derived).
- **Primary CTA:** none drawn by the host. The only host-owned filled control in the region is the "Open Kiwi Chat" launcher.
- **Exit / next:** other tabs; app-initiated back, forward, home and class-scoped paths; the class switcher; chat navigation actions.
- **Stage 1 hooks:** the veil and error Alerts; the paper boundary; the launcher's size and elevation (position is Stage 2, TD-18).
- **Tour defects (in boundary):** TD-C1 (host surfaces; heuristic 4, sev 1); TD-07 (heuristic 4, sev 1, via SR-1); TD-05, TD-09, TD-10, TD-11 (heuristic 4, sev 1), TD-12 (heuristic 2, sev 1) and TD-13 (heuristic 1, sev 2), all via SR-6.
- **Out of boundary, recorded:** TD-17 (the embedded app styles itself: lowercase "kiwi" wordmark with a purple full stop, purple eyebrow, cards, left workspace nav; it changes only where it reads the public tokens, G5); TD-18 (on this page the launcher sits bottom-left and covers the app's sidebar progress label "Questions completed 2/5"); the tour's mixed-script headings ("… NA 与均值", "Demo · CSS 数据分析作业") are the G7 mixed-script case, rendered in the embedded app's own text.
- **Source-found:** SF-51 (host differences, see A-12).
- **Not observed live:** lookup and iframe errors and the handshake. The redirect on a direct load was seen live on 2026-10-16 `[L]` (R-5).

### S-05 · Sign-in — `/login`

- **Source:** `src/pages/Login.tsx:25` (route `src/App.tsx:68-74`); SR-5 (split variant); `src/components/Brand/KiwiWordmark.tsx`, `src/components/GoogleLoginButton/GoogleLoginButton.tsx:21`.
- **Roles (Appendix A):** all. **Guard (code):** `UnprotectedRoute`: signed-out only. A signed-in user is redirected to `/consent` first if consent is required and missing, otherwise to `/admin` (instructor, admin) or `/` (D-2; `src/auth/UnprotectedRoute.tsx:15-26`).
- **Density:** comfortable · **Paper surfaces:** none.
- **Purpose:** Signs a returning user in with email and password, or with Google where enabled.
- **Entry points:** any `ProtectedRoute` or `AdminProtectedRoute` visited signed out (`src/auth/ProtectedRoute.tsx:51-53`; `AdminProtectedRoute.tsx:15-17`); sign-out (R-3); the middleware's session end, `/login?timeout=true` (`src/utils/middleware.ts:46-52,64-71,80-88`); the "Log in" link on S-06 (`src/pages/Register.tsx:79-86`); a typed URL.
- **Content blocks (in order):**
  1. SR-5's aside from `md` (the wordmark, the quote, the sub-line).
  2. Session-expired banner, a warning Alert "Your session has expired. Please log in again.", only with `?timeout=true` — `Login.tsx:100-117`.
  3. Below `md`, `InstitutionLogo` above the heading — `:119-128`.
  4. `h1` "Welcome back to " followed by the `KiwiWordmark` (Montserrat 800 italic), `nowrap` from `md` — `:129-147`; the subtitle "Continue learning with AI-powered support." — `:148-159`.
  5. An error Alert "Login failed. Please check your credentials and try again." — `:161-168,92-94`.
  6. With password login on (the default): a form with "Email" (type email, required) and "Password" (required, with an icon button aria-labelled "Show password" / "Hide password"), and a full-width filled pill button "Login" (radius 999, 48 px) — `:170-264`.
  7. With Google on: a divider "OR" (only beside the password form) and an outlined `GoogleLoginButton` "Continue with Google" with the four-colour Google mark (literal brand hex values) — `:266-280`; `GoogleLoginButton.tsx:60-66`.
  8. With neither on: a warning Alert reading **"No sign-in method is enabled. Unset VITE_DISABLE_PASSWORD_LOGIN or set VITE_ENABLE_GOOGLE_LOGIN=true."** — `:282-287`.
  9. The footer line: with Google on "Don't have an account? " and a link "Create one" to `/register`; otherwise "Need an account? Ask your administrator." — `:289-313`.
- **States:**
  - `m:loaded` Default `[T]`: the aside, "Welcome back to Kiwi", a purple pill "Login", "Need an account? Ask your administrator."
  - `m:timeout-banner` Session expired `[T]`: an orange-tinted box above the heading.
  - `m:submitting` Submitting `[S]`: the fields and the visibility toggle are disabled and the button shows a spinner and "Signing in…" (`:241-264`).
  - `m:none` Loading, empty: none (the page is a form; the only wait is the submitting state above).
  - `m:error` Error `[S]`: the error Alert; typing clears it. Every failure gets the same message, whether wrong credentials, network, or a failing profile or consent call (`:90-94,185-188`).
  - `m:to:S-01` `m:to:A-01` Success `[S]`: the role is fetched; instructors and admins go to `/admin`, everyone else to `/`; if the role call fails the user is treated as a student (`:59-89`).
  - `m:config:google` `m:config:google-only` `m:config:neither` Configuration `[S]`: password only; password plus Google (the divider, the Google button, "Create one"); Google only (no form, no divider); neither (the warning above).
- **Primary CTA:** "Login" → authenticates and redirects (`:241-264`). The Google button is outlined in this layout.
- **Exit / next:** S-01 (students) or `/admin` (R-1 then A-01); S-06 through "Create one"; Google through `{VITE_BACKEND_URL}/auth?frontend={origin}` (`GoogleLoginButton.tsx:29`), returning to S-07.
- **Stage 1 hooks:** the tagline and the aside (SR-5), the wordmark (the one place Montserrat stays), a mascot at sign-in (G9), stored Display preferences honoured (there is no top bar here).
- **Companion (Phase 4):** the idle still in `companion.slot.signin` 96 px from `md` and `companion.slot.inline` 64 px below it, above the `h1` display line at the form column's left edge (24 px right of the ruler, 12 at 390), 24 px above the line; on the `--companion-backing` disc in dark mode; the aside gets no mascot (SR-5). Still only: nothing animates on sign-in. The stored Kiwi animation choice is read here for the chat panel's sake, not for this slot.
- **Tour defects (in boundary):** TD-C1 (heuristic 4, sev 1); TD-19 (through SR-5: the white wordmark on the light gradient panel, 1.8 to 2.0:1 computed and not measured live; WCAG 2.2 SC 1.4.3; heuristic 4, **sev 3, release-blocking against done-when 2**) and TD-20 (the aphoristic tagline; heuristic 2, sev 1).
- **Out of boundary, recorded:** B2 (the "Your session has expired." banner also appears after a permission failure).
- **Source-found:** SF-01 (the missing campus image and the contrast of the white wordmark); SF-06 (environment-variable names in a user-facing alert).
- **Not observed live:** submitting, error, the Google flow and the configuration variants; the 390 px layout.

The user lands on `/`, then `ProtectedRoute` returns signed-out users to S-05.

- **Not observed live:** the whole page.

### S-08 · MCP OAuth authorize — `/authorize`

- **Source:** `src/pages/MCPAuthorize.tsx:14` (route `src/App.tsx:84-87`); `AuthLayout` (centred variant), `FormCard` (glass), `LogoBox` (`src/components/LogoBox/LogoBox.tsx`), `GoogleLoginButton`.
- **Roles (Appendix A):** "per route guard". **Guard (code):** **none** (D-3): the route has no wrapper, so signed-out and signed-in visitors both reach it, and the consent redirect never applies. **Entry visibility:** valid only with `?oauth_pending=true`.
- **Density:** comfortable · **Paper surfaces:** none. **Never recoloured:** the logo image (`LOGO_URL`, "shown as-is") and the campus photo behind the page (`AuthLayout.tsx:126`; the file is missing, SF-01). · **Density overrides:** none.
- **Purpose:** Kiwi, acting as the OAuth authorization server for an external MCP client, lets the visitor sign in (if needed) and approve the pending request, then sends the browser to the client's redirect URL.
- **Entry points:** an external MCP client opens the backend `GET /auth/authorize`, which validates the request and redirects to `{FRONTEND_URL}/authorize?oauth_pending=true` (`../kiwi/src/auth/auth.controller.ts:260-324`). Nothing inside the app links here (grep).
- **Content blocks (in order):** the decision order is invalid flag (`MCPAuthorize.tsx:100`), then signed in (`:120`), then signed out (`:175`).
  1. Shell: `AuthLayout` centred (a full-viewport campus photo under a gradient from `primary.dark` at about 72% alpha) around a glass `FormCard` (elevation 12, `rgba(255,255,255,0.2)` fill, blur, white text); no top bar or sidebar. The chat launcher appears only in the signed-in view — `AuthLayout.tsx:118-136`; `FormCard.tsx:9-37`.
  2. View A, flag missing or not exactly "true", for everyone: the logo; `h6` in the error colour "Invalid Authorization Request"; "This page is only accessible during an OAuth authorization flow."; no button or link — `:100-117`.
  3. View B, signed in: the logo; `h6` "Authorize Application"; "An application is requesting access to your account."; "Signed in as: {user.username}" (the account email); an error banner that can never carry a message; a contained full-width "Authorize" (a spinner replaces the label); an outlined full-width "Cancel" — `:119-173`.
  4. View C, signed out: an info banner "Sign in to authorize the application to access your account."; an error banner; the logo (alt "{INSTITUTION_NAME} logo", 140 px); with password login on, a form with "Your Email", "Password" and a contained full-width "Login & Authorize"; with Google on, a contained pill "Login with Google"; with both off, only the banner and logo and no message — `:175-261`.
  5. Overlays: none.
- **States:**
  - `m:view:invalid` `m:view:signed-in@S,I,A` `m:view:signed-out@out` Default `[S]`: View B or C per sign-in state; View A without the flag.
  - `m:submitting` Loading `[S]`: no page-level loading; only an in-button 24 px spinner replaces the label; controls are disabled while authorising with no reason text (`:155,165,223,234,243`).
  - `m:none` Empty: none.
  - `m:view:invalid` Error `[S]`: invalid flag → View A. For a signed-in "Authorize" failure every error is caught once, logged to the console, and the user is sent to `/` (`:55-61`); the message "Authorization failed" is never shown. For signed-out login the server's reply arrives as the browser's own response to a native form POST, for example the API text "Invalid email or password" (backend `../kiwi/src/auth/auth.controller.ts:157,164`; inferred).
  - `m:to:S-01` Success `[S]`: **no success screen.** Signed-in "Authorize" posts with a bearer token; the code comment says the cross-origin redirect is blocked and treated as success, then `navigate('/')` (`:35-62`; unverified at runtime). Signed-out "Login & Authorize" submits a hidden form to `{basePath}/auth/oauth-login`, the browser leaves the SPA and the backend redirects to the client; neither path stores `authToken` or calls `login()` (`:65-98`).
  - Page-specific `[S]`: only `oauth_pending` is read; the page **never names the requesting application or its scopes** (`:26-27,129-131`); "Cancel" navigates to `/` and calls no API, so the pending request is not declined; empty fields can be submitted (no `required`, no `type="email"`); if both login methods are on, two filled buttons show together.
- **Primary CTA:** View B "Authorize" → POST `{basePath}/auth/oauth-authorize` (`:150-159`); View C "Login & Authorize" → native form POST (`:237-246`) and "Login with Google" when enabled; View A none.
- **Exit / next:** View A none (browser back); View B success or any caught error → the client's `redirectUrl` or `/` (S-01), "Cancel" → `/`; View C leaves to the backend or Google and then the client's callback.
- **Stage 1 hooks:** the mascot slot at sign-in and the error state; the glass `FormCard` (blur, translucent fill) conflicts with the brief's "no glass effects" (SF-63); the missing campus photo.
- **Companion (Phase 4):** the centred card's heading is preceded by `companion.slot.inline` 64 px (all widths): the idle still in views B and C, the error still in view A ("Invalid Authorization Request"); on the `--companion-backing` disc in dark mode. Still only.
- **Tour defects (in boundary):** TD-C1 (heuristic 4, sev 1); TD-05, TD-09, TD-10, TD-11 (heuristic 4, sev 1), TD-12 (heuristic 2, sev 1) and TD-13 (heuristic 1, sev 2), all via SR-6.
- **Source-found:** SF-41 (a consent screen that does not name the app or scopes, offers no real deny, and reports no outcome; against Nielsen 1 and 3; outside Stage 1 as a flow); SF-01 (the missing `bg-campus.jpg`); SF-42 (a glass `FormCard` with white text on a photo background, which depends on the missing image for contrast); SF-63 (the glass `FormCard` against the brief's "no glass effects", apart from its contrast dependency).
- **Not observed live:** the whole page.

### S-09 · Consent — `/consent`

- **Source:** `src/pages/Consent.tsx:16` (route `src/App.tsx:104-110`); `src/components/PDFViewer/PDFViewer.tsx`.
- **Roles (Appendix A):** "per route guard". **Guard (code):** `ProtectedRoute`: any signed-in user. **The page only exists when `VITE_CONSENT_FORM_URL` is set:** without it the component returns `<Navigate to="/" replace />` (`Consent.tsx:45-47`; R-4). The consent gate that sends users here is `ProtectedRoute`, `UnprotectedRoute` and `AdminProtectedRoute` while `user.consent` is false (`src/auth/ProtectedRoute.tsx:55-58`).
- **Density:** comfortable · **Paper surfaces:** the consent PDF, rendered continuously by `PDFViewer pdfFile={formUrl} continuous` (`Consent.tsx:56`). **Never recoloured:** the PDF pages. · **Density overrides:** the PDF toolbar (tool chrome, compact).
- **Purpose:** Shows the consent document and records the user's agreement before they can use Kiwi.
- **Entry points:** the consent redirect from any wrapper (`ProtectedRoute.tsx:55-58`; `UnprotectedRoute.tsx:15-18`; `AdminProtectedRoute.tsx:19-22`); a typed URL.
- **Content blocks (in order):** the page uses raw HTML elements and no layout, top bar or card:
  1. `<h1>Consent Form</h1>` — `Consent.tsx:52`.
  2. `<p>Please read the document below and accept to continue.</p>` — `:53`.
  3. The PDF viewer (toolbar "Search", "Thumbnails", "First", "Previous", "Page", "Next", "Last", "Zoom out", "Zoom in", "Fit width", "Open / print", "Fullscreen"; `PDFViewer.tsx:106-203`).
  4. Only when `user.consent === false`: a form with a date field "Today's date" (default today), a checkbox "I agree to participate" and a contained "Submit", disabled until the date is non-empty and the box is ticked, with no reason shown — `:58-98`.
  5. SR-6 launcher (the user is signed in).
- **States:**
  - `m:loaded` Default `[S]`: as above.
  - `m:loading` Loading `[S]`: the viewer's own "Loading PDF…".
  - `m:none` Empty: none.
  - `m:error` Error `[S]`: the viewer's red Alert "Couldn't load this PDF." with "Retry" (`PDFViewer.tsx:76-89`); a failed submit is `console.error` only and the user sees nothing (`Consent.tsx:39-41`).
  - `m:to:S-01` Success `[S]`: the call succeeds, the user is updated with `consent: true` (the system role is dropped and re-fetched) and sent to `/` (`:34-38`); no confirmation text.
  - `m:consented` Page-specific `[S]`: with `consent` already true the form is not rendered and the page only shows the document.
- **Primary CTA:** "Submit" (contained) → records consent and goes to S-01 (`:87-96`).
- **Exit / next:** S-01 on success; nothing else (no way out for a user who declines).
- **Stage 1 hooks:** the unstyled heading and paragraph take the type scale; the paper PDF; the form controls.
- **Tour defects (in boundary):** TD-C1 (the contained "Submit"; heuristic 4, sev 1); TD-05, TD-09, TD-10, TD-11 (heuristic 4, sev 1), TD-12 (heuristic 2, sev 1) and TD-13 (heuristic 1, sev 2), all via SR-6.
- **Source-found:** SF-05 (the page is unstyled raw HTML with no layout; a failed submit is silent; declining has no path).
- **Not observed live:** the whole page (the tour deployment had no consent form configured).

### S-10 · Not found — `*`

- **Source:** `src/App.tsx:194-198`: `{ path: "*", element: <div>Not found</div> }`, inside `RootLayout`.
- **Roles (Appendix A):** all. **Guard (code):** none, so signed-out visitors see it too.
- **Density:** comfortable · **Paper surfaces:** none.
- **Purpose:** Catches any URL no other route matches.
- **Entry points:** any path with two or more segments that no route matches, for example `/admin/typo` or `/a/b/c`; **the student OAuth return**, because `ProtectedRoute` navigates to `/class/{classId}` and `/class/{classId}/pdf/{pdfId}`, which are not routes (D-6, SF-03; `src/auth/ProtectedRoute.tsx:31-35`). A **one-segment** unknown path such as `/typo` does *not* land here: it matches `/:classID`, so S-02 renders under SR-2 and the layout shows a spinner that never clears (SF-04).
- **Content blocks (in order):** one `<div>` holding the text "Not found". No heading, no layout, no top bar, no link back, no document title of its own. When signed in, SR-6's launcher is also on the page.
- **States:** `m:loaded` a single state, `[S]`. No loading, empty, error or success.
- **Primary CTA:** none.
- **Exit / next:** none on the page; the browser's back button, or the launcher.
- **Stage 1 hooks:** the brief allows restyling the **content** of this existing catch-all route and a mascot illustration here (G9); no region, route or navigation may be added.
- **Companion (Phase 4):** the walk still (the standing bird) in `companion.slot.not-found` 96 px from `md` and `inline` 64 below it, on the ruled column above the `--text-2xl` line and the way back (Phase 5 copy), on the `--companion-backing` disc in dark mode. Still only.
- **Tour defects (in boundary):** TD-C1 (none specific; not toured; heuristic 4, sev 1); TD-05, TD-09, TD-10, TD-11 (heuristic 4, sev 1), TD-12 (heuristic 2, sev 1) and TD-13 (heuristic 1, sev 2), all via SR-6.
- **Source-found:** SF-10 (a bare `<div>` with no recovery path); SF-03 and SF-04 (how users actually arrive, and the one-segment trap).
- **Not observed live:** the page.

Until the role request resolves everyone sees "All Classes" and the button (`:36-39`).

  5. Loading placeholders: six wave skeletons, 252 px high — `:222-239`.
  6. Empty state (`className="admin-empty-state"`, which no stylesheet defines): a 120 px outlined school icon, `h6` "No classes found" or "No classes assigned", body "Get started by creating your first class" / "Try adjusting your search or creating a new class" or, for instructors, "You have not been assigned to any classes yet" / "Try adjusting your search terms", and a contained "Create First Class" only without a search term and for non-instructors — `:261-314`.

Please try again." in block 3; the grid and skeletons hide and the search field is disabled with no reason; "Retry" is a **full page reload** (`:103,206,213-220`). A role-lookup failure is console-only and the page takes the all-classes path; a failed single-class lookup silently drops that class (`:50-53,62-64`).

### A-03 · System analytics — `/admin/analytics`

- **Source:** `src/pages/admin/Analytics.tsx:229` (route `src/App.tsx:139`); `src/components/Analytics/AnalyticsDateRangeControl.tsx`, `StandaloneUserActivity.tsx`, `StandaloneDailySessions.tsx`, `StandaloneTopActions.tsx`, `StandaloneUserDuration.tsx`, `StandaloneUserEngine.tsx`, `StandaloneTopTokenUsers.tsx`, `chartXAxis.ts`, `chartAnimation.ts`; `src/components/DateTimeRangePicker/DateTimeRangePicker.tsx`; chart colours `src/Theme.ts:65-71`; frame SR-4 (`AdminPage`, `AdminPageHeader`: `Analytics.tsx:363-382`).
- **Roles (Appendix A):** A (admin-only). **Instructors (decision of 2026-10-16):** no access; an instructor who reaches the page should see a no-permission state, not be signed out and not be sent back with an `alert()`. Today D-8 (a record-only defect row in the matrix). **Guard (code):** `ProtectedRoute` only (B1, D-5), plus an in-page check (D-7): after the first render `authControllerGetUserRole` runs and any role other than admin is sent to `/admin/classes`, while the data request starts regardless (`Analytics.tsx:241-252,320-324`); a failed role request goes to `/login`. Appendix A makes the page admin-only, so the turn-away is intended and its form is the defect: an instructor should see a no-permission state, not an `alert()` and a bounce (D-7, D-8). **Entry visibility:** sidebar "System Analytics" is admin-only (`AdminLayout.tsx:62-67`); the chat agent's sitemap lists admin and instructor (`src/agent/sitemap.ts:65-70`), so the agent can offer an instructor a page that is admin-only (D-8).
- **Density:** compact · **Paper surfaces:** none · **Never recoloured:** none; the series colours are data encodings. · **Density overrides:** none (numbers and charts; tool chrome throughout).
- **Purpose:** An admin dashboard of system-wide usage (users, sessions, interactions, product-usage mix) for a chosen date range, plus a list of the top token users with a per-session drill-down.
- **Entry points:** sidebar "System Analytics"; the chat agent (`analytics`); a non-admin is redirected away from it (not toward it). No other link.
- **Content blocks (in order):**
  1. Page header: `h1` "System Analytics"; description "Usage and engagement across Kiwi."; action: the date-range control — a calendar icon button (aria-label "Choose analytics date range"), a period button reading the preset label (default "Last 7 Days" after a derived match) or "{MMM d} – {MMM d}", and an outlined refresh icon button (aria-label "Refresh analytics", tooltip "Updated {time}") — `Analytics.tsx:365-381`; `AnalyticsDateRangeControl.tsx:94-200`.
  2. Heading "Overview" and four tiles (outlined, 132 px, label, `h4` value, caption, a 40 px icon in `text.secondary`): "Active users" ("Unique active users"), "Sessions" ("Total user sessions"), "Interactions" ("Total recorded user actions"), "Average session" ("Mean active time per session", shown as `{n} min`) — `:384-424,55-121`.
  3. Heading "Activity over time" and two area charts, "Hourly Active Users" / "Daily Active Users" (subtitle "Unique users active each hour" / "…each day") and "Hourly Sessions" / "Daily Sessions" (subtitle "Sessions started each hour" / "…each day"): `type="monotone"` curves, 2.5 px stroke, a vertical gradient fill from 0.3 to 0.05 opacity of the series colour, dashed horizontal grid lines only, an X axis of at most six custom ticks (first and last unlabelled), **a Y axis with no formatter or domain** (so counts of people can show fractional ticks), tooltips "Time: …" / "Date: …" over "Active users: {n}" / "Sessions: {n}" — `:426-450`; `StandaloneUserActivity.tsx:115-179`; `chartXAxis.ts:1-86`.
  4. Heading "Product usage" and three cards: "Top Actions" (subtitle "Interactions grouped by action type"; a horizontal bar chart of six categories with abbreviated axis names "Chat", "PDF", "Run code", "Export", "Other code", "Other" and longer tooltip names), "User Duration" (subtitle "Estimated active time by activity"; a donut, centre "{hours}" over "hours", legend rows with minutes and percent; minutes are estimated as chat actions x 2 plus code actions x 5), "User Engine Choice" (subtitle "Model usage distribution"; a donut by engine; legend still lists "OpenAI", "QWEN" and "Unknown" with "0 requests" when empty) — `:452-474`; `StandaloneTopActions.tsx`; `StandaloneUserDuration.tsx:49-52`; `StandaloneUserEngine.tsx:139-237`.
  5. Heading "Top token users" and a full-width card: a list with rank, email or "(no email)", user id, "{n} sessions" and "{n} tokens", each row a button; it is **not driven by the date range** (`:476-483`; `StandaloneTopTokenUsers.tsx:103-119`).
  6. Overlays: the period menu ("Last 24 Hours", "Last 7 Days", "Last 30 Days", "Last 3 Months", "Last 6 Months", "Last 12 Months"); the calendar popover `m:ov:range-popover` (month title, "Previous month" / "Next month", "Start", "Start time", "End", "End time", an outlined "Apply" disabled while the range is invalid with no reason, the caption "Local ({timeZone})"); the dialog "Token Usage Detail" `m:ov:token-dialog` (a sortable table "Session ID", "Start", "End", "Usage", "Tokens", full screen with a "Sort by" select below `sm`, "No session details.") — `AnalyticsDateRangeControl.tsx:202-239`; `DateTimeRangePicker.tsx:272-530`; `StandaloneTopTokenUsers.tsx:330-507`.
- **Series colours (literal, `src/Theme.ts:65-71`):** `series1` `#6A1B9A` (Active users, Chat Action, Chat, OpenAI), `series2` `#CF007E` (Sessions, PDF Action, IDE, QWEN), `series3` `#FF4E4C` (Run Code), `series4` `#FFA600` (Export), `series5` = `grey.500` `#9e9e9e` (both "Other" categories and "Unknown"). Chart animation 1,000 ms, off under reduced motion (`chartAnimation.ts:3-12`).

### A-07 · MCP server registry — `/admin/mcp-servers`

- **Source:** `src/pages/admin/MCPServerManagement.tsx:41` (route `src/App.tsx:152-155`, wrapped in `AdminProtectedRoute`); dialogs `src/components/MCP/MCPServerDialog.tsx`, `MCPTestResultDialog.tsx`, `DeleteConfirmDialog.tsx`; frame SR-4 (`AdminPage`, `AdminPageHeader`: `MCPServerManagement.tsx:39,280-303`).
- **Roles (Appendix A):** A (admin-only). **Instructors (decision of 2026-10-16):** no access; an instructor who reaches the page should see a no-permission state, not be signed out and not be sent back with an `alert()`. Today D-8 (a record-only defect row in the matrix). **Guard (code):** `AdminProtectedRoute`: no user → `/login`; consent missing → `/consent`; admin status unknown → a centred spinner; not admin and not instructor → `/`. It therefore **admits instructors** (D-1; `src/auth/AdminProtectedRoute.tsx:15-42`). **Entry visibility:** sidebar "MCP Server Registry" is admin-only (`src/pages/admin/AdminLayout.tsx:86-91`); the chat-agent sitemap says admin (`src/agent/sitemap.ts:89-94`).
- **Density:** compact · **Paper surfaces:** none · **Density overrides:** none (the nearest reading surfaces are the tool-schema JSON and the error text in the test dialog; the rest is tool chrome).
- **Purpose:** An admin lists, creates, edits, deletes and connection-tests the global MCP servers; assigning a server to classes happens on A-22.
- **Entry points:** sidebar "MCP Server Registry"; **the return from an OAuth round trip** (below); the chat agent (`mcp-servers`); a typed URL. No other page links here.
- **OAuth round trip, as coded:** "Test Connection" on a server that needs OAuth and has no active user connection opens "Authorization Required" (`MCPServerManagement.tsx:139-153`); "Authorize" stores `sessionStorage["mcp_oauth_return"]` = `{"returnTo":"/admin/mcp-servers","action":"test","serverId":…}` and sets `window.location.href` to the provider (`:187-202`); the backend callback redirects the browser to `{FRONTEND_URL}/?mcp_oauth_success=true` or `…/?mcp_oauth_error=<message>` (`../kiwi/src/mcp/mcp-user.controller.ts:109-153`); route `/` mounts `ProtectedRoute`, whose mount effect reads and deletes the key and navigates to `returnTo`, rendering nothing meanwhile (`src/auth/ProtectedRoute.tsx:17-28,47-49`). `action` and `serverId` are never read, so the test does not re-run, and `mcp_oauth_success` / `mcp_oauth_error` are read nowhere in `src/`, so **no success or error message for the round trip is ever shown**.
- **Content blocks (in order):**
  1. Page header: `h1` "MCP Server Management"; description "Manage global MCP servers and assign them to classes from class settings."; actions: outlined "Refresh" (disabled while loading, no reason) and contained "Create Server" — `MCPServerManagement.tsx:280-300`.
  2. ### A-08 · App registry — `/admin/kiwi-apps`

- **Source:** `src/pages/admin/KiwiAppsAdmin.tsx:95` (route `src/App.tsx:156-159`, wrapped in `AdminProtectedRoute`); frame SR-4 (`AdminPage`, `AdminPageHeader` with title and description only: `KiwiAppsAdmin.tsx:568-572`); all other UI is inline MUI.
- **Roles (Appendix A):** A (admin-only). **Instructors (decision of 2026-10-16):** no access; an instructor who reaches the page should see a no-permission state, not be signed out and not be sent back with an `alert()`. Today D-8 (a record-only defect row in the matrix). **Guard (code):** `AdminProtectedRoute`, which admits instructors too (D-1; `src/auth/AdminProtectedRoute.tsx:40`). **Entry visibility:** sidebar "App Registry" is admin-only (`src/pages/admin/AdminLayout.tsx:92-97`); the chat-agent sitemap says admin.
- **Density:** compact · **Paper surfaces:** none · **Density overrides:** none (tables, chips, dialogs; the long strings are the UI URL and the system prompt, each cut to one line with the full text in a tooltip).
- **Purpose:** Lets a system admin whitelist app slugs (issuing a one-time registration secret) and review, inspect and unregister the Kiwi Apps that have self-registered, including approving or revoking each app's LLM prompts.
- **Entry points:** sidebar "App Registry"; the chat agent (`kiwi-apps`); a typed URL. No other page links here.
- **Content blocks (in order):**
  1. Page header: `h1` "Kiwi Apps Management"; description "Manage the app whitelist and registered Kiwi Apps. Apps must be whitelisted before they can self-register." — `KiwiAppsAdmin.tsx:569-572`.

  3. Whitelist toolbar: outlined "Refresh", contained "Add to Whitelist" — `:583-601`. Table columns "Slug", "Base URL", "Description", "Status", "Created", "Actions"; the slug in monospace; a status chip "Approved" (success, check icon), "Pending" (warning), "Rejected" (error) or the raw value; **Created is `new Date(date).toLocaleString()` with no locale passed**, or "Never"; row actions: icon buttons with tooltips "Edit" and a red "Remove" — `:656-723,526-565`.
  4. Registered Apps `m:loaded:registered` toolbar: only an outlined "Refresh". Table columns (expander), "Name", "Slug", "Category" (a chip with the raw category), "Version", "Status" ("Active" / "Inactive"), "Last Seen", "Actions" (a red trash with tooltip "Unregister") — `:735-878`. An expandable row holds three sections: "Registered UIs" (a nested table "UI Slug", "Name", "URL", "Placement", "Allowed Roles", "Order", "Status" "Enabled" / "Disabled"; "Loading UIs...", "No UIs registered for this app."), "App Scopes" ("Maximum Allowed Scopes (from whitelist)", "Prompt Scopes (from registered prompts)", "Max scopes define what this app can request. Enable/disable specific scopes per class in class settings." with no link) and "LLM Prompts" (a table with "Name", "Prompt ID", "System Prompt", "Settings", "Status" "Approved" / "Revoked" / "Pending", and a text "Revoke" or a contained green "Approve"; "Approve prompts to allow the app to use them for LLM requests. Review the system prompt before approving.") — `:881-1252`.
  5. Overlay `m:ov:add-dialog`, "Add to Whitelist" (sm): "Enter the app details. A registration secret will be generated that the app must use to self-register."; fields "App Slug" (placeholder "e.g., codegen", helper "Lowercase identifier for the app"), "Registration Secret" (masked, prefilled with a generated 48-character secret, helper "Minimum 32 characters. Share this with the app developer.", tooltips "Show secret" / "Hide secret" / "Generate new secret"), "Base URL (optional)", "Description (optional)", select "Status" ("Approved", "Pending", "Rejected"); "Maximum Allowed Scopes" with its caption and an "Add" button; actions "Cancel", contained "Add" — `:1263-1408`.

(`:170,221,245,281,386,416,464`); info snackbars for copies ("Webhook URL copied to clipboard", "Secret copied to clipboard").

(`:942,1054`). *Busy state* — only the Test dialog and the two token buttons; create, update and delete have none. *Partial failure* — regenerate deletes the old token and then creates the new one, so a failed second call leaves no token (`:439-452`). *One-time values* — the secret returned at creation exists only in React state and no dialog shows it; the reveal then reads "Only shown on creation" after a reload (`:164-166,1264`); a new token stays visible in the row Alert after the dialog is dismissed by backdrop or Escape (`:834,954,923-933`). *Test failure* — the dialog still shows the info Alert "Test event sent. 0 webhooks triggered." under the error snackbar (`:298-301,798-803`). Filled buttons inside dialogs only: "Create", "Update", "Delete", "Send Test Event", "Regenerate Token", "Create Token".

- **Source:** `src/pages/admin/WebhookEventApprovals.tsx:95` (route `src/App.tsx:151`). Frame from SR-4 (`AdminPage`, `AdminPageHeader`, `AdminFormSurface`: `WebhookEventApprovals.tsx:545-546,591,633,667`). Tables, dialogs and chips are inline MUI; no colour literals.
- **Roles (Appendix A):** A (admin-only). **Instructors (decision of 2026-10-16):** no access; an instructor who reaches the page should see a no-permission state, not be signed out and not be sent back with an `alert()`. Today D-8 (a record-only defect row in the matrix). **Guard (code):** `ProtectedRoute` only (`src/App.tsx:129-135,151`), so any signed-in user reaches it (B1, D-5). **Entry visibility:** sidebar "Webhook Approvals" is admin-only (`src/pages/admin/AdminLayout.tsx:104-109`); it is not in the chat-agent sitemap.

- **Purpose:** An admin approves or rejects pending webhook events, one by one or in bulk, and reviews a 30-day audit trail of those decisions.
- **Entry points:** sidebar "Webhook Approvals" (`AdminLayout.tsx:104-109`); button "Event Approvals" with a pending-count badge on A-09 (`WebhookManagement.tsx:573-582`); a typed URL. No email or redirect.
- **Content blocks (in order):**

Row: checkbox; event-type chip with icon and colour from a nine-entry map (several types share icon and colour, so the label is the only unique cue, `:70-93`); first 8 characters of the id plus "..." in monospace; payload cut at 100 characters plus an eye icon button with no tooltip; date via `toLocaleString()` (`:779-781`); icon buttons with tooltips "Approve" and "Reject" (`:782-805`); client-side `TablePagination` (`:812-819`).

Row: action chip (success + check icon for "approved", error + cancel icon for **any other value**, `:864-871`); id prefix; performer email with the user id as tooltip; `toLocaleString()` date; reason or "-"; eye icon button with no tooltip; server-side pagination (`:908-915`).

This will execute the associated actions for each event."; single text "Are you sure you want to approve this webhook event?" plus, for `user.enroll` only, an info Alert "This will execute the user enrollment action associated with this event."; actions "Cancel", "Approve" ("Approving..." while busy) — `:1139-1189`.

  11. Overlay `m:success` `m:error`, snackbar: bottom right, 6,000 ms — `:1239-1253`.
- **States:**
  - `m:loaded:pending` Default (loaded) `[S]`: header, cards, note, tabs, Pending table at 10 rows per page (`:104,544-920`).
  - `m:loading` Loading `[S]`: a centred spinner on the Pending tab (`:676-679`) and on the Audit tab (`:826-829`); silent auto-refresh shows none; no skeletons.
  - `m:empty` Empty `[S]`: Pending shows a 64 px outlined check-circle icon in `success.main`, `h6` "No pending webhook events" and "All webhook events have been processed", with no button; Audit shows an outlined history icon, "No audit history available" and "Approved and rejected events will appear here" (`:680-696,830-846`). An icon, not an illustration.
  - `m:error` Error `[S]`: a manual Pending fetch failure shows the snackbar "Failed to fetch pending webhook events" and, on a first load, the green "No pending webhook events" block beneath it (`:157-163`); silent auto-refresh and Audit failures are console-only (`:157-158,193-197`); approve and reject failures show a snackbar with `error?.response?.data?.message` or "Failed to approve event" / "Failed to reject event" and close the dialog (`:315-325,353-363`).
  - `m:success` Success `[S]`: snackbars "Event approved and executed successfully", "Event rejected successfully", "Bulk approval completed: {successCount} approved, {failCount} failed", "Bulk rejection completed: {successCount} rejected, {failCount} failed" (success, or warning when any failed) (`:297,335,382-386,414-418`).
  - Page-specific `[S]`: *zero selected* — the bulk bar is not rendered and the "Selected" card reads 0 (`:632,589`). *Selection scope* — the selection is not reset on page or tab change, select-all picks only the current page, and the bar count uses the whole set (`:430-433,482-488,636`). *Disabled without a stated reason* — row, bulk and dialog buttons while processing (`:789,799,643,652,1170,1183`). *Auto-refresh* — default off; when on, silently refetches Pending every 5 s, and nothing on screen states the interval (`:121,141-149`). *Audit window* — fixed to the last 30 days and not stated on screen; the "Audit history" card reads 0 until the Audit tab has been opened (`:128-139,173-182`). *Bulk partial failure* — per-event failures are console-only; only the warning snackbar count shows (`:377,409`). *Reason* — the reject call sends no reason, so "Reason" shows "-" for decisions made here (`:331,892`). Icon-only controls without tooltip or label: checkboxes, eye buttons (`:704-714,735-738,771-776,896-901`).

"Approve Selected" (contained, success) appears once a row is selected (`:638-646`); the dialogs' "Approve" and "Reject" are filled (`:1173-1187,1221-1235`). "Reject Selected" and "Clear Selection" are outlined.
- **Exit / next:** none in the page (`Breadcrumbs`, `Link` and `navigate` are imported or declared and never rendered or called, `:28-29,96`). The user stays after approve or reject and leaves by the sidebar.

### A-11 · User management — `/admin/user-management`

- **Source:** `src/pages/admin/UserManagement.tsx:101` (route `src/App.tsx:162`); all other JSX is inline in the 1,889-line file. SR-4 helpers: `AdminPage maxWidth="xl"` (`UserManagement.tsx:933`), `AdminPageHeader` (`:934-959`), `AdminFormSurface` (`:1341`).
- **Roles (Appendix A):** A (admin-only). **Instructors (decision of 2026-10-16):** no access; an instructor who reaches the page should see a no-permission state, not be signed out and not be sent back with an `alert()`. Today D-8 (a record-only defect row in the matrix). **Guard (code):** `ProtectedRoute` only (B1, D-5). **Entry visibility:** sidebar "User Management" is admin-only (`src/pages/admin/AdminLayout.tsx:56-61`); the chat-agent sitemap lists it for admins (`src/agent/sitemap.ts:101-106`).
- **Density:** compact · **Paper surfaces:** none · **Density overrides:** none (tool chrome: toolbar, filter pills, table, pagination, results table; the only prose is one-sentence helper text).
- **Purpose:** One admin workspace to see every account (signed up or only whitelisted), switch its access on or off, change its system role, reset a password-account's password, bulk-add emails or password accounts, and assign instructors to classes.
- **Entry points:** sidebar "User Management"; the chat agent's navigation destination `user-management`; a typed URL. No other page links here.
- **Content blocks (in order):**
  1. Page header: `h1` "User management"; description "Control account access, system roles, registrations, and instructor assignments from one workspace."; actions: outlined "Assign instructor" and contained "Add users" — `UserManagement.tsx:934-959`.
  2. Toolbar: a static label "Table" with a table icon (aria-label "Current view: Table"; it is not a control and no second view exists); search field, placeholder "Search" (aria-label "Search accounts", matches email only); outlined "Export CSV", disabled when no rows match, no reason shown, which downloads `whitelist_{YYYY-MM-DD}.csv` with header "Email,Status,Operation Status,Error" — `:1363-1410,707-724`.
  3. Filter row (aria-label "Account filters"): three pill-shaped selects with leading icons — Access ("All access", "Enabled", "Disabled"), Role ("All roles", "User", "Instructor", "Admin"), Account status ("All statuses", "Registered", "Pending signup") — and a text button "Clear filters" that appears only while a search or filter is active — `:1426-1501`.
  4. List region: the loading branch, the empty branch, or the table (from `lg`, 1200 px) / the cards (below `lg`) — `:1503-1526`, `:1529`, `:1754`.
  5. Table (aria-label "User accounts", fixed layout): columns "Account" (sortable, 32%), "Access" (sortable), "Role", "Account status", "Account action". Row: bold email with caption "OAuth" or "Password"; a small green switch (aria-label "Enable access for {email}" / "Disable access for {email}") with the text "Enabled" / "Disabled"; a borderless role select ("User", "Admin", "Instructor") tinted warning for admin and info for instructor; a bordered status box with a 7 px dot and "Pending" / "Registered"; the action cell reading italic "Awaiting Registration", an outlined "Reset password" (password accounts) or italic "Managed by OAuth" — `:1545-1747`. Under the table sort headers; there is no sort control in the card layout, although the sort state applies to both.
  6. Cards (below `lg`): bold email; an outlined chip "OAuth" / "Password" and a filled chip "Pending" (warning) / "Registered" (success); the switch with the caption "Enabled" / "Disabled"; a labelled "Role" select; the action as caption "Awaiting registration" or "Password managed by OAuth" or the outlined "Reset password" — `:1755-1857`.
  7. Pagination: MUI `TablePagination` (5, 10, 25, 50; default 10; MUI default strings), hidden when there are no rows — `:1859-1875`.
  8. Overlay `m:ov:add-dialog`, Dialog "Add users" (md): subtitle "Choose whether to grant access or create password-based accounts."; tabs "Whitelist access" · "Password accounts". Whitelist tab: `h3` "Grant product access", text "Existing accounts are enabled immediately. New email addresses remain pending until first sign-in.", field "Email addresses" (placeholder "student@example.edu", helper "Enter one address per line. Existing whitelist entries are ignored."). Password tab: `h3` "Create password accounts", text "Use this only for non-SSO users. SSO users should sign in with OAuth.", select "Class" (first class preselected), field "Email addresses" (placeholder "person@example.com", helper "Enter one address per line. SSO-domain addresses will be skipped."), then a results block "Registration results ({n})" with the **generated passwords in plain monospace** beside "Email", "Class" (a class ID), "Status" (the raw words pending / success / error) and "Error"; actions "Export results", "Cancel" (reads "Close" once results exist), contained "Grant access" or "Create accounts" ("Adding users…" / "Registering users…") — `:962-1203`. **No confirmation step** before a bulk add.
  9. Overlay `m:ov:instructors-dialog`, Dialog "Instructor assignments" (md): subtitle "Assign an instructor to a class or update an existing assignment."; selects "Class" and "Instructor", a multi-select "Permissions" ("Manage Materials", "View Analytics"); an info Alert "{selectedInstructor} is already assigned to this class. Saving will update their permissions." when the pair exists; `h3` "Current assignments" with "Loading assignments…", "No instructors assigned to this class yet." or rows with an "Edit" text button; actions "Cancel" and contained, `color="secondary"`, "Assign instructor" (reads "Update permissions" when the pair exists) — `:1205-1339`. It stays open after success.
  10. Overlay `m:success` `m:error`, snackbar with a closable Alert, 5,000 ms, no `anchorOrigin` — `:1880-1884`. Select menus; a Tooltip with the full registration error (`:1123`); the native `alert()` on 403 and 404 (`src/utils/middleware.ts:105-110`).
- **States:**
  - `m:loaded:table` Default (loaded) `[T]` (card layout seen at about 960 px): sort Account ascending, 10 rows, all filters "all"; registered users absent from the whitelist show "Disabled"; whitelist-only emails appear as Pending rows (`UserManagement.tsx:266-281,393-411`).
  - `m:loading` Loading `[S]`: a centred spinner with "Loading accounts…" replaces the list; toolbar and filters stay; no skeleton (`:1503-1512`). Classes load separately ("Loading classes…").
  - `m:empty` Empty `[S]`: a group icon, "No accounts found", then "Try clearing or changing the current filters." or, with no filters, "Add an email to the whitelist to get started."; "Clear filters" appears twice when filters are active. The same block shows after a failed fetch and for no search results (`:1513-1526`).
  - `m:error` Error `[S]`: snackbar only, no inline Alert and no retry: "Failed to fetch users or whitelist", "Failed to load classes", "Failed to update status", "Failed to change role for {email}: {message}", "Failed to reset password for {email}: {message}", "Failed to assign instructor to class", "Failed to update instructor permissions" (`:429,134,480-507,563,594,869-880,807-818`). Toggles and role changes revert automatically. For role change and password reset the expression at `:553-556` and `:584-587` mixes `||` and `?`, so a response carrying a `message` probably prints the whole object (derived, not run).
  - `m:success` Success `[S]`: "Successfully enabled {email}" / "Successfully disabled {email}"; "Role changed to {newRole} for {email}" (raw lowercase role); "Password reset for {email}: {newPassword}", the only place the new password is shown; "Added {n} users to whitelist. {m} failed."; "Registration completed: {n} successful, {m} failed"; "Updated permissions for {email}"; "Successfully assigned {email} to class {classId}" (`:474,535,577,702-703,221-222,798,852`).
  - Page-specific `[S]`: *validation* (snackbar): "Please select a class", "Please enter at least one email address", "Cannot register SSO-domain emails: {emails}. SSO users should use OAuth login.", "Please select both a class and an instructor"; the whitelist tab shows red helper text "All emails are already in the whitelist"; **no email-format check** (`emailRegex` is declared and never used, `:292`). *Disabled controls* with no reason anywhere: the row switch while its request runs, every role select and "Reset password" while any change runs, the role select on Pending rows (always "User"), "Grant access" and "Create accounts" while blank (`:1644,1665,1729,1663-1665,1187,1196`). **No confirmations and no undo** for disabling access, changing a role (including to Admin) or "Reset password"; consequence wording appears only afterwards in the snackbar (`:446-600`). *Failed whitelist adds* keep an ordinary-looking row because the row error is never rendered (`:675,690,1616`); new optimistic rows read "Registered" until reload (`:634-646`). *Permission denied* has no in-page handling.
- **Primary CTA:** "Add users" → opens the "Add users" dialog on "Whitelist access" (`:910-913,948-956`). No other filled button in the page region ("Assign instructor", "Export CSV", "Reset password" are outlined). Filled inside dialogs: "Grant access", "Create accounts", "Assign instructor" / "Update permissions" (secondary colour).
- **Exit / next:** none; dialogs close in place; the page downloads `whitelist_{YYYY-MM-DD}.csv` and `user_registrations_{YYYY-MM-DD}.csv` (`:719,243`).
