### A-12 · Admin app view (host frame) — `/admin/app/:appSlug`, `/admin/app/:appSlug/class/:classId`, `/admin/class/:classID/app/:appSlug`

- **Source:** `src/pages/admin/AdminAppPage.tsx:28` (routes `src/App.tsx:160,161,183`, none wrapped in `AdminProtectedRoute`) rendering `AdminAppView` (`src/pages/admin/AdminAppView.tsx:42`); `src/apps/adminNavigation.ts`, `src/context/AdminNavigationContext.tsx`. No SR-4 helper is used: the frame is full-bleed. **Only the host frame is specified; the embedded app's content is out of scope.**
- **Roles (Appendix A):** I, A. **Guard (code):** `ProtectedRoute` only (B1, D-5). **Entry visibility:** an admin-panel UI appears in the sidebar only for roles in its `allowedRoles` (`AdminLayout.tsx:222-269,476-487`); an instructor on the classless route is redirected to their first assigned class's scoped route or `/admin/classes` (`AdminLayout.tsx:384-408`).
- **Density:** compact (the host chrome); the embedded app sets its own. · **Paper surfaces:** the Kiwi App iframe (`AdminAppView.tsx:563-580`): the embedded document stays light in dark mode (G5); the host frame and veil follow the mode. **Never recoloured:** everything inside the iframe. · **Density overrides:** none drawn by the host.
- **The three routes:** route 1 `/admin/app/:appSlug` is the global scope (no class, no enablement check, no `classId` in the iframe URL; a token is requested with an empty body, issued to system admins only); route 2 `/admin/app/:appSlug/class/:classId` is the class scope and the URL shape the host itself generates; route 3 `/admin/class/:classID/app/:appSlug` is a legacy shape that no sidebar item or switcher produces and that mounts the admin class wrapper. Routes 2 and 3 behave the same in page code. Changing the class re-runs the lookup (`AdminAppPage.tsx:32-36,42-51`; `AdminAppView.tsx:78-79,91-122,192-194`; `src/apps/adminNavigation.ts:54-60,80-85`).
- **Purpose:** Looks up the app's admin-panel UI by slug and hosts it in a full-height iframe, handing it the user and class context and an app token over `postMessage`.
- **Entry points:** the sidebar item named after the app (the UI name or the app name, with an apps icon); the top-bar class switcher on an app route (`AdminLayout.tsx:413-426`); the instructor redirect; the chat agent; bookmarks.
- **Content blocks (in order):**
  1. The shell, with the app's item selected and, if the app sends `kiwi:adminNavigation`, validated child items under it (at most 20, label up to 80 characters, internal paths only) (`AdminLayout.tsx:476-487`; `AdminAppView.tsx:353-376`; `adminNavigation.ts:87-152`).
  2. Lookup: a centred spinner with "Loading admin panel..." (`AdminAppPage.tsx:92-110`).
  3. Lookup error: a centred red Alert with the text or "Admin panel not found": "Missing app slug", `App "{appSlug}" does not have an admin panel`, "Failed to load app admin panel"; no retry (`:112-127,45,79,83`).
  4. "Not enabled" panel `m:not-enabled` (class routes only, when the class lacks the app): an apps-icon tile, the heading "Exercise Manager is not enabled for this class", the text "Enable the Codegen app in this class before opening its exercise management tools.", and for system admins a contained "Manage Class Apps" (to A-23) or for others an info Alert "Ask an administrator to enable Codegen for this class." — `AdminAppView.tsx:405-489`. The names are hard-coded although the host renders any app.
  5. Iframe-load error: a centred red Alert "Failed to load {appName}" with a refresh icon button (aria-label "Retry loading {appName}") — `AdminAppView.tsx:491-519`.
  6. Default frame: a borderless `Paper` (elevation 0, full size) holding a loading veil (`background.paper`, spinner and "Loading {appName}...") over the iframe. The iframe is mounted only once the user id, role context and class enablement are resolved, has `title={appName}`, `border: none`, `display: none` until `load`, no host-set background, no `allow` attribute, and the sandbox "allow-scripts allow-same-origin allow-forms allow-popups allow-popups-to-escape-sandbox allow-downloads"; its `src` is the UI URL plus `appId`, `userId`, `placement=admin-panel`, `classId` (class scope) and `uiSlug` — `AdminAppView.tsx:521-581,186-199`. **No toolbar, title bar, breadcrumb or status label surrounds it.**
  7. Bridge handshake (no visible status): on `kiwi:ready` the host sends `kiwi:context` and `kiwi:appToken`; `kiwi:requestToken` gets a new token; a token failure is sent to the app, not drawn (`AdminAppView.tsx:288-346`).
  8. No overlay owned by the page. The Kiwi Chat launcher (SR-6) overlays the frame.
- **States:**
  - `m:loaded` Default `[S]`: the iframe fills the main area under the top bar.
  - `m:loading` Loading `[S]`: two sequential indicators ("Loading admin panel...", then the veil "Loading {appName}..."); the veil also covers the time the iframe is unmounted and has no timeout if the user id never resolves (derived; `AdminAppView.tsx:126-138,562`).
  - `m:none` Empty: none; the nearest are the lookup errors and the "not enabled" panel.
  - `m:error:lookup` `m:error:iframe` Error `[S]`: blocks 3 and 5, fixed English strings (the real error is logged only); a failed class-enablement check does not block, the frame loads (`:108-114`).
  - `m:none` Success: none.
  - Page-specific `[S]`: a `class-admin-panel` UI is accepted when no `admin-panel` UI exists (`AdminAppPage.tsx:65-74`), while the host always sends `placement: 'admin-panel'` (`AdminAppView.tsx:191,333`).
- **Primary CTA:** none in the default frame (the host draws no buttons); in the "not enabled" panel "Manage Class Apps" → `/admin/class/{classId}/kiwi-apps` (`AdminAppView.tsx:469-478`).
- **Exit / next:** "Manage Class Apps" → A-23; app-supplied sidebar items navigate inside the iframe through `kiwi:adminNavigate`; otherwise the sidebar and class switcher.
- **Stage 1 hooks:** the veil and error Alerts (empty-state frame, loading); the paper boundary around the iframe; the sandbox is unchanged.
- **Tour defects (in boundary):** TD-C1 (host surfaces only; heuristic 4, sev 1); TD-07 (heuristic 4, sev 1, via SR-1); TD-05, TD-09, TD-10, TD-11 (heuristic 4, sev 1), TD-12 (heuristic 2, sev 1) and TD-13 (heuristic 1, sev 2), all via SR-6.
- **Out of boundary, recorded:** B1 (guard).
- **Source-found:** SF-51 (the admin and student hosts differ: the admin sandbox adds `allow-downloads`, the admin host checks `event.source` as well as the origin, and their message handlers differ, `AdminAppView.tsx:579,311` against `ClassAppView.tsx:668,403-404`); SF-66 (the host gets a class-scoped app token for every role that has a class role, whatever the UI's `allowedRoles`).
- **Not observed live:** the whole page (the tour reached only the student-side app tab).

- **Source:** `src/pages/admin/ClassEdit.tsx:37`, rendered with no props by both routes (`src/App.tsx:167-168`); `ClassCard`, `KiwiWordmark`; frame SR-4 (`AdminPage maxWidth="lg"`, `AdminPageHeader` with title and description only: `ClassEdit.tsx:217-221`) and `AdminFormSection`-style sections written inline. **The two routes do not differ:** the component reads only `classID`, and the sidebar marks "Edit Class" selected for both (`ClassLayout.tsx:172-174`).

  2. An error Alert, only when an error is set — `:223-227`.

Section "Class-specific instructions" (helper "Added to Kiwi's built-in instructions in every chat for this class."): a switch "Add class-specific instructions"; on → a 6-row field "Instructions" with the helper "Appended after Kiwi's built-in teaching and tool guidance. While an experiment is active, its variant's instructions are used instead."; off → "Kiwi uses only its built-in instructions for this class." — `:362-432`.
  7. Action row: outlined "Cancel"; contained "Save Changes" with a save icon ("Saving..."); right-aligned from `sm`, Save above Cancel below — `:435-462`.
  8. Aside "Class preview" ("This is how the class appears in the class list."): a `ClassCard` fed by the name, professor and current image, sticky from `lg`; with no image the grey gradient with the italic "Kiwi" wordmark; blank fields show "Unnamed class" and "Instructor TBD" — `:465-494`.
  9. Overlay `m:success`: a filled success snackbar "Class metadata updated successfully!", 3,000 ms, default bottom-left — `:498-511`. No dialog and no destructive confirmation.
- **States:**
  - `m:loaded` Default `[T]`: two columns with the section title and description on the left and fields on the right, labels above the fields, the purple instructions switch, and the "Class preview" card with the grey wordmark fallback. The tour called it "the most composed admin page — a good pattern to carry forward".
  - `m:loading` Loading `[S]`: the header stays, then a status row with a 24 px spinner and "Loading class data..." (`:195-214`).
  - `m:none` Empty `[S]`: none; missing values give blank fields and the preview fallbacks.
  - `m:error` Error `[S]`: the top Alert, client-authored text only: "Failed to load class data. Please try again." (the form still renders blank, no retry), "Failed to upload image. Please try again.", "Failed to update class metadata. Please try again."; server messages are console-only (`:102,123,173`).
  - `m:success` Success `[S]`: the snackbar, then a navigation to the index URL after 1,500 ms (`:167-170`).
  - `m:submitting` Page-specific `[S]`: saving disables Cancel and Save with no reason and reads "Saving..."; **no validation** (`noValidate`, nothing checked in `handleSubmit`; `required` only adds the label asterisk) (`:229,128-177,271-285`); the image uploads at selection time, before Save, and attaches only on Save (`:111-126,143-150`); turning the instructions switch off clears the prompt fields and a save then removes saved instructions (inferred from the backend storing the object wholesale); no delete-class control and no unsaved-changes guard.
- **Primary CTA:** "Save Changes" → `classControllerUpdateMetadata` (`:453-461,160-165`). The only filled button.
- **Exit / next:** Cancel and success → the index URL (the same component, `:169,193,447`).
- **Stage 1 hooks:** the class-card image fallback in the preview (mascot pose); the switch colour rule; labels above fields (kept); this page is the reference pattern for the admin form frame.
- **Companion (Phase 4):** the "Class preview" card's image fallback: `companion.slot.inline` 64 px idle still on the `--surface-hover` cover tile (TD-04); on the `--companion-backing` disc in dark mode.
- **Tour defects (in boundary):** TD-C1 (heuristic 4, sev 1); TD-04 (the preview card repeats the grey wordmark fallback; heuristic 8, sev 1); TD-22 (the instructions switch is purple while the "Enabled" switch on A-11 is green; heuristic 4, sev 2); TD-07 (heuristic 4, sev 1, via SR-1); TD-05, TD-09, TD-10, TD-11 (heuristic 4, sev 1), TD-12 (heuristic 2, sev 1) and TD-13 (heuristic 1, sev 2), all via SR-6.
- **Out of boundary, recorded:** B1 (guard).
- **Source-found:** SF-47 (the success snackbar reads "Class metadata updated successfully!" with an exclamation mark, against the brief's voice rule; the post-save redirect lands on the same page).
- **Not observed live:** error, loading and success states; the instructor view.

### A-14 · Materials — `/admin/class/:classID/manage-materials`

- **Source:** `src/pages/admin/Materials.tsx:101` (route `src/App.tsx:169`); `MaterialRow.tsx`, `MaterialSlots.tsx`, `materialsListing.ts`, `materialsStaging.ts`; frame SR-4 (`AdminPage` at `lg` while loading and `xl` once loaded, `AdminPageHeader` with title and description only: `Materials.tsx:428,448-452`). The upload UI is inline (`:454-677`); `BatchMaterialsUpload.tsx` does not exist. The route `batch-upload` redirects here (R-2).
- **Roles (Appendix A):** I, A. **Guard (code):** `ProtectedRoute` only (B1, D-5). **Entry visibility:** the class sub-menu item "Materials" is hidden from an instructor without `canManageMaterials` (`src/pages/admin/ClassLayout.tsx:37,153-166`); the route has no such check.
- **Density:** compact · **Paper surfaces:** none (no document content is rendered; the list shows file names only) · **Density overrides:** none.
- **Purpose:** Lets an admin or instructor upload PDFs as new lecture or recitation materials, then manage each material's student copy, answer key and reference files and watch server-side processing.
- **Entry points:** class sub-menu "Materials"; the redirect R-2; from A-24, its back arrow ("Back to Materials") and its one-item sidebar (`AnnotateDocument.tsx:230-238,1412-1417`); the class switcher keeps the sub-path; the chat agent (`class-materials`).
- **Content blocks (in order):**
  1. Loading shell (first fetch only): `AdminPage` with a centred spinner and no header — `Materials.tsx:426-434`.
  2. Page header: `h1` "Materials"; description "Upload a lecture or a recitation here; add its answer key and any reference material from the material itself. Processing runs on the server, so you can close this page once files are queued." — `:449-452`.
  3. Card "New materials": a leading cloud-upload icon at the far left (grey), `h6` "New materials", "Each file becomes its own material, as the copy students see. Answer keys go on the material below, whenever they are ready.", an outlined "Choose PDFs" that **carries its own cloud-upload start icon, so the same icon appears twice** (card lead and button) — `:454-500` (icons `:465-468,481-488`). No drag-and-drop zone exists.
  4. Staging table `m:staged`, only when files are staged: "File", "Type", "Title", "Available from" and an unlabelled actions column; per row the file name, a red error caption, attached-file chips ("Answer key" or "Reference" with a "Separate" text button), warnings ("Looks like an answer key, but nothing here matches it. On its own, students would see the answers." and "Looks like the answer key for “{title}”. As a new material, students would see the answers." with "Attach it there instead"), a Type select ("Lecture", "Recitation") guessed from the file name, a label-less Title field prefilled with the file name, a label-less date field prefilled with today's UTC date, and an unlabelled delete icon — `:502-656`; `materialsStaging.ts:47-80`. Below it a contained "Create {n} material" / "Create {n} materials" with a play icon — `Materials.tsx:658-673`.
  5. Card "Materials": a folder icon, `h6` "Materials", the summary "{n} material(s)" plus " · {k} need attention" — `:694-714`. Controls: a search field (placeholder "Search titles and file names"), a label-less filter select ("All materials", "Needs attention ({k})", "Missing answer key", "Processing failed"), a label-less sort select ("Newest first", "Oldest first", "By title"), an icon button "Collapse all" — `Materials.tsx:716-782`; `materialsListing.ts:69-74,86-120`.
  6. List groups "Lectures" then "Recitations", each a strip with an `overline` label and a count chip. **The capitals on screen are CSS:** the strings are "Lectures" and "Recitations" and MUI's `overline` variant uppercases them (`:799-810`; `node_modules/@mui/material/styles/createTypography.js:12-14,76`; `src/Theme.ts:91-93`).
  7. Material row, collapsed: the whole row is a click target (no role or tab stop); a chevron, a warning icon with tooltip "Needs attention", the title, chips ("No student copy", "Processing failed", "No answer key" in red outline; **"processing" in lowercase** in info colour; "{n} reference(s)"), and a date via `toLocaleDateString(undefined, { month: "short", day: "numeric" })` (chip labels `materialsListing.ts:16-21`), i.e. the **browser locale, no year** — `MaterialRow.tsx:27-31,134-178`. Expanded: fields "Title" and "Available from", a "Delete material" icon, and slot rows "Student copy", "Answer key" (recitations only), "Reference" with hover help (`materialsStaging.ts:120-125`: "What students see.", "The same pages with the answers. The tutor reads this; students never see it.", "Extra material for the tutor. Not shown to students."); an empty slot reads "Not uploaded — students cannot see this material at all." / "Not uploaded — the tutor only has the questions." / "None" with a text button "Upload student copy" / "Upload answer key" / "Add reference"; a filled slot shows the file name, a status chip ("pending", "processing" or the raw stage word, "ready", "failed"; the raw backend error in a tooltip) and icon buttons with tooltips "Annotate", "Replace this file, keeping its annotations", "Run the pipeline again", "Delete file" — `MaterialRow.tsx:181-243`; `MaterialSlots.tsx:23-214`.
  8. Footer caption "{n} material(s) hidden by the current filter." — `:814-826`.
  9. Overlays: the dialog "Replace this file?" `m:ov:replace-dialog` (body “{fileName}” is swapped for the file you pick next…; actions "Cancel", "Choose file"); the dialog "Delete this material?" / "Delete this file?" `m:ov:delete-dialog` (bodies “{title}” and its files stop appearing for students. Chat history that referenced it is kept. / “{fileName}” is removed permanently, along with its annotations and everything the tutor learned from it.; actions "Cancel", a red text "Delete"); a toast `m:success` `m:error` (standard Alert, 5,000 ms, default bottom left) — `:831-886`.
- **States:**
  - `m:loaded` Default `[T]` (about 960 px): the "New materials" card with the outlined "Choose PDFs", and the list with search, filter, sort and the group header "LECTURES 1"; the tour saw the search placeholder truncated.
  - `m:loading` Loading `[S]`: block 1 on the first fetch only; later reloads are silent; the list re-polls every 4,000 ms while any file is pending or processing (`:64-73,149-153`).
  - `m:empty` Empty `[S]`: "No materials yet." or "Nothing matches these filters."; slot empties as above; a failed first load shows the empty text beneath the error toast (`:793-795,134-139`).
  - `m:error` Error `[S]`: toasts "Could not load materials.", "Only PDF files are accepted." (warning), the raw `error.message` or "Upload failed.", "Could not queue reprocessing.", "Delete failed.", "Could not save the material."; a red caption on a staged row; a pipeline failure shows the red chips and the file's "failed" chip (`:137,169,317,337,351,371,391`).
  - `m:success` Success `[S]`: the toast "Uploaded. Processing continues on the server — you can leave this page.", shown even when some rows failed; replace, re-run, delete and field saves show no confirmation (`:261-269`).
  - `m:uploading` Page-specific `[S]`: while uploading, row controls and Create are disabled with no reason and the batch cannot be cancelled; staged files live in component state only, so a reload empties the staging table; the type guess uses file-name words (recitation, worksheet, exam, 习题, 作业 …) and folds files named answer(s), solution(s), key, 答案, 解答 under the matching worksheet (`materialsStaging.ts:47-51,76-78,138-172`); the row date label is local time while the editor field is UTC, so they can differ by a day (inferred; `MaterialRow.tsx:27-31,204-206`).
- **Primary CTA:** "Create {n} material(s)" (contained) once files are staged (`:659-672,210-270`); before that the only call to action is the outlined "Choose PDFs". No other filled button.
- **Exit / next:** the "Annotate" icon opens A-24 in a new tab (`MaterialSlots.tsx:166-174`); the success toast says the user may leave.
- **Stage 1 hooks:** chip colour rules (a mix of red outline, info outline and filled success); the two upload icons; the empty list text (empty-state frame); destructive wording is already consequence-first in the dialogs.
- **Tour defects (in boundary):** TD-C1 (heuristic 4, sev 1); TD-23 (an upload icon sits outside the "Choose PDFs" button, measured 13 px left of the button's border; heuristic 4, sev 1; **source shows two icons, the card-leading one and the button's own, and the measured offset cannot be matched to either, so it needs a re-measure**); TD-24 (dates follow the browser locale, "12月31日" under `zh-CN`; heuristic 4, sev 2); TD-25 (the search placeholder is truncated at about 960 px, to re-check at 1280; heuristic 6, sev 1); TD-26 (the group header reads "LECTURES 1" in capitals; the string is title case and the capitals are CSS; heuristic 4, sev 1); TD-07 (heuristic 4, sev 1, via SR-1); TD-05, TD-09, TD-10, TD-11 (heuristic 4, sev 1), TD-12 (heuristic 2, sev 1) and TD-13 (heuristic 1, sev 2), all via SR-6.
- **Out of boundary, recorded:** B1 (guard).
- **Source-found:** SF-48 (chip casing is mixed: "No student copy" against "processing", "pending", "ready", "failed" and raw stage words; the capitalised label for "processing" is defined and never rendered, `materialsListing.ts:20`; `MaterialRow.tsx:73`).
- **Not observed live:** staging, expanded rows, dialogs, errors, polling.

### A-15 · Class flags — `/admin/class/:classID/feature-flags`

- **Source:** `src/pages/admin/ClassSpecificFeatureFlags.tsx:10` (route `src/App.tsx:173`); `AddClassFeature.tsx` (with `hideClassSelection`), `ListClassFeatures.tsx`; frame SR-4 (`AdminPage maxWidth="xl"`, `AdminPageHeader`: `:53-57`).
- **Roles (Appendix A):** A (admin-only). **Instructors (decision of 2026-10-16):** no access; an instructor who reaches the page should see a no-permission state, not be signed out and not be sent back with an `alert()`. Today D-8 (a record-only defect row in the matrix). **Guard (code):** `ProtectedRoute` only (B1, D-5). **Entry visibility:** the class sub-menu item "Class Flags" is hidden from instructors, as the decision wants (`src/pages/admin/ClassLayout.tsx:38,154-156`); not in the sitemap. A shortcut card in the unrouted `ClassManagement.tsx:141-146`.
- **Density:** compact · **Paper surfaces:** none · **Density overrides:** none.
- **Purpose:** Lets an admin grant a feature to one class (optionally with an expiry) and see which features that class currently has.
- **Entry points:** class sub-menu "Class Flags" (SR-3); the class switcher keeps the sub-route; a typed URL.
- **Content blocks (in order):**
  1. Page header: `h1` "Feature Flags"; description "Control staged feature access for {className || classID}." (the class id shows until the name loads or if its lookup fails) — `:53-57`.
  2. Two columns from `lg` (4/12 and 8/12): left, the "Class access" card without the Class select (a row "Target" with a small outlined chip holding the **raw class id**; "Feature"; "Expiration (optional)"; contained "Add class flag") — `AddClassFeature.tsx:114-164`; right, "Assigned flags" ("Features currently available to this class."; outlined "Refresh"; a plain table "Feature", "Expiration", "Status" with a chip "Active" (green, filled) or "Expired" (outlined); no Edit control, no pagination) — `ListClassFeatures.tsx:47-160`.
  3. Overlay `m:success` `m:error`: the add snackbar (4,000 ms).
- **States:**
  - `m:loaded` Default `[S]`: header and both cards.
  - `m:none` Loading `[S]`: none; the table has no skeleton or spinner (`:13,18-26`).
  - `m:empty` Empty `[S]`: "No feature flags are assigned to this class." (`ListClassFeatures.tsx:150`).
  - `m:error` Error `[S]`: a failed list load is console-only and shows the empty text; an add failure shows the error snackbar; a missing route parameter shows an Alert "No class ID was provided." (`:38-41,44-50`).
  - `m:success` Success `[S]`: "Class feature flag added."
  - Page-specific `[S]`: "Add class flag" is disabled until a feature is chosen, with no reason; the expiry fields have the UTC-versus-local handling of A-06.
- **Primary CTA:** "Add class flag" → `featureFlagsControllerAddClassFeature`, then reloads the list (`AddClassFeature.tsx:50-76,156-164`).
- **Exit / next:** none; no back button.
- **Stage 1 hooks:** chips; the empty row (empty-state frame).
- **Tour defects (in boundary):** TD-C1 (heuristic 4, sev 1); TD-21 (sidebar "Class Flags" against heading "Feature Flags"; heuristic 4, sev 2); TD-24 (the expiration column uses `toLocaleString()`, found in source, `ListClassFeatures.tsx:129`; heuristic 4, sev 2); TD-07 (heuristic 4, sev 1, via SR-1); TD-05, TD-09, TD-10, TD-11 (heuristic 4, sev 1), TD-12 (heuristic 2, sev 1) and TD-13 (heuristic 1, sev 2), all via SR-6.
- **Out of boundary, recorded:** B1 (guard).

### A-16 · Enroll students — `/admin/class/:classID/enroll-students`

- **Density:** compact · **Paper surfaces:** the templated-email previews in the dialog, `srcDoc` iframes with their own CSS (`src/components/Email/EmailSelectedUsersDialog.tsx:346-487`). **Never recoloured:** the email HTML (purple `#5C3B6E` header, grey `#f5f5f5` body, white card; Appendix C). · **Density overrides:** the email preview and the editable announcement message are reading surfaces; the summary row, search, roster table and pagination are tool chrome.
- **Purpose:** For one class, paste email addresses to enrol them, then view the combined roster (active and pending), tick rows, email them or remove them.
- **Entry points:** class sub-menu item "Enroll Students" (SR-3); the top-bar class switcher keeps the sub-route (`AdminLayout.tsx:410-439`); the chat agent (`class-enroll`); a typed URL. The "Enroll Students" card in `ClassManagement.tsx:159-164` is unreachable (that component is imported nowhere).
- **Content blocks (in order):**
  1. Page header: `h1` "Enroll Students"; description "Add students, review pending enrollments, and manage the class roster." — `ClassEnroll.tsx:256-259`.
  2. Info Alert "Managing enrollment for class: " with the bold class name, or the raw class ID until the name arrives (permanently if that fetch fails and no name came in router state) — `:261-265,236-247`.
  3. Enrolment panel, a flat Paper filled with a literal `#f5f5f5`: multiline field (6 rows, white fill), label "Email Addresses (one per line) *" (the asterisk is typed in the string; there is no `required`), helper "Enter email addresses, one per line", no placeholder; a full-width contained large button "ENROLL USERS" with a person-add icon — `:269-292`. The capitals are in the string (`:290`); the theme sets `textTransform: "none"` (`src/Theme.ts:99`).
  4. Section heading `h5` "Enrolled Users" — `:294-303`.
  5. Summary and bulk row (flex, space-between, no responsive props): "Total: {n} users ({a} active, {p} pending)" (counts ignore the search); contained primary "Email Selected ({n})"; outlined error-colour "Remove Selected ({n})"; search field "Search users..." 300 px wide — `:305-347`.
  6. Roster table in a Paper: header row on a literal `#f5f5f5` with select-all checkbox, "Email", "Role", "Status"; rows with a checkbox, the email, a role chip ("Admin" warning, "Professor" secondary, "Student" primary, which is also the fallback) and a status chip ("Active" success, "Pending" info); active roster first, then pending, API order, 15 rows per page — `:349-407,79-84,198-214`.
  7. Pagination, centred, rendered only above 15 matches — `:409-417`.
  8. Overlay `m:success` `m:error`, snackbar with a closable Alert, 3,000 ms — `:420-428`.
  9. Overlay `m:ov:email-dialog`, Dialog "Send Email to Selected Users" (md): info Alert "Sending to {n} selected user(s):" with chips "{n} pending" and "{n} active"; "Recipients:" list (email, status chip and role chip as **raw lowercase** words); a pending-only note "Welcome Email Template: Pending users will receive the standard welcome email with access instructions. No custom message needed."; a mixed note "Mixed Selection: Pending users will receive welcome emails, while active users will receive the class announcement below."; for active or mixed: a disabled field "Class Name (for subject line)", an editable 6-row "Announcement Message" with helper "This message will be sent using the class announcement template" and a default text; an "Email Preview" panel (HTML and plain-text tabs, or two stacked previews when mixed); actions "Cancel" and contained "Send Email" ("Sending...") — `EmailSelectedUsersDialog.tsx:189-540`. On success the dialog closes and the page says "Emails sent successfully!".
  10. Overlay `m:G-2`: the native `alert()` on 403 and 404 (`src/utils/middleware.ts:105-110`).
- **States:** (★ marks the four undrawn states of the mock review that belong to this page, and the two page details that its findings 1 and 10 concern; the fifth undrawn state, streaming with Stop, is in SR-6)
  - `m:loaded` Default `[T]` (about 960 px wide): header, class Alert, enrolment panel, "Enrolled Users", summary row, table, pagination when needed.
  - `m:empty-roster` Loading `[S]`: **none**; until both roster requests return the page reads "Total: 0 users (0 active, 0 pending)" with the empty-roster row; "ENROLL USERS" and "Remove Selected" have no in-flight state (`ClassEnroll.tsx:72,249-252,312`).
  - `m:empty-roster` ★ Empty roster `[S]`: one centred table row "No users enrolled yet", with the header row and select-all checkbox still shown; the same output appears while loading or after a failed load (`:366-375`).
  - `m:no-results` ★ No search results `[S]`: the same single row reading "No users found matching your search"; the search is a case-insensitive substring match on email; there is no clear-search control; `currentPage` is not reset when the query changes (`:372,82-88,339`).
  - `m:zero-selected` ★ Zero selected `[S]`: "Email Selected (0)" and "Remove Selected (0)" stay visible and disabled with no tooltip or reason; ticks on other pages or hidden by the search still count, because the selection is one id list, and select-all ticks only the current page (`:318,327,353-358,73,97-104`).
  - `m:invalid-entry` ★ Invalid email `[S]`: **no client-side detection.** The text is split on newlines, trimmed and sent as typed (the variable is named `validEmails`); the only check is "Please enter at least one email address"; the field has no error styling and its helper never changes. The backend accepts any address with no account as a pending enrolment (`../kiwi/src/class/class.service.ts:197-199,228-233`), so a mistyped address surfaces only as a "Pending" row (inferred, not run) (`:149-156`).
  - `m:error` Error `[S]`: snackbar "Please enter at least one email address"; the raw `error.message` when it contains "ConflictException"; "Unexpected error occurred. Please try again."; "Failed to remove users. Please try again."; "Failed to load users" (`:153,174-179,143,231`). A missing route parameter replaces the page with "Error: Class ID not found" (`:45-55`, unreachable through the route pattern). The textarea keeps its text after a failed enrol.
  - `m:success` Success `[S]`: "Users enrolled successfully!", "Selected users removed successfully!", "Emails sent successfully!" (`:165,134,439`). After enrolling, the textarea clears, the roster reloads and rows whose email exactly equals a submitted address are ticked (`:167-170,218-227`).
  - `m:paginated` Page-specific `[S]`: *partial success* — no such state; the response is checked for truthiness only, so there is one generic message and no counts, and on a 409 the roster is not reloaded (`:163,172-182`). ★ *Remove selected* — **no confirmation and no undo**; the call runs at once and the snackbar offers no action (`:106-146,420-428`); a failure of the second call leaves rows on screen that the server already removed. ★ *Pagination* — MUI `Pagination` (arrow buttons with aria-labels "Go to previous page" / "Go to next page"), rendered only above one page, and on page 1 the previous button is rendered **disabled** (not hidden); no "Showing x-y of z" text (`:409-417`; `node_modules/@mui/material/usePagination/usePagination.js:112`). Select-all, row checkboxes and the search field have no label or aria-label (`ClassEnroll.tsx:354,380,334`).
- **Primary CTA:** "ENROLL USERS" → enrols the typed addresses, clears the field, reloads the roster and ticks the new rows (`:148-183,281-291`). Other filled buttons in the view: "Email Selected ({n})" (`:314-322`); in the dialog "Send Email". "Remove Selected ({n})" is outlined.
- **Exit / next:** none; after enrolling the new rows are pre-ticked, which enables "Email Selected ({n})"; after a send the ticks clear.
- **Stage 1 hooks:** the empty-roster and no-results rows (empty-state frame); chip colour rules; the all-caps button; destructive "Remove Selected" (Phase 3 gives it a distinct variant, Phase 5 a consequence-first label).
- **Tour defects (in boundary):** TD-C1 (heuristic 4, sev 1); TD-32 (textarea in a grey panel, label on a white strip, helper repeats the label; heuristic 8, sev 1); TD-33 ("ENROLL USERS" in capitals, full width; heuristic 4, sev 1); TD-34 (toolbar buttons wrap onto three and two lines at about 960 px; not reproduced at 1280 or 1440 per the mock review; heuristic 8, sev 2); TD-35 (a filled purple role chip beside a filled green status chip in every row; heuristic 8, sev 2); TD-40 (several accents in one view; heuristic 8, sev 2); TD-21 (case: "Enroll Students" against "User management"; heuristic 4, sev 2); TD-37 (the email previews in the dialog carry the solid `#5C3B6E` header and the "Kiwi Platform" footer; heuristic 4, sev 2); TD-07 (heuristic 4, sev 1, via SR-1); TD-05, TD-09, TD-10, TD-11 (heuristic 4, sev 1), TD-12 (heuristic 2, sev 1) and TD-13 (heuristic 1, sev 2), all via SR-6.
- **Out of boundary, recorded:** B1 (guard); TD-18 (the Kiwi Chat launcher covers the Status column at the tour width).
- **Source-found:** SF-27 (hard-coded `#f5f5f5` fills on the panel and header row, `ClassEnroll.tsx:269,279,352`; the dialog shows raw lowercase status and role words, `EmailSelectedUsersDialog.tsx:252,257`); SF-59 ("ENROLL USERS" and "Email Selected ({n})" are both filled in one view); SF-64 (the class lookup behind the roster answers 200 for every role); SF-65 (the roster read uses `ProfGuard`, so an instructor assigned to the class is refused).
- **Not observed live:** every state except the default; the dialog.

Today `ProfGuard` refuses them (D-9) and the class sub-menu hides the entry (D-4). **Guard (code):** `ProtectedRoute` only (B1, D-5). **Entry visibility:** the class sub-menu item "Class Email" is hidden from instructors although the decision grants the page to the instructors of the class (D-4; `src/pages/admin/ClassLayout.tsx:42,155`); the chat-agent sitemap lists it for admin and instructor (`src/agent/sitemap.ts:131-135`). Inside the page `user?.isAdmin` decides the UI: admins get two tabs, anyone else gets the announcement form only (`ClassEmail.tsx:64,94,161`).
- **Density:** compact · **Paper surfaces:** none · **Density overrides:** the 12-row and 10-row message inputs are long prose input; tabs and composer controls are tool chrome.
- **Purpose:** Sends one email or announcement to the students of the class in the URL.
- **Entry points:** class sub-menu item "Class Email" (SR-3); the top-bar class switcher keeps the sub-route when the class changes (`AdminLayout.tsx:424-429`); the chat agent (`class-emails`); a typed URL. A "Class Email" card in `ClassManagement.tsx:165-170` is unreachable (that component is imported nowhere).
- **Content blocks (in order):**
  1. Page header: `h1` "Send Emails"; description "Send an email or announcement to students in {className}." with {className} falling back to "this class" — `ClassEmail.tsx:144-147`.
  2. If the class has no students: warning Alert "No students enrolled in this class yet." in place of everything below — `:149-152`.
  3. Otherwise a Paper opening with secondary text "Send an email to {n} students in this class" (`:154-159`), then: *non-admin* — the announcement composer alone (`:161-196`); *admin* — tabs "Custom Email" and "Announcement Format" (`:197-247`).
  4. Custom Email panel: "Recipients: {n} selected", "Subject", "Message" (12 rows, placeholder "Compose your message..."), filled "Send Email" (`EmailComposer.tsx:85-177`).
  5. Announcement composer `m:loaded:announcement`: info Alert "This will send a formatted announcement email with the class name and your name as the professor."; an outlined card "Class Details" with "Class: {className}", "From: {professorName}", "Recipients: {n} student(s)"; field "Subject Override (Optional)" (placeholder "Leave empty to use default subject format", helper "Default: [Class Name] Announcement from [Professor Name]"); a required 10-row field "Announcement Message" (placeholder "Type your announcement message here..."); filled "Send Announcement" — `AnnouncementComposer.tsx:55-128`.
  6. Overlay `m:success` `m:error`: snackbar, closable Alert, 6,000 ms — `ClassEmail.tsx:251-263`. No dialog, no confirm-before-send.
- **States:**
  - `m:loaded:custom@A` Default `[S]`: the Paper with the form(s); admins start on "Custom Email" (`:43`); send buttons disabled until text exists, with no reason (`EmailComposer.tsx:170`; `AnnouncementComposer.tsx:118`).
  - `m:loading` Loading `[S]`: a skeleton inside `AdminPage` with no header (`ClassEmail.tsx:133-139`).
  - `m:empty` Empty `[S]`: the warning Alert above.
  - `m:error` Error `[S]`: snackbar "Failed to load class information" (`:80-86`), after which the warning Alert also shows; announcement failures: snackbar with the message or "Failed to send announcement" (`:187-193,236-242`); a Custom Email failure shows an inline Alert in the composer with the message or "Failed to send email".
  - `m:success` Success `[S]`: snackbars "Email sent successfully to {n} recipients" and "Announcement sent successfully to {n} recipients" (`:119-123,182-186,231-235`), n being the page's own count, not the API's.
  - `m:submitting` Page-specific `[S]`: after a resolved Custom Email send the composer's `sending` flag is not reset, so "Sending..." stays until the tab is switched (derived). Both announcement callbacks catch their own errors, so the composer clears Message and Subject Override after a failed send as well as a successful one (derived; `AnnouncementComposer.tsx:43-47`). "{n} students" has no singular on the page sentence (`ClassEmail.tsx:158`).
- **Primary CTA:** non-admin and the admin "Announcement Format" tab: "Send Announcement" → `emailControllerSendClassAnnouncement`; admin "Custom Email" tab: "Send Email" → `emailControllerSendToClass` (`ClassEmail.tsx:92-103,168-180,217-229`). One composer is visible at a time.

  2. Scope control, the only filter: a MUI `Select` with no visible label and no aria-label; closed state shows a book icon and "All lectures" or the lecture's file name (fallback "Lecture"); the menu lists "All lectures" then one row per lecture with its question-count pill — `:256-377`. **No date-range control exists**, and the API's weekly series is fetched and never rendered (`src/utils/classAnalyticsApi.ts:18-21,29`).

Heading "Overview" and four KPI tiles (1, 2 or 4 per row), each an outlined 104 px Paper with a label, a value (`h4`) and a 40 px icon in `primary.main` (`#6a1b9a`): "Questions", "Active students", "Topics", "Active lectures" (shown as `{active}/{total}`; with a lecture chosen it becomes "Last activity" with a date or "—") — `:419-465,38-102`. **No baseline, delta, trend arrow or sparkline on any tile.**
  6. Panel "Student intent" (`IntentsPanel.tsx:26-218`): overline "Student intent"; helper "What students are trying to get from the AI — and the topics inside each intent"; one 12 px horizontal 100%-stacked bar, a tooltip "{label} · {count} ({share}%)" on each segment, no axis, tick or legend, no text alternative on the bar; below it one clickable card per intent (colour square, label, count, "{share}% of questions · {n} student|students", topic chips "{topic} ×{n}"). Seven literal hues from `tokens.ts:44-52`: concept `#3b5bdb`, answer-seeking `#c2255c`, guidance `#0b7285`, debugging `#d9480f`, logistics `#495057`, exploration `#7048e8`, other `#868e96`. Backend labels: "Concept clarification", "Answer seeking", "Guided practice", "Debugging help", "Course logistics", "Exploration", "Other".

Panel "Topics" (`TopicsPanel.tsx:47-259`; left column at `lg`): overline "Topics"; helper "What students ask about in {lectureName}" or "What students ask about across the class"; an icon-only refresh button (tooltip "Re-analyze all questions", aria-label "Re-analyze topics", disabled while generating with no reason on the control); while generating, the note "Analyzing student questions… this usually takes under a minute." in a literal amber `#946300` above an indeterminate 3 px `LinearProgress` in violet `#5b3a8c`; on failure "Topic analysis failed: {topics.error}. Use the refresh button to retry."; the ranked list with an inline 4 px bar per topic (bar width = count / largest count, so the top topic is always 100%; raw integer count; row is `role="button"`), "{n} student|students" and up to four page chips "p.{n} ×{count}"; footer "{n} topics · {n} questions analyzed" and "Updated {toLocaleString}" — `:69-257`.

Panel "Student chats" (`ChatBrowser.tsx:61-251`; right column): overline "Student chats"; helper "Open a student|page to read the full conversation"; toggle "By student" · "By page"; a scrolling list of rows (a 36 px badge, the label, a secondary line, the question count, a chevron). Student labels are anonymised by the backend ("Student {n}", fallback "Unknown Student").

Overlays: the lecture menu; tooltips; a question dialog `m:ov:questions-dialog` for a topic or an intent (title with a colour square, sub-line "{n} question|questions", rows of question text, student label, page chip, timestamp; close aria-label "Close question list"); a chat-detail dialog `m:ov:chat-dialog` (title the student label or "{fileName} · p.{n}", rows with "(empty message)" fallback and a toggle aria-label "Show AI answer" / "Hide AI answer", "AI answer" overline, "No stored answer for this question.", footer "Load more ({loaded} of {total})"); a PDF-page dialog `m:ov:pdf-dialog` (title "{fileName} · page {n}", a toolbar labelled "PDF controls" with "Search", "Thumbnails", "First", "Previous", "Page", "Next", "Last", "Zoom out", "Zoom in", "Fit width", "Open / print", "Fullscreen") — `QuestionListDialog.tsx`, `ChatDetailDialog.tsx`, `PdfPageDialog.tsx`, `PDFViewer.tsx:106-203`. A PDF dialog opens over the dialog that launched it.

The header icon is passed and discarded (`experimentUi.tsx:110`).

(create) / "Update groups, assignment, and prompts. Active experiments use saved changes on future chats." (edit); back button "Back to Experiments" — `:617-626`.

Group preview is unavailable until it loads."); subheading "Student groups" with text button "Reshuffle" (seeded) or "Recompute" (manual); then the preview, one outlined box per variant with a 4 px left border in a literal engine colour (`#6366f1` for qwen, `#10b981` for openai), an engine chip, a count chip ("{n} students · target {pct}%") and one chip per student email, or "No students" — `:661-868`.

Review before approving.", "Imported {file.name}. Review before approving.", "Randomly assigned {n} students across {k} groups." (`:235,257,513,544,556`).

### A-21 · Experiment detail — `/admin/class/:classID/experiments/:experimentId`

- **Source:** `src/pages/admin/experiments/ExperimentDetail.tsx:77` (route `src/App.tsx:180`); shared `experimentUi.tsx`, `grouping.ts`; SR-7; only `AdminPageHeader` of SR-4, through `PageHeader`. Centred column of at most 920 px inside `ExperimentTheme` (`ExperimentDetail.tsx:198-199`).

- **Density:** compact · **Paper surfaces:** none · **Density overrides:** the prompt text boxes and the description paragraph are reading surfaces; accordions, chips, stat pills and the action row are tool chrome.
- **Purpose:** Shows one experiment's status, assignment strategy and, per variant, its engine, allocation, student and chat counts, student list and system prompt, and lets the instructor edit it, activate or re-activate it, turn it off or export its config as JSON.

- **Content blocks (in order):**
  1. Page header: `h1` the experiment name (no fallback for an empty name); description `{assignment label} · seed "{seed}" · by {unit}` (labels "Seeded split", "Random on first chat", "Manual lists"); a status chip; a back arrow (aria-label "Back to Experiments") — `ExperimentDetail.tsx:165-175,201-205`.
  2. A dismissible error Alert with the raw `{error}` — `:208-212`.
  3. Action row: outlined "Edit"; by status contained "Activate" (draft) or "Re-activate" (completed), or a red outlined "Turn off" (active); always outlined "Export JSON", which downloads `{name or "experiment"}.json` with member lists stripped — `:214-241,136-154`. **No delete control and no confirmation wording** for Activate, Re-activate or Turn off exist on this page.
  4. Description paragraph, only when set — `:243-251`.
  5. Overline "Variants · {n}" — `:253-260`.
  6. One card per variant, stacked with a staggered fade-in (the whole "variant comparison": no side-by-side view, chart or results panel): label, key (monospace), engine chip (raw id), allocation chip "{n}%"; two stat pills "students" and "chats"; an accordion "Students in this group" (collapsed; chip "{n} students" always plural; email chips or "No students"; absent for the random strategy); an accordion "System prompt" (collapsed; chip "Customized" or "Using default"; the prompt in a white bordered box or "(empty — class default applies)") — `:261-424`.
  7. SR-7, rendered only in the loaded state — `:426`. On this URL it starts as the launcher unless the user left it open on another experiment page.
- **States:**
  - `m:loaded` Default `[S]`: as above.
  - `m:loading` Loading `[S]`: a full-page violet spinner replaces everything (header, buttons and the assistant), on first load and after every Activate, Re-activate or Turn off (`:95,156-161`).
  - `m:empty` Empty `[S]`: "No students" in a group; "(empty — class default applies)"; no description block; no empty state for zero variants (the heading would read "Variants · 0").
  - `m:error` Error `[S]`: an unknown id or a failed first load shows an Alert "{error || "Not found"}" and nothing else, with no header, back button or retry (`:162-163`); later failures show the dismissible Alert above the actions. The counts, analytics, roster and defaults requests each swallow errors (`:96-102`), giving zero pills and "Using default" chips with no message.
  - `m:none` Success `[S]`: none; no snackbar; Export gives a browser download with no message.
  - Page-specific `[S]`: no busy state during Activate or Turn off (`:127-134,223-237`); an empty variant prompt with a non-empty class default is labelled "Customized" while its text reads "(empty — class default applies)" (derived from code).
- **Primary CTA:** "Activate" (draft) or "Re-activate" (completed) → `experimentApi.activate` then a reload (`:223-232`). For an active experiment there is no filled button; "Turn off" is outlined.
- **Exit / next:** back arrow → A-19 (`:205`); "Edit" → A-20 (`:218`); SR-7 → A-20 prefilled.

  - `m:empty` Empty `[S]`: "No chunks", "No chunks found on this page", the summaries text, "No matches"; a blank source shows `<p>Please select a file</p>`.
  - `m:error:pdf` Error `[S]`: visible only for the PDF itself — a red Alert "Couldn't load this PDF." with "Retry" — and "Search failed. Try again."; **everything else is console-only**: a failed document fetch leaves the area blank, comment save failure silently removes the new pin, summary failures change nothing (`PDFViewer.tsx:76-89`; `AnnotateDocument.tsx:572-574,1046-1051,1128-1137`).
  - `m:none` Success `[S]`: **no confirmation anywhere** (no snackbar or Alert is imported); the effect is the optimistic pin change or the refreshed summary list.
  - Page-specific `[S]`: no page-level Save, each action persists on its own through plain `fetch`; an empty comment makes Save a silent no-op; chunk add, edit and delete call the API and only log the result, with no state update in source (inferred: the table does not change until reload); chunk delete has no confirmation; cancelling the `prompt()` still sends the edit with unchanged text; a newly drawn chunk is sent with the text "text..." and a fixed 1700 x 2200 layout (`:895-937,1339-1390,156-157,178`).
- **Primary CTA:** "Add Document Summary" (contained, header action) → the summary dialog (`:1418-1422`). Also filled while open: the selected chunk's number button in the rail, "Save" in the comment panels and "Save" / "Save Summary" in the dialog.
- **Exit / next:** the back arrow and the sidebar "Materials" → A-14 (`:1412-1417,230-238`).
- **Stage 1 hooks:** the paper PDF page and its neutral frame; literal highlight colours (`#1976d2`, red) become tokens with the role "annotation"; pin visibility needs a legend (copy); the native `prompt()`.
- **Tour defects (in boundary):** TD-C1 (heuristic 4, sev 1); TD-24 (the summary author line uses `toLocaleString()`, found in source, `DocumentSummaryDialog.tsx:315`; heuristic 4, sev 2); TD-07 (heuristic 4, sev 1, via SR-1); TD-05, TD-09, TD-10, TD-11 (heuristic 4, sev 1), TD-12 (heuristic 2, sev 1) and TD-13 (heuristic 1, sev 2), all via SR-6.
- **Out of boundary, recorded:** B1 (guard).
- **Source-found:** SF-49 (destructive and editing actions without confirmation or feedback: chunk delete, comment delete, summary delete, failed saves; chunk edit through a browser `prompt()`).
- **Not observed live:** the whole page.

### Redirect-only routes

Listed as redirects, not pages. R-1 to R-3 are redirect-only routes; R-4 is conditional (the route is page S-09 when a consent form is configured) and R-5 is an effect of the class layout, not a route. Each renders nothing of its own or a bare placeholder.

| ID | Route | Mechanism and target | Condition | Anchor | What the user sees |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| R-1 | `/admin` (index) | `<Navigate to="classes" replace />` → `/admin/classes` (A-01) | Any signed-in user who reaches `/admin` | `src/App.tsx:137` | Nothing; the admin shell mounts, then A-01 |
| R-2 | `/admin/class/:classID/batch-upload` | `<Navigate to="../manage-materials" replace />` → `/admin/class/:classID/manage-materials` (A-14) | Always; kept for bookmarks and the old sidebar link | `src/App.tsx:170-172` | Nothing; A-14 |
| R-3 | `/logout` | `ProtectedRoute` mounts `Logout`, which calls `logout()` during render and returns `<div>Logout</div>`; with the user cleared, `ProtectedRoute` re-renders into `<Navigate to="/login" replace />` (S-05) | Signed-in user; reached by the top bar's Logout, a plain anchor `href="/logout"` that reloads the page | `src/App.tsx:88-94`; `src/pages/Logout.tsx:3-6`; `src/components/TopNav/TopNav.tsx:234-244`; `src/auth/ProtectedRoute.tsx:51-53` | The bare word "Logout" for a moment, then S-05 (SF-02) |
| R-4 | `/consent` (conditional) | `<Navigate to="/" replace />` (S-01) | No `VITE_CONSENT_FORM_URL` is configured; otherwise the route is page S-09 | `src/pages/Consent.tsx:45-47` | Nothing; S-01 |
| R-5 | `/:classID/app/:appSlug` on a typed, reloaded or new-tab URL (not a route: an effect of the class layout) | `StudentClassLayout` replaces the route with `/:classID` (S-02) while the enabled-app list is still empty, because `currentTabIndex` is -1 | Any role, on a direct load; reaching the page through the app tab does not redirect | `src/pages/ClassLayout.tsx:85,117,193-207` | Materials (S-02) instead of the app. **Seen live on 2026-10-16 with the admin account `[L]`:** the typed URL `/local-css-demo/app/ics-r-lab` ended at `/local-css-demo` with no iframe (defect B6) |

**Guard redirects** (behaviour of the wrappers, recorded for the matrix; none is a route):

- `ProtectedRoute`: signed out → `/login`; consent required and missing → `/consent`; a stored OAuth return context → its `returnTo`, or `/class/{classId}` (D-6) — `src/auth/ProtectedRoute.tsx:17-58`.
- `UnprotectedRoute`: signed in → `/consent` if consent is missing, else `/admin` (instructor, admin) or `/` — `src/auth/UnprotectedRoute.tsx:15-26`.
- `AdminProtectedRoute`: signed out → `/login`; consent missing → `/consent`; admin status unknown → a centred spinner; neither admin nor instructor → `/` — `src/auth/AdminProtectedRoute.tsx:15-42`.
- Page-level: `StudentClassLayout` replaces an unknown tab with the first tab, which is what makes R-5 (`src/pages/ClassLayout.tsx:192-207`); `AdminLayout` sends an instructor on the classless admin-app route to their first assigned class or `/admin/classes` (`AdminLayout.tsx:384-408`); A-03 sends non-admins to `/admin/classes` (`Analytics.tsx:245-247`); A-02 returns to `/admin/classes` on close (`AddNewClass.tsx:393-394`).

## Verification matrix

What later phases and the implementation verify against, per the brief's done-when 2 and G13: **route x role x state x mode x viewport**.

**One rule.** Roles follow the intended access model, Appendix A as the user updated it on 2026-10-16: student accounts cannot enter the teacher and admin interface (`/admin/*`), instructors and admins can enter the student interface, the system-level admin pages and Class feature flags and Class MCP settings are admin-only, and the instructors of a class may use Enrolment and Class email. **A role gets a verified row if and only if Appendix A grants it.** A role that today's guards let in but Appendix A does not grant gets a record-only defect row: a student on a `/admin/*` page (defect B1, recorded in [Students on `/admin/*`](#students-on-admin-b1), with no row) and an instructor on an admin-only page (defect D-8, a record-only row in the Page rows and a cell in [Instructors on admin-only pages](#instructors-on-admin-only-pages-d-8)). A defect row has no state, mode or viewport cells; today's outcome stays as evidence, and the intended outcome is a no-permission state, which does not exist today. Where today's guards refuse a role that Appendix A grants (the instructors of a class on A-16 and A-17, D-9), the row stays a verified row and its Today column records the refusal.

What today's guards do is recorded next to the model: every page was walked once per role in source (none was run), driven by the guards (the route wrapper, the layout's and the page's own redirects, the page's first-load requests and whether each passes through the session middleware, and the backend guards and inline checks behind each request). The result is the [Role outcomes](#role-outcomes) tables, one cell per page and role with its source anchors, the two defect sections, and a Today column on every page row. The Page rows' States column is **generated from the `m:<id>` tags in the [Page specs](#page-specs)** (each States bullet and each overlay item carries its id). All of it was re-checked against its sources when this file was written: every page x role cell has an outcome and exactly one row or defect record; every States bullet and overlay item has an id; every row's states come from ids; and the guard list of every request in the pass equals the guard decorators in the backend source. A later phase that adds or changes a state or a guard changes its id, its outcome cell and its row together. Display-menu preferences (theme, Kiwi animation) and the motion settings (`VITE_ENABLE_MASCOT_MOTION`, reduced motion) are not a matrix dimension; they are verified in Phases 4 and 7 against done-when 7 and 8.

### Dimensions

| Dimension | Values | Rule |
|---|---|---|
| Route | the 40 leaf routes of `src/App.tsx`: 39 are pages, folded to 34 page entries (multi-route pages list every route), and `/logout` is a redirect-only leaf (R-3) | The two `<Navigate>` routes (R-1, R-2) are counted outside the 40 (46 router entries = 1 root + 3 layouts + 2 `<Navigate>` + 40 leaves). The five redirects have a row of their own (Redirect rows): R-1 to R-3 are redirect-only routes, R-4 is the conditional redirect of `/consent` (the route is page S-09 when a consent form is configured) and R-5 is the direct-load redirect of S-04. |
| Role | signed out; S; I; A | By the one rule: S, I and A on every student, auth and fallback route (signed out too on the auth and fallback pages); A on all 24 admin pages; I on the 12 admin pages that Appendix A grants it, as verified rows; I on the 12 admin-only pages as record-only defect rows; **no S row on any `/admin/*` page**. A row's Outcome is what the role gets by design (`renders`, `redirects to ...`, or `defect record: no-permission state expected`) with, in square brackets, the flags that name a discrepancy or a role-specific control: `D-1` the wrapper admits instructors although the page is admin-only, `D-2` the sign-in pages redirect a signed-in user, `D-3` `/authorize` has no wrapper, `D-4` the class sub-menu hides an entry that Appendix A grants the instructor, `D-7` the page turns the role away itself, `D-8` an instructor on an admin-only page is signed out or bounced instead of seeing a no-permission state, `D-9` the backend refuses the instructors of a class on Enrolment and Class email, "view-switch icon, SR-1" and "Experiment row, SR-6e" the two controls that instructors and admins have on the student pages. The **Today** column says what the guards do now where that differs (`renders, then B2`); `same` where it does not. Signed out is a row only for the auth and fallback pages; elsewhere it is a redirect to `/login` (G-4). |
| State | the ids in the page specs: `loaded`, `loading`, `empty`, `error`, `success`, `submitting`, named page states, `ov:` overlays, `native:` browser dialogs | The states the page spec defines, "where the page has them" (done-when 2), **for the role as the page is meant to work for it**: a verified row whose Today is B2 still lists the page's states, because the refusal is the defect and not the design. A redirect row and a defect record have no cells; their outcome is recorded. A state the spec marks absent ("none") is tagged `m:none` and has no row. States the demo data cannot show need a fixture or a stalled or failed request; the spec marks them `[S]`. |
| Mode | light, dark | Both for every verification row. A redirect row has a mode dimension only where it renders something of its own: R-3's bare word "Logout" takes the mode's colours; R-1, R-2, R-4 and R-5 render nothing. Dark ships behind `VITE_ENABLE_DARK_MODE` (G3). Paper surfaces stay light in dark mode (G5). |
| Viewport | 1280x800 and 1440x900 for **every** route; 390x844 for **student, auth and fallback routes** (S-01 to S-10) | Admin routes are desktop-only (G13). Layouts that appear below 1200 px (the A-11 card layout) fall outside the verification viewports. |

### Role outcomes

Each cell below is **today's outcome** for an intended page and role, a conclusion from source and not an observation: this pass ran nothing and started no backend, and the tour (which used an older backend) observed none of these cells. The only live observations of these cells are the coordinator's checks of 2026-10-16 with an admin account, marked `[L]`: the course list (S-01) with its view-switch icon, the Experiment toggle row on a student class page, and a typed app URL (R-5). For every page and role the pass read (1) the route wrapper in `src/App.tsx` and `src/auth/`, (2) the redirects in the layouts and in the page, (3) the page's first-load requests and the client each one uses (middleware or plain `fetch`, G-3), (4) the backend route's guards, taken from the controller decorators by script (250 of the 257 generated client operations matched a backend route; the 7 that did not are MCP transport and well-known metadata endpoints that no page calls; the direct `fetch()` calls were read by hand and are named by path), and the handler's or service's own role checks, and (5) what the page does with each answer. Three scripted checks back the cells: every request's guard list equals the decorators, every status agrees with the guard table below (or names the inline check that differs), and every anchor resolves to a real line. A blind second reading of the first-load requests and the outcome of the 16 pages where a missed request would change a result (A-01, A-02, A-03, A-12, A-13, A-14, A-18 to A-21, A-23, A-24 and S-01 to S-04) agreed on every role; for S-04 it also found that a direct load redirects, which is R-5.

**Roles (fixtures).**

| Role | Fixture |
|---|---|
| signed out | No `authToken` in `localStorage`, or no `user` in the auth context. |
| S | `systemRole` `user`; enrolled as a student in the class under test; no instructor assignment; no professor enrollment. |
| I | `systemRole` `instructor`; actively assigned to the class under test with `canManageMaterials` and `canViewAnalytics`; no professor enrollment (assigning an instructor creates none: `../kiwi/src/instructor-class/instructor-class.service.ts:14-62`). Whether an instructor or an admin is also enrolled as a student in the class (an admin who created a class is enrolled as its professor) decides S-01's `loaded` and the comment pins of S-02; a cell says so where that, a permission flag or the missing professor enrollment decides the result. |
| A | `systemRole` `admin`. |
| All signed-in roles | Have consented, or no consent form is configured. Otherwise every route behind a wrapper first redirects to `/consent` (`src/auth/ProtectedRoute.tsx:55-58`, `src/auth/UnprotectedRoute.tsx:16-18`, `src/auth/AdminProtectedRoute.tsx:19-22`). |

**Outcome classes.** `renders`: no first-load request is refused for the role and nothing redirects it. `B2`: a first-load request answers 401 through the session middleware, so the role sees the first frame and is then signed out. `alert`: a first-load request answers 403 or 404 and the browser shows a native `alert()`; the page stays. `redirect`: the front end sends the role elsewhere.

**Mechanisms that apply to every page.**

| Row | Mechanism | Where |
|---|---|---|
| G-1 | **B2.** A call that goes through the session middleware and answers 401 is retried once after a token refresh (`GET /auth/refresh-token`, open to every signed-in role); the retry's 401, or a refresh that fails, clears `authToken` and `authUser` and sets `window.location.href` to `/login?timeout=true`, which shows the session-expired banner (S-05). The page is left, whatever it showed. If the refresh call itself is refused, `refreshAuthToken` first sets `/login?sessionExpired=true`, which S-05 does not read; which address wins is a navigation race and is not settled from source. | `src/utils/middleware.ts:6-41,61-102`; `src/pages/Login.tsx:100-104` |
| G-2 | **alert.** A 403 or 404 raises the browser's own `alert()` ("You do not have permission to perform this action." / "The requested resource was not found."); the response is then returned to the caller as usual. The alert is drawn by the browser, so its check is the wording and the trigger, not contrast. | `src/utils/middleware.ts:104-110` |
| G-3 | **Scope.** Only calls made with a client that carries the middleware pass through it: the default `apiClient` and the email, audit, webhook, token, MCP, tool, kiwi-app, class-app and permission clients of `src/utils/api.ts`, and so `experimentApi`, apart from its edit-save `PUT`. Direct `fetch()` calls (the class-analytics reads, the student comment and summary reads, A-24's comment and summary reads), `apiClientWithoutMiddleWare` and the app-token bridge client do not, so a refusal there is silent: no refresh, no alert, no sign-out. A cell names such calls where they matter. | `src/utils/api.ts:22-42,44-53,63-64`; `src/utils/experimentApi.ts:106-135,193-194` |
| G-4 | **Signed out.** On every page behind `ProtectedRoute` or `AdminProtectedRoute` a visitor without a user is redirected to `/login`, so signed out is a row only for the auth and fallback pages. | `src/auth/ProtectedRoute.tsx:51-53`; `src/auth/AdminProtectedRoute.tsx:15-17` |

**Backend guards the pages meet** (what each does to the three fixtures):

| Guard | Passes | S | I | A | Failure | Where |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `SmartJwtAuthGuard` (app-wide) | a bearer access token and, outside `/mcp`, a session | pass | pass | pass | 401 | `../kiwi/src/auth/auth.module.ts:83-84`; `../kiwi/src/auth/auth.guard.ts:573-656` |
| `JwtAuthGuard` | the same check | pass | pass | pass | 401 | `../kiwi/src/auth/auth.guard.ts:137-197` |
| `AdminGuard` | `systemRole` `admin` | refused | refused | pass | 401 | `../kiwi/src/auth/auth.guard.ts:53-81` |
| `ProfGuard` | admin, or a `professor` enrollment (an instructor assignment creates none) | refused | refused (no professor enrollment) | pass | 401 | `../kiwi/src/auth/auth.guard.ts:84-133` |
| `InstructorClassGuard` | admin, a professor of the class, or an instructor assigned to it (an active assignment is enough; the permission flags are not read); a request that names no class is refused | refused | pass for the assigned class | pass | 401 | `../kiwi/src/auth/auth.guard.ts:319-350`; `../kiwi/src/instructor-class/instructor-class.service.ts:174-195` |
| `InstructorRoleGuard` | admin, a professor enrollment, or `systemRole` `instructor` | refused | pass | pass | 401 | `../kiwi/src/auth/auth.guard.ts:353-407` |
| `AnalyticsAdminGuard` | admin | refused | refused | pass | **403** | `../kiwi/src/auth/auth.guard.ts:410-438` |
| `AnalyticsAccessGuard` | admin, a professor enrollment in any class, or an instructor with `canViewAnalytics`; the class-scoped analytics handlers then check the class again | refused | pass with `canViewAnalytics` for that class | pass | **403** | `../kiwi/src/auth/auth.guard.ts:441-506`; `../kiwi/src/analytics/class-analytics.controller.ts:41-48` |
| `MaterialManageGuard` | admin, a professor of the class, or an instructor with `canManageMaterials` for it | refused | pass with `canManageMaterials` | pass | **403** (404 for an unknown material or document) | `../kiwi/src/instructor-class/material-manage.guard.ts:27-80` |
| No guard, or `JwtAuthGuard` only, with a refusal inside the handler | the service checks enrollment or ownership itself (for example `ensureStudentDocumentAccess`: 401 without an enrollment; `canReadDocument`: 404) | per call | per call | per call | 401 or 404 | `../kiwi/src/document/document.service.ts:1512,1554`; a cell names the call |

**Shared shell and global parts** (their first-load requests do not decide any page's outcome):

**What the pass shows for I and A on the 24 admin pages.**

| Role | Granted pages: renders | Granted pages: refused (B2) | Admin-only pages: B2 after the first frame | Admin-only pages: alert, then redirect | Admin-only pages: renders, the submit is refused (B2) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| I | 10 (A-01, A-12, A-13, A-14, A-18, A-19, A-20, A-21, A-23, A-24) | 2 (A-16, A-17) | 10 (A-04, A-05, A-06, A-07, A-08, A-09, A-10, A-11, A-15, A-22) | 1 (A-03) | 1 (A-02) |
| A | 24 (all) | 0 | n/a | n/a | n/a |

- **Instructors on the 12 admin-only pages** are signed out on 10 of them after the first frame, bounced with an `alert()` on A-03, and let into a form whose submit signs them out on A-02, instead of seeing a no-permission state (D-8, register B4). On A-04, A-05, A-07 and A-08 the wrapper even lets them in (D-1).
- **The instructors of a class are refused on Enrolment and Class email** (A-16, A-17; D-9, register B5): `ProfGuard` needs a professor enrolment, and assigning an instructor creates none (SF-65). Their class sub-menu entries are hidden too, as is Class Apps (A-23, which renders for them): D-4.
- **The sidebar already shows an instructor only the entries they may use among the system pages.** The nine system entries other than Classes are admin-only (`AdminLayout.tsx:50-110`); no entry an instructor sees leads to an admin-only page, so they reach those pages by a typed URL or through the chat agent's sitemap, which lists `new-class` and `analytics` for instructors (SR-3).
- **Four guards answer 401, not 403.** `AdminGuard`, `ProfGuard`, `InstructorClassGuard` and `InstructorRoleGuard` end the session; only `AnalyticsAdminGuard`, `AnalyticsAccessGuard` and `MaterialManageGuard` answer 403 and show an `alert()`. B2 is therefore the usual outcome of a refusal and `alert` the rare one.
- **An instructor's permission flags decide three pages**: A-14 and A-24 need `canManageMaterials` and A-18 needs `canViewAnalytics`; without them the instructor gets the student's outcome of the guard section below.
- **Plain `fetch` calls refuse silently** (G-3): the comment and summary reads of S-02 for an unenrolled I or A (no pins, no banner), and A-18's analytics reads when a flag is missing (an inline error).
- **The student class shell opens for any role and any class** (SF-64): an instructor or admin who is not enrolled still gets S-02 to S-04. The one enrollment-sensitive request, `POST /document`, answers 404 with an `alert()` for an instructor without `canManageMaterials`.

### Students on `/admin/*` (B1)

This is the guard section for students. **Intended outcome.** A student account cannot enter the teacher and admin interface: every `/admin/*` URL should turn a student away, to `/`. **Today** the `/admin` layout route is wrapped in `ProtectedRoute` only (`src/App.tsx:129-135`), so a student reaches the admin shell and what happens next depends on the page; the four pages wrapped in `AdminProtectedRoute` already turn a student away (`src/App.tsx:140-159`). This is defect **B1**. It is outside the Stage 1 boundary (a guard and routing change, not a visual one), and its fix, a role guard on the `/admin` layout route, is tracked separately. The table records today's outcome per page with source anchors, from the same pass as the Role outcomes above. **These cells are not verification rows: no S row exists for any `/admin/*` page.** Phase 7 verifies the turn-away once B1 is fixed (Appendix D).

What a student sees today is not uniform. The session ends after the first frame (B2) on 12 pages, but 5 pages render in full (A-01, A-02, A-12, A-18, A-23; A-01 lists every class in the system, SF-64), 2 raise an `alert()` and stay (A-14, A-24), and 5 redirect (A-03, A-04, A-05, A-07, A-08; A-04, A-05, A-07 and A-08 at the route wrapper before the page mounts, A-03 from inside the page after two `alert()`s, and it sends the student on to A-01, which renders). 

| Page | Today for a student |
|---|---|
| A-01 Classes | **renders**: `GET /class/list` and `GET /class/id` answer 200 (`JwtAuthGuard`): "All Classes", every card and the Create New Class control, as for an admin; its write, `POST /class/add`, is `AdminGuard` and answers 401 (B2) (`src/pages/admin/Classes.tsx:88-97`; `../kiwi/src/class/class.controller.ts:300-317`; `../kiwi/src/class/class.controller.ts:92`) |
| A-02 New class | **renders**: the create-class dialog is open from the first frame and the page sends no request on mount; the first refusal comes from the image upload (`POST /class/image`, `InstructorRoleGuard`) or the submit (`POST /class/add`, `AdminGuard`), both 401, then B2 (`src/pages/admin/AddNewClass.tsx:75-87,107,126,391-394`; `../kiwi/src/class/class.controller.ts:92,148`) |
| A-03 System analytics | **redirect** to `/admin/classes` (A-01): the first frame is `loading`; `GET /auth/role` answers 200 and the page navigates to A-01, but `POST /analytics/DateFiltered` (`AnalyticsAccessGuard`) and `GET /analytics/top-token-users` (`AnalyticsAdminGuard`) are still sent and answer 403, so the browser shows two `alert()`s; no 401, so no B2 (`src/pages/admin/Analytics.tsx:241-252`; `../kiwi/src/auth/auth.guard.ts:410-438,441-506`; `src/utils/middleware.ts:105-110`) |
| A-04 System email | **redirect** to `/` (S-01): `AdminProtectedRoute` shows a spinner while `isAdmin` is undefined, then redirects a user who is neither admin nor instructor; the page never mounts, so none of its requests is sent (no alert, no B2) (`src/auth/AdminProtectedRoute.tsx:25-42`; `src/App.tsx:140-143`) |

### Instructors on admin-only pages (D-8)

This is the guard section for instructors, built like the students' one. **Intended outcome.** Appendix A makes 12 admin pages admin-only: New class (A-02), the system-level Analytics, Email, Email audit, System flags, MCP servers, Kiwi Apps, Webhooks, Webhook approvals and User management (A-03 to A-11), and, inside a class, Class feature flags and Class MCP settings (A-15, A-22). An instructor who reaches one of them should see a **no-permission state**: not be signed out (B2), and not be sent back with a browser `alert()`. That state does not exist today, so it has no page entry; it is recorded as defect D-8 (register B4), outside the Stage 1 boundary. **Today** the guards let the instructor in. `AdminProtectedRoute` admits instructors on A-04, A-05, A-07 and A-08 (D-1); the other pages sit behind `ProtectedRoute` only. On A-04 to A-11, A-15 and A-22 the backend refuses the first request with 401, which the front end treats as an expired session (B2); A-03 raises an `alert()` and redirects (D-7); A-02 renders its form and the submit is refused (B2). By the one rule each of these is a **record-only defect row** in the Page rows (no state, mode or viewport cells), and the table below keeps today's outcome as evidence for the fix. The admin sidebar already hides every one of these pages from an instructor (SR-3), so an instructor reaches them by a typed URL; the one other way in is the chat agent's sitemap, which lists A-02 (`new-class`) and A-03 (`analytics`) for instructors (`src/agent/sitemap.ts:60-70`).

### Redirect rows

Five redirects have a row of their own: the three redirect-only routes (R-1 to R-3), the conditional redirect of `/consent` (R-4; with a consent form configured the route is page S-09 instead) and the direct-load redirect of S-04 (R-5). Each is observed once per role, viewport and, where it renders something of its own, mode. R-3 shows the bare word "Logout" (SF-02) and so has a mode dimension; R-1, R-2, R-4 and R-5 render nothing and have none. A redirect that follows a page, as the page cells above record it, is observed on that page's own row.
